# 밤 습격 물량의 단일 조정 지점 (2026-08-04 2차 피드백: 초반 난이도 완화).
# 밤 1회차 기준: 스폰 간격 0.67초, 동시 개체 상한 35마리, 밤 전환 즉시 소환 5마리.
# 주기가 오를수록 후반에는 간격 하한 0.22초·상한 MAX_ENEMIES로 수렴한다.
# _current_spawn_interval() / _current_enemy_limit() / _night_raid_burst_count()가 사용한다.
const NIGHT_SPAWN_INTERVAL_BASE := 0.72
const NIGHT_SPAWN_INTERVAL_STEP := 0.05
const NIGHT_SPAWN_INTERVAL_FLOOR := 0.22

# 일시정지 모달에서 필드(playing)로 돌아온 직후의 유예 무적 (2026-08-04 3차 피드백 ⑮).
# 사용자 요구 원문: "스킬을 확정했을 때 0.5초 정도 무적 시간을 줘. 플레이하기가 힘들어.
# 바로 몹한테 맞아서 난이도가 너무 높아."
# 부여 지점은 _grant_modal_return_invulnerability() 한 곳이며 실제 피격 무효는
# player.gd의 대시 무적과 같은 경로(invulnerability)를 재사용한다.
const MODAL_RETURN_INVULN := 0.5
# 마왕 카드 획득 토스트 타이밍 (2026-08-04 3차 피드백 ⑲: "마왕 팝업이 너무 짧아. 3초 정도 유지").
# HOLD는 페이드 인/아웃을 제외한 "화면에 온전히 보이는" 시간이다.
const BOSS_TOAST_FADE_IN := 0.18
const BOSS_TOAST_SLIDE_IN := 0.24
const BOSS_TOAST_HOLD := 3.0
const BOSS_TOAST_FADE_OUT := 0.35
# 3초 유지 중에 다른 토스트 사건이 겹치면 덮어쓰지 않고 이만큼까지 큐에 쌓아 차례로 보여준다.
const BOSS_TOAST_QUEUE_MAX := 3
# 성 입장/퇴장 페이드 커튼(_play_scene_transition) 타이밍.
# 커튼이 SCENE_TRANSITION_FADE_IN 동안 닫힌 뒤 midpoint 콜백이 실행되고, 그 다음
# SCENE_TRANSITION_TAIL 동안은 아직 화면이 가려져 있다. 성에서 필드로 나올 때의
# 유예 무적은 midpoint에서 부여되므로 이 꼬리 시간만큼 더 줘야 실제로 보이는 0.5초가 된다.
const SCENE_TRANSITION_FADE_IN := 0.2
const SCENE_TRANSITION_HOLD := 0.08
const SCENE_TRANSITION_FADE_OUT := 0.28
const SCENE_TRANSITION_TAIL := SCENE_TRANSITION_HOLD + SCENE_TRANSITION_FADE_OUT
const PROGRESS_PATH := "user://the_unchosen_progress.cfg"

#   ③ 테두리 색: 강조색을 테두리에 쓰지 않고 중립 색 2종으로만 두르고,
#      강조는 헤딩 글자색과 3px 액센트 바로만 표현한다(강조색 남용 정리).

func _ready() -> void:
	process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	rng.randomize()
	_load_progress()
	_ensure_input_actions()
	sound_manager = SOUND_SCRIPT.new()
	add_child(sound_manager)
	_build_ui()
	_show_menu()
	var args := OS.get_cmdline_user_args()
	if "--smoke-test" in args:
		call_deferred("_run_smoke_test")
	elif "--combat-test" in args:
		call_deferred("_run_combat_test")
	elif "--world-test" in args:
		call_deferred("_run_world_test")
	elif "--v4-test" in args:
		call_deferred("_run_v4_test")
	elif "--v4-castle-test" in args:
		call_deferred("_run_v4_castle_test")
	elif "--stress-test" in args:
		call_deferred("_run_stress_test")
	elif "--preview-choice" in args:
		call_deferred("_run_choice_preview")
	elif "--preview-boss" in args:
		call_deferred("_run_boss_preview")
	elif "--preview-world" in args:
		call_deferred("_run_world_preview")
	elif "--preview-night" in args:
		call_deferred("_run_night_preview")
	elif "--preview-onboarding" in args:
		call_deferred("_show_onboarding")
	elif "--preview-fate" in args:
		call_deferred("_run_fate_preview")
	elif "--preview-evolution" in args:
		call_deferred("_run_evolution_preview")
	elif "--preview-trial" in args:
		call_deferred("_run_trial_preview")
	elif "--preview-castle" in args:
		call_deferred("_run_castle_preview")
	elif "--preview-build" in args:
		call_deferred("_run_build_preview")
	elif "--preview-item" in args:
		call_deferred("_run_item_preview")
	elif "--preview-effects" in args:
		call_deferred("_run_effects_preview")
	elif "--preview-toast" in args:
		call_deferred("_run_toast_preview")
	elif "--capture-lobby" in args:
		call_deferred("_run_visual_capture", "lobby")
	elif "--capture-character" in args:
		call_deferred("_run_visual_capture", "character")
	elif "--capture-onboarding" in args:
		call_deferred("_run_visual_capture", "onboarding")
	elif "--capture-world" in args:
		call_deferred("_run_visual_capture", "world")
	elif "--capture-factory" in args:

func _run_smoke_test() -> void:
	automated_test = true
	_start_game()
	await get_tree().create_timer(0.2).timeout
	for index in 4:
		_show_skill_choice("test")
		_choose_skill(current_pair[0], current_pair[1])
		_factory_lane_pressed(index % factory.slots.size(), 0)
		await get_tree().create_timer(0.04).timeout
	boss_items.append("r_greatsword_01")
	_challenge_demon_king()
	_begin_boss_battle()
	await get_tree().create_timer(0.2).timeout
	if is_instance_valid(boss):
		while boss.active_modules.has("rollback"):
			boss.active_modules.erase("rollback")
		boss.take_damage(100000.0, player.global_position)
	await get_tree().create_timer(0.6).timeout
	print("SMOKE_TEST_COMPLETE state=%s level=%d cards=%d rejections=%d boss_items=%d factory_slots=%d" % [state, level, selected_skills.size(), rejected_skills.size(), boss_items.size(), factory.slots.size()])
	await _quit_test_cleanly()

func _run_combat_test() -> void:
	_start_game()
	await get_tree().create_timer(12.0).timeout
	print("COMBAT_TEST_COMPLETE state=%s kills=%d xp=%d enemies=%d phase=%s" % [
		state, kills, experience, get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("enemies").size(), "night" if is_night else "day"
	])
	await _quit_test_cleanly()

func _run_world_test() -> void:
	_start_game()
	await get_tree().create_timer(0.2).timeout
	var starter: Dictionary = world.get_nearest_interactable(Vector2(250.0, -250.0), 120.0)
	var demon_feature: Dictionary = world.get_nearest_interactable(world.get_demon_castle_position(), 120.0)
	var river_blocked: bool = not world.is_walkable(Vector2(820.0, 250.0))
	var bridge_open: bool = world.is_walkable(Vector2(820.0, 0.0))
	var special_fields_walkable := world.is_walkable(world.get_demon_castle_position())
	for trial_position: Vector2 in world.get_trial_camps().values():
		special_fields_walkable = special_fields_walkable and world.is_walkable(trial_position)
	var generation_check := world.validate_generation_near(Vector2.ZERO)
	var sample_point := Vector2(1240.0, 1320.0)
	var sample_before := world.get_tile_id(sample_point)
	var streaming_rules_ok := true
	for step in 12:
		var direction := -1.0 if step % 2 == 0 else 1.0
		var far_point := Vector2(direction * (12000.0 + step * 7800.0), -direction * (9000.0 + step * 5300.0))
		var far_check := world.validate_generation_near(far_point)
		streaming_rules_ok = streaming_rules_ok and bool(far_check.get("local_rules", false)) and bool(far_check.get("seams", false))
	var sample_after := world.get_tile_id(sample_point)
	var generation_stats := world.get_generation_stats()
	var cache_bounded := int(generation_stats.get("cached_chunks", 0)) <= 72
	var deterministic := sample_before == sample_after
	print("WORLD_TEST_COMPLETE starter=%s demon=%s river_blocked=%s bridge_open=%s special_walkable=%s wfc_local=%s seams=%s streaming=%s deterministic=%s bounded=%s algorithm=%s cache=%d demon_distance=%d" % [
		starter.get("type", "none"), demon_feature.get("type", "none"), river_blocked, bridge_open, special_fields_walkable,
		generation_check.get("local_rules", false), generation_check.get("seams", false), streaming_rules_ok, deterministic, cache_bounded,
		generation_stats.get("algorithm", "unknown"),
		int(generation_stats.get("cached_chunks", 0)), int(world.get_demon_castle_position().length())
	])
	await _quit_test_cleanly()

func _run_v4_test() -> void:
	automated_test = true
	_start_game()
	await get_tree().create_timer(0.25).timeout
	var rarity_counts := ItemLibrary.rarity_counts()
	var item_catalog_ok := ItemLibrary.ITEMS.size() == 57 and int(rarity_counts["common"]) == 21 and int(rarity_counts["rare"]) == 15 and int(rarity_counts["unique"]) == 12 and int(rarity_counts["hero"]) == 9
	var skill_variety_ok := DealCardLibrary.SKILLS.size() >= 28
	var combat_profiles_ok := true
	for definition: Dictionary in DealCardLibrary.all_with_specials():
		skill_variety_ok = skill_variety_ok and float(definition.get("duration", 0.0)) >= 0.8
		var deals_damage := float(definition.get("damage", 0.0)) > 0.0
		combat_profiles_ok = combat_profiles_ok and (not deals_damage or float(definition.get("range", 0.0)) > 0.0)
		var reaction := DealCardLibrary.knockback_profile(definition)
		combat_profiles_ok = combat_profiles_ok and (not deals_damage or float(reaction.get("stun", 0.0)) > 0.0)
	var initial_shape_ok := factory.slots.size() == 3 and not factory.trailing_bridge and factory.compile_group(0).size() == 1 and String(factory.compile_group(0)[0].get("id", "")) == "basic"
	var item_semantics_deck: FactoryDeck = FACTORY_SCRIPT.new()
	item_semantics_deck.reset()
	item_semantics_deck.place_card(1, 0, ItemLibrary.instance("u_rapier_01"))
	var item_group := item_semantics_deck.compile_group(1)
	var item_semantics_ok := item_group.size() == 1 and String(item_group[0].get("card_kind", "")) == "item" and is_zero_approx(float(item_group[0].get("duration", 1.0))) and item_semantics_deck.total_reload() < item_semantics_deck.raw_total_reload()
	var construction_deck: FactoryDeck = FACTORY_SCRIPT.new()
	construction_deck.reset()
	for _step in 14:
		construction_deck.build_next_piece()
	var construction_ok := construction_deck.slots.size() == 10 and not construction_deck.trailing_bridge and construction_deck.construction_count == 14
	var first_split: bool = construction_deck.split_slot(0)
	var second_split: bool = construction_deck.split_slot(0)
	var split_ok: bool = first_split and second_split and construction_deck.slots[0]["lanes"].size() == 3 and construction_deck.split_upgrades_used == 2
	construction_deck.add_inventory(DealCardLibrary.instance("cleave", 1))
	construction_deck.add_inventory(DealCardLibrary.instance("cleave", 1))
	var fusion_ok := construction_deck.fuse("cleave", 1) and construction_deck.get_rank_count("cleave", 2) == 1

	player.global_position = Vector2(250.0, -250.0)
	_refresh_interactable()
	var physical_e := InputEventKey.new()
	physical_e.physical_keycode = KEY_E
	physical_e.pressed = true
	_unhandled_input(physical_e)
	var castle_input_ok := state == "castle_interior" and inside_castle and is_instance_valid(castle_interior) and castle_interior.services.has("card_fusion") and castle_interior.npcs.size() == 4
	if inside_castle:
		_exit_castle()

	factory.place_card(0, 0, DealCardLibrary.instance("cleave", 1))
	_reset_player_cycle()
	player.set_facing_direction(Vector2.RIGHT, true)
	await get_tree().create_timer(0.12).timeout
	var live_effect: CycleSkillEffect
	for node: Node in gameplay_root.get_children():
		if node is CycleSkillEffect and not (node as CycleSkillEffect).boss_mode:
			live_effect = node as CycleSkillEffect
			break
	var direction_before := Vector2.ZERO if not is_instance_valid(live_effect) else live_effect._current_facing()
	var turn_target := _spawn_enemy_instance(player.global_position + Vector2(0.0, -92.0), 2, "", false, "", false, "wild_boar")
	var turn_target_health: float = float(turn_target.health) if is_instance_valid(turn_target) else 0.0
	player.set_facing_direction(Vector2.UP, true)
	await get_tree().create_timer(0.12).timeout
	var direction_after := Vector2.ZERO if not is_instance_valid(live_effect) else live_effect._current_facing()
	var turned_target_hit: bool = not is_instance_valid(turn_target) or float(turn_target.health) < turn_target_health
	var live_direction_ok: bool = direction_before.is_equal_approx(Vector2.RIGHT) and direction_after.is_equal_approx(Vector2.UP) and turned_target_hit
	_show_skill_choice("test")
	var choice_ui_ok := state == "choice" and choice_buttons.size() == 3 and String(choice_buttons[0].get_meta("choice_role", "")) == "skill" and "보유중" in String(choice_buttons[0].get_meta("owned_text", "")) and String(choice_buttons[2].get_meta("choice_role", "")) == "cancel"
	# 단일 포커스 모델 검사: 방향키가 항상 지정된 한 칸으로 직행하고 강조는 항상 1개만 남는다.
	# Godot 내장 Control 포커스 이동이 두 번째 선택자로 끼어들지 않도록 focus_mode도 함께 확인한다.
	var single_focus_ok := _choice_highlight_count() == 1 and choice_selected_index == 0
	for choice_button: Button in choice_buttons:
		single_focus_ok = single_focus_ok and choice_button.focus_mode == Control.FOCUS_NONE
	for probe: Array in [[KEY_RIGHT, 1], [KEY_DOWN, 2], [KEY_LEFT, 0], [KEY_UP, 0], [KEY_D, 1], [KEY_S, 2], [KEY_W, 1]]:
		var probe_event := InputEventKey.new()
		probe_event.keycode = int(probe[0])
		probe_event.pressed = true
		_unhandled_input(probe_event)
		single_focus_ok = single_focus_ok and choice_selected_index == int(probe[1]) and _choice_highlight_count() == 1
	_clear_overlay()
	get_tree().paused = false
	state = "playing"
	_show_factory_menu("place", DealCardLibrary.instance("time_cut", 1), "playing")
	_factory_lane_pressed(1, 0)
	var immediate_place_ok := state == "playing" and String(factory.get_card(1, 0).get("id", "")) == "time_cut"
	var before_drag_left := factory.get_card(0, 0)
	var before_drag_right := factory.get_card(1, 0)
	_show_factory_menu("edit", {}, "playing")
	_on_factory_card_dropped({"zone":"rail", "slot":0, "lane":0, "has_card":true}, {"zone":"rail", "slot":1, "lane":0, "has_card":true})
	var drag_swap_ok := String(factory.get_card(0, 0).get("id", "")) == String(before_drag_right.get("id", "")) and String(factory.get_card(1, 0).get("id", "")) == String(before_drag_left.get("id", ""))
	_close_factory_menu()

	# 아이템 획득 새 플로우: 후보 2개 이지선다 → 고른 1개는 보관함 직행, 남은 1개는 마왕에게.
	# 어떤 경우에도 factory_place(즉시 배치)로 진입하지 않는다.
	var storage_before := factory.inventory.size()
	var boss_items_before := boss_items.size()
	_show_item_offer("treasure")
	var item_pair_ok := state == "item_choice" and current_item_pair.size() == 2 and choice_buttons.size() == 2
	item_pair_ok = item_pair_ok and String(current_item_pair[0].get("id", "")) != String(current_item_pair[1].get("id", ""))
	item_pair_ok = item_pair_ok and _choice_highlight_count() == 1
	var kept_item_id := String(current_item_pair[0].get("id", "")) if current_item_pair.size() == 2 else ""
	var passed_item_id := String(current_item_pair[1].get("id", "")) if current_item_pair.size() == 2 else ""
	_choose_offered_item(0)
	var item_to_storage_ok := item_pair_ok and state == "playing" and factory.inventory.size() == storage_before + 1
	item_to_storage_ok = item_to_storage_ok and String(factory.inventory[factory.inventory.size() - 1].get("id", "")) == kept_item_id
	item_to_storage_ok = item_to_storage_ok and String(factory.inventory[factory.inventory.size() - 1].get("kind", "")) == "item"
	item_to_storage_ok = item_to_storage_ok and boss_items.size() == boss_items_before + 1 and boss_items[boss_items.size() - 1] == passed_item_id

	# 초반 원거리 배제 검사(2차 피드백⑩). targeting 모듈이 붙는 두 경로를 함께 막았는지 본다.
	#   ① 네이티브 원거리 몬스터: monster_library.gd MONSTERS.unlock
	#   ② 부채 기반 랜덤 모듈: enemy.gd setup()의 debt 루프
	# 두 경로 모두 MonsterLibrary.ranged_gate_ok(cycle) 한 곳을 통과해야 한다.
	var early_ranged_ok := MonsterLibrary.ranged_table_ok()
	early_ranged_ok = early_ranged_ok and not MonsterLibrary.ranged_gate_ok(1)
	early_ranged_ok = early_ranged_ok and MonsterLibrary.ranged_gate_ok(MonsterLibrary.ranged_unlock_cycle())
	for spawn_row: Dictionary in MonsterLibrary.spawn_table(1, true):
		early_ranged_ok = early_ranged_ok and not bool(spawn_row.get("ranged", false))
	var saved_night := is_night
	var saved_cycle := cycle_number
	var saved_debts := rejected_skills.duplicate()
	is_night = true
	cycle_number = 1
	# targeting으로 매핑되는 부채(투사체/연쇄/대시)만 8장 넘겨 최악의 조건을 만든다.
	# 게이팅이 없으면 마물 1마리가 원거리가 될 확률이 1 - 0.9^8 ≈ 57%다.
	rejected_skills.assign(["thunder", "targeting", "dash_blade", "blade_fan", "earth_splitter", "boomerang_blade", "phantom_step", "recursion"])
	var night_one_limit := _current_enemy_limit()
	var night_one_interval := _current_spawn_interval()
	var night_one_burst := _night_raid_burst_count()
	for _probe in 40:
		var probe_position: Vector2 = world.find_walkable_near(player.global_position, rng, 300.0, 700.0)
		var probe_enemy := _spawn_enemy_instance(probe_position)
		if not is_instance_valid(probe_enemy):
			continue
		var probe_modules: Array = probe_enemy.active_modules
		early_ranged_ok = early_ranged_ok and not probe_modules.has(MonsterLibrary.RANGED_MODULE)
	# 밤 1회차 물량 완화(피드백⑩)도 같은 플래그로 회귀 방지한다.
	early_ranged_ok = early_ranged_ok and night_one_limit <= 40 and night_one_interval >= 0.5 and night_one_burst <= 6
	is_night = saved_night
	cycle_number = saved_cycle
	rejected_skills.assign(saved_debts)

	# 초반 낮 선공몹 배제 검사(3차 피드백⑭). behavior 4 = 기본 선공·시야 추적.
	# 밤은 행동 유형과 무관하게 전원 습격이라 게이팅 대상이 아니므로 밤에는 남아 있어야 한다.
	var early_day_peace_ok := MonsterLibrary.AGGRO_DAY_UNLOCK_CYCLE >= 5 and MonsterLibrary.AGGRO_DAY_UNLOCK_CYCLE <= 10
	# behavior 4 종이 실제로 여러 종 존재해야 이 검사가 의미를 가진다(빈 집합 통과 방지).
	var aggro_species := 0
	for archetype: Dictionary in MonsterLibrary.MONSTERS:
		if int(archetype["behavior"]) == MonsterLibrary.AGGRO_BEHAVIOR:
			aggro_species += 1
	early_day_peace_ok = early_day_peace_ok and aggro_species >= 3
	for peace_cycle in range(1, MonsterLibrary.AGGRO_DAY_UNLOCK_CYCLE):
		early_day_peace_ok = early_day_peace_ok and not MonsterLibrary.aggro_gate_ok(peace_cycle, false)
		for spawn_row: Dictionary in MonsterLibrary.spawn_table(peace_cycle, false):
			early_day_peace_ok = early_day_peace_ok and int(spawn_row["behavior"]) != MonsterLibrary.AGGRO_BEHAVIOR
		var night_aggro_rows := 0
		for spawn_row: Dictionary in MonsterLibrary.spawn_table(peace_cycle, true):
			if int(spawn_row["behavior"]) == MonsterLibrary.AGGRO_BEHAVIOR:
				night_aggro_rows += 1
		early_day_peace_ok = early_day_peace_ok and night_aggro_rows > 0
	# 해금 주기부터는 낮에도 등장하고, 주기가 오를수록 비중이 커진다(램프업).
	var unlock_day_share := 0.0
	var later_day_share := 0.0
	for spawn_row: Dictionary in MonsterLibrary.spawn_table(MonsterLibrary.AGGRO_DAY_UNLOCK_CYCLE, false):
		if int(spawn_row["behavior"]) == MonsterLibrary.AGGRO_BEHAVIOR:
			unlock_day_share += float(spawn_row["share"])
	for spawn_row: Dictionary in MonsterLibrary.spawn_table(MonsterLibrary.AGGRO_DAY_UNLOCK_CYCLE + 2, false):
		if int(spawn_row["behavior"]) == MonsterLibrary.AGGRO_BEHAVIOR:
			later_day_share += float(spawn_row["share"])
	early_day_peace_ok = early_day_peace_ok and unlock_day_share > 0.0 and later_day_share > unlock_day_share
	# 강제 경로가 게이트 때문에 빈 후보로 죽지 않아야 한다.
	#   기본: 낮 초반의 forced_behavior=4는 그 주기에서 허용된 순한 몹으로 대체된다.
	#   override: 시련 캠프 정예·미믹·보스 하수인은 그대로 선공몹을 받는다.
	var gated_forced := MonsterLibrary.roll(rng, 1, false, MonsterLibrary.AGGRO_BEHAVIOR)
	early_day_peace_ok = early_day_peace_ok and not gated_forced.is_empty() and int(gated_forced["behavior"]) != MonsterLibrary.AGGRO_BEHAVIOR
	var override_forced := MonsterLibrary.roll(rng, 1, false, MonsterLibrary.AGGRO_BEHAVIOR, true)
	early_day_peace_ok = early_day_peace_ok and not override_forced.is_empty() and int(override_forced["behavior"]) == MonsterLibrary.AGGRO_BEHAVIOR
	# 런타임: 낮 주기 1의 필드 population을 40번 굴려도 선공몹이 한 마리도 없어야 한다.
	var day_saved_night := is_night
	var day_saved_cycle := cycle_number
	is_night = false
	cycle_number = 1
	for _day_probe in 40:
		var day_archetype := MonsterLibrary.roll(rng, cycle_number, is_night)
		early_day_peace_ok = early_day_peace_ok and int(day_archetype["behavior"]) != MonsterLibrary.AGGRO_BEHAVIOR
	is_night = day_saved_night
	cycle_number = day_saved_cycle

	# 모달 복귀 무적 검사(3차 피드백⑮). 대시 무적과 같은 경로(player.invulnerability)다.
	player.shield_charges = 0
	player.rollback_charges = 0
	player.dash_time_left = 0.0
	player.invulnerability = 0.0
	player.grace_invulnerability = 0.0
	_show_factory_menu("edit", {}, "playing")
	_close_factory_menu()
	var modal_invuln_ok := state == "playing" and player.invulnerability >= MODAL_RETURN_INVULN - 0.001
	modal_invuln_ok = modal_invuln_ok and player.grace_invulnerability > 0.0
	var health_before_modal := player.health
	player.take_damage(31.0, player.global_position + Vector2.RIGHT * 40.0)
	modal_invuln_ok = modal_invuln_ok and is_equal_approx(player.health, health_before_modal)
	# 음성 대조: 무적이 끝나면 다시 맞아야 한다(무적이 영구화되지 않았는지).
	player.invulnerability = 0.0
	player.grace_invulnerability = 0.0
	player.take_damage(31.0, player.global_position + Vector2.RIGHT * 40.0)
	modal_invuln_ok = modal_invuln_ok and player.health < health_before_modal
	player.heal_full()

	# 마왕 토스트 3초 유지 검사(3차 피드백⑲).
	# 예전 코드는 대기(tween_interval)와 페이드 아웃을 parallel()로 묶어 등장 0.35초 뒤에
	# 이미 투명해졌다. 유지 구간 중간에 완전 불투명인지 실제로 기다려 확인한다.
	boss_toast_queue.clear()
	if is_instance_valid(boss_toast):
		boss_toast.free()
	boss_toast = null
	_show_boss_growth_toast([{"id":"cleave", "name":"회전베기", "debt_desc":"토스트 유지 검사"}])
	var toast_hold_ok := BOSS_TOAST_HOLD >= 3.0 and is_instance_valid(boss_toast)
	var showing_toast := boss_toast
	# 표시 중에 새 사건이 오면 덮어쓰지 않고 대기열에 쌓여야 한다.
	_show_boss_growth_toast([{"id":"thunder", "name":"낙뢰", "debt_desc":"토스트 큐 검사"}])
	toast_hold_ok = toast_hold_ok and boss_toast == showing_toast and boss_toast_queue.size() == 1
	await get_tree().create_timer(BOSS_TOAST_FADE_IN + BOSS_TOAST_SLIDE_IN + 1.2, true, false, true).timeout
	toast_hold_ok = toast_hold_ok and is_instance_valid(boss_toast) and is_equal_approx(boss_toast.modulate.a, 1.0)
	# 현재 토스트가 끝나면 대기열의 다음 토스트가 이어서 뜬다.
	_finish_boss_growth_toast(boss_toast)
	toast_hold_ok = toast_hold_ok and boss_toast_queue.is_empty() and is_instance_valid(boss_toast)

	rejected_skills.assign(["cleave", "cleave", "rapid_slash", "thunder", "meteor_blade", "moon_barrier"])
	boss_items.assign(["u_greatsword_01", "r_ring_02"])
	var test_boss_deck := _build_boss_factory()
	var boss_shape_ok := test_boss_deck.slots.size() == 10
	for slot: Dictionary in test_boss_deck.slots:
		boss_shape_ok = boss_shape_ok and slot["lanes"].size() == 2
	_challenge_demon_king()
	var boss_preview_ok := state == "boss_preview" and get_tree().paused and boss_factory.slots.size() == 10
	# "돌아가서 준비한다"로 필드에 되돌아간 뒤 다시 도전할 수 있어야 한다 (P1-2).
	# 예전에는 마왕성에서 E를 누른 순간 되돌릴 방법이 없었다.
	_cancel_boss_preview()
	boss_preview_ok = boss_preview_ok and state == "playing" and not get_tree().paused
	_challenge_demon_king()
	boss_preview_ok = boss_preview_ok and state == "boss_preview" and get_tree().paused
	_begin_boss_battle()
	await get_tree().create_timer(0.16).timeout
	var boss_runtime_ok := state == "boss" and is_instance_valid(boss) and is_instance_valid(boss_cycle) and not boss_cycle.reload_enabled
	print("V4_TEST_COMPLETE items=%s skills28=%s combat_profiles=%s item_separate=%s initial=%s construction14=%s split3=%s fusion=%s physical_e=%s live_direction=%s choice_cancel=%s single_focus=%s immediate_place=%s item_pair_storage=%s drag_swap=%s early_ranged_gate=%s early_day_peace=%s modal_invuln=%s boss_toast_hold=%s boss20=%s boss_preview=%s boss_runtime=%s reload=%.2f" % [
		item_catalog_ok, skill_variety_ok, combat_profiles_ok, item_semantics_ok, initial_shape_ok, construction_ok, split_ok, fusion_ok, castle_input_ok, live_direction_ok, choice_ui_ok, single_focus_ok, immediate_place_ok, item_to_storage_ok, drag_swap_ok, early_ranged_ok, early_day_peace_ok, modal_invuln_ok, toast_hold_ok, boss_shape_ok, boss_preview_ok, boss_runtime_ok, factory.total_reload()
	])
	await _quit_test_cleanly()

func _run_v4_castle_test() -> void:
	automated_test = true
	_start_game()
	await get_tree().create_timer(0.2).timeout
	gold = 999
	var starter := world.get_nearest_interactable(Vector2(250.0, -250.0), 120.0)
	_enter_castle(starter)
	var fusion_npc_ok: bool = inside_castle and castle_interior.services.has("card_fusion") and castle_interior.services.size() == 4
	_show_card_shop(false)
	var shop_ok: bool = state == "camp" and shop_offers.size() == 4
	var old_gold := gold
	_refresh_shop(10)
	var refresh_ok: bool = shop_refresh_count == 1 and shop_offers.size() == 4 and gold == old_gold - 10
	# 상점의 아이템 카드 구매는 단일 구매 유지 + 즉시 배치 없이 보관함 직행이어야 한다.
	var shop_item_index := -1
	for offer_index in shop_offers.size():
		if String((shop_offers[offer_index]["card"] as Dictionary).get("kind", "skill")) == "item":
			shop_item_index = offer_index
			break
	var shop_storage_ok := shop_item_index >= 0
	if shop_storage_ok:
		var purchased_id := String((shop_offers[shop_item_index]["card"] as Dictionary).get("id", ""))
		var shop_storage_before := factory.inventory.size()
		_buy_shop_offer(shop_item_index)
		shop_storage_ok = state == "camp" and factory.inventory.size() == shop_storage_before + 1
		shop_storage_ok = shop_storage_ok and String(factory.inventory[factory.inventory.size() - 1].get("id", "")) == purchased_id
		shop_storage_ok = shop_storage_ok and bool(shop_offers[shop_item_index].get("sold", false))
	_close_base_camp()

	factory.add_inventory(DealCardLibrary.instance("time_cut", 1))
	factory.add_inventory(DealCardLibrary.instance("time_cut", 1))
	_show_fusion_service()
	_fuse_card("time_cut", 1)
	var npc_fusion_ok: bool = factory.get_rank_count("time_cut", 2) == 1
	if state == "camp": _close_base_camp()

	_show_factory_mage()
	_buy_factory_upgrade("split", 120)
	_factory_lane_pressed(0, 0)
	var mage_ok: bool = factory.split_upgrades_used == 1 and factory.slots[0]["lanes"].size() == 2 and state == "castle_interior"
	# 적용할 칸이 남지 않은 강화는 구매 자체가 막혀야 한다(소프트락 방지).
	# repeat를 모든 칸에 적용한 뒤 can_apply_upgrade가 false로 뒤집히는지 확인한다.
	var mage_gate_ok: bool = factory.can_apply_upgrade("repeat")
	for slot_index in factory.slots.size():
		factory.upgrade_slot(slot_index, "repeat")
	mage_gate_ok = mage_gate_ok and not factory.can_apply_upgrade("repeat")
	# 소진된 강화를 그래도 구매하면 골드가 환불되고 upgrade 화면으로 들어가지 않아야 한다.
	state = "camp"
	var mage_gate_gold := gold
	_buy_factory_upgrade("repeat", 95)
	mage_gate_ok = mage_gate_ok and gold == mage_gate_gold and state == "camp"
	# 정상 구매 뒤 ESC 취소는 지불한 골드를 그대로 돌려줘야 한다.
	var refund_gold := gold
	_buy_factory_upgrade("duration", 72)
	var refund_ok: bool = state == "factory_upgrade" and gold == refund_gold - 72
	_cancel_factory_upgrade()
	refund_ok = refund_ok and gold == refund_gold and state == "castle_interior"

	# 망각의 사제 / 운명의 직조사는 FactoryDeck(보관함 + 레일)을 봐야 한다.
	# 딜싸이클 개편 이후 두 서비스가 옛 player.applied_skills를 보고 있어 영구 고장이었다.
	gold = 500
	factory.add_inventory(DealCardLibrary.instance("cleave", 2))
	var remove_before := factory.inventory.size()
	var remove_rejections := rejected_skills.size()
	state = "camp"
	_use_service("skill_remove")
	var npc_remove_ok: bool = factory.inventory.size() == remove_before - 1 and rejected_skills.size() == remove_rejections + 1
	npc_remove_ok = npc_remove_ok and String(rejected_skills[rejected_skills.size() - 1]) == "cleave" and factory.get_rank_count("cleave", 2) == 0
	factory.add_inventory(DealCardLibrary.instance("thunder", 3))
	var swap_before := factory.inventory.size()
	state = "camp"
	_use_service("skill_swap")
	var npc_swap_ok: bool = factory.inventory.size() == swap_before and String(rejected_skills[rejected_skills.size() - 1]) == "thunder"
	npc_swap_ok = npc_swap_ok and int(factory.inventory[factory.inventory.size() - 1].get("rank", 0)) == 3
	if state == "camp":
		_close_base_camp()
	gold = 999

	factory.add_inventory(DealCardLibrary.instance("shield_bash", 5))
	factory.add_inventory(DealCardLibrary.instance("moon_barrier", 5))
	factory.add_inventory(ItemLibrary.instance("c_longsword_01"))
	_check_first_advancement()
	await get_tree().process_frame
	await get_tree().process_frame
	var first_choice_ready: bool = state == "factory_place" and player.advancement_tier == 1
	if first_choice_ready:
		_factory_lane_pressed(0, 0)
	var first_advancement_ok: bool = player.advancement_tier == 1 and not player.class_skill_id.is_empty() and boss_advancement_skills.size() == 1
	for direction in ["north", "east", "south", "west"]:
		player.add_trophy_orb(direction)
	var boosted_castles := 0
	for index in 100:
		if world.get_castle_services("boost_test_%d" % index, true).has("awakening_master"):
			boosted_castles += 1
	var awakening_boost_ok: bool = boosted_castles >= 80
	state = "camp"
	_begin_advancement(ClassLibrary.by_id("paladin"), 2)
	await get_tree().process_frame
	await get_tree().process_frame
	var second_choice_ready: bool = state == "factory_place" and player.advancement_tier == 2
	if second_choice_ready:
		_factory_lane_pressed(0, 0)
	var second_advancement_ok: bool = player.advancement_tier == 2 and player.trophy_orbs.is_empty() and boss_advancement_skills.size() == 2 and bool(DealCardLibrary.by_id(player.class_skill_id).get("tier2", false))
	print("V4_CASTLE_TEST_COMPLETE fusion_npc=%s shop=%s refresh=%s shop_item_storage=%s fusion=%s mage=%s mage_gate=%s upgrade_refund=%s npc_remove=%s npc_swap=%s first=%s awakening_boost=%s second=%s" % [fusion_npc_ok, shop_ok, refresh_ok, shop_storage_ok, npc_fusion_ok, mage_ok, mage_gate_ok, refund_ok, npc_remove_ok, npc_swap_ok, first_advancement_ok, awakening_boost_ok, second_advancement_ok])
	await _quit_test_cleanly()

func _run_stress_test() -> void:
	automated_test = true
	_start_game()
	await get_tree().create_timer(0.2).timeout
	cycle_number = 5
	player.invulnerability = 999.0
	for _rank in 3:
		player.apply_skill("flame_field")
		player.apply_skill("aura")
		player.apply_skill("guardian_blade")
	for index in 112:
		var angle := TAU * float(index) / 112.0
		var ring := 330.0 + float(index % 7) * 58.0
		var spawn_position := player.global_position + Vector2.from_angle(angle) * ring
		if not world.is_walkable(spawn_position):
			spawn_position = world.find_walkable_near(player.global_position, rng, 320.0, 760.0)
		_spawn_enemy_instance(spawn_position)
	for index in 135:
		spawn_burst(player.global_position + Vector2(index % 9, index % 7), GamePalette.CYAN, 8, 90.0, 0.55)
	for index in 100:
		_spawn_or_merge_xp_orb(player.global_position + Vector2(index * 2.0, 160.0), 1)
	_rebuild_enemy_spatial()
	await get_tree().create_timer(4.0).timeout
	var orb_count := get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("xp_orbs").size()
	var bounded_ok := active_enemies.size() <= MAX_ENEMIES + 26 and active_effect_nodes <= MAX_TRANSIENT_EFFECTS and orb_count <= MAX_XP_ORBS
	var structure_ok := not enemy_spatial.is_empty() and is_instance_valid(skill_effect_controller)
	print("STRESS_TEST_COMPLETE state=%s enemies=%d effects=%d xp_orbs=%d spatial_cells=%d fps=%d bounded=%s structure=%s" % [
		state, active_enemies.size(), active_effect_nodes, orb_count, enemy_spatial.size(), Engine.get_frames_per_second(), bounded_ok, structure_ok
	])
	await _quit_test_cleanly()

func _quit_test_cleanly() -> void:
	get_tree().paused = false
	if is_instance_valid(sound_manager) and sound_manager.has_method("stop_all"):
		sound_manager.stop_all()
	boss_toast_queue.clear()
	if is_instance_valid(boss_toast):
		boss_toast.free()
	boss_toast = null
	if is_instance_valid(overlay):
		overlay.free()
	overlay = null
	if is_instance_valid(gameplay_root):
		gameplay_root.free()
	gameplay_root = null
	await get_tree().create_timer(0.12).timeout
	get_tree().quit()

func _run_visual_capture(capture_name: String) -> void:
	automated_test = true
	match capture_name:
		"character":
			_show_character_select()
		"onboarding":
			# 2026-08-07: 온보딩은 완전 정적 화면이라 애니메이션 대기가 필요 없다.
			# 대신 페이지 전부를 -pN.png로 따로 저장해 한 번의 캡처로 4장을 모두 검수한다.
			var onboarding_directory := ProjectSettings.globalize_path("res://art/screenshots/qa")
			DirAccess.make_dir_recursive_absolute(onboarding_directory)
			for page_index in ONBOARDING_PAGE_COUNT:
				_show_onboarding(page_index)
				await get_tree().process_frame
				await get_tree().process_frame
				var page_path := onboarding_directory.path_join("onboarding-minimal-v2-p%d.png" % (page_index + 1))
				var page_error := get_viewport().get_texture().get_image().save_png(page_path)
				print("VISUAL_CAPTURE_PAGE page=%d path=%s error=%d" % [page_index + 1, page_path, page_error])
			# 대표 캡처(onboarding-minimal-v2.png)는 핵심 규칙 페이지인 딜싸이클로 남긴다.
			_show_onboarding(1)
		"world":
			_start_game()
			await get_tree().create_timer(0.55).timeout
			state = "preview"
			get_tree().paused = true
			_update_hud()
		"factory":
			_start_game()
			await get_tree().create_timer(0.35).timeout
			factory.place_card(0, 0, DealCardLibrary.instance("cleave", 2))
			factory.place_card(1, 0, ItemLibrary.instance("r_rapier_01"))
			factory.place_card(2, 0, DealCardLibrary.instance("rapid_slash", 1))
			factory.split_slot(1)
			factory.place_card(1, 1, DealCardLibrary.instance("time_cut", 1))
			for skill_id in ["flame_field", "thunder", "moon_barrier", "meteor_blade"]:
				factory.add_inventory(DealCardLibrary.instance(skill_id, 1))
			_show_factory_menu("edit")
			# 모달 등장 애니메이션이 끝난 뒤 찍어야 실제 화면 밝기로 확인할 수 있습니다.
			await get_tree().create_timer(0.45).timeout
		"effects":
			_start_game()
			await get_tree().create_timer(0.35).timeout
			factory.place_card(0, 0, DealCardLibrary.instance("sword_rain", 3))
			factory.place_card(1, 0, DealCardLibrary.instance("gravity_well", 2))
			factory.place_card(2, 0, DealCardLibrary.instance("blade_fan", 2))
			for index in 12:
				_spawn_enemy_instance(player.global_position + Vector2.from_angle(TAU * float(index) / 12.0) * 135.0, 2, "", false, "", false, "skeleton")
			_reset_player_cycle()
			await get_tree().create_timer(0.9).timeout
			state = "preview"
			get_tree().paused = true
			_update_hud()
		"boss":
			# 마왕 딜싸이클 미리보기는 클라이맥스 직전 화면인데 그동안 캡처 대상이 아니어서
			# 육안 검수를 한 번도 받지 못했습니다 (P1-2).
			_start_game()
			await get_tree().create_timer(0.35).timeout
			rejected_skills.assign(["cleave", "cleave", "rapid_slash", "thunder", "meteor_blade", "moon_barrier", "recursion", "execution"])
			boss_items.assign(["u_greatsword_01", "r_ring_02", "h_neck_01"])
			_challenge_demon_king()
			await get_tree().create_timer(0.4).timeout
		"result":
			_start_game()
			await get_tree().create_timer(0.35).timeout
			for skill_id in ["cleave", "rapid_slash", "thunder"]:
				factory.build_next_piece()
				factory.build_next_piece()
				factory.place_card(factory.slots.size() - 1, 0, DealCardLibrary.instance(skill_id, 2))
			factory.place_card(0, 0, DealCardLibrary.instance("time_cut", 3))
			rejected_skills.assign(["cleave", "thunder", "moon_barrier"])
			_show_result(true)
			await get_tree().create_timer(0.45).timeout
		_:
			pass
	await get_tree().process_frame
	await get_tree().process_frame
	var capture_directory := ProjectSettings.globalize_path("res://art/screenshots/qa")
	DirAccess.make_dir_recursive_absolute(capture_directory)
	var capture_path := capture_directory.path_join("%s-minimal-v2.png" % capture_name)
	var error := get_viewport().get_texture().get_image().save_png(capture_path)
	print("VISUAL_CAPTURE_COMPLETE name=%s path=%s error=%d" % [capture_name, capture_path, error])
	get_tree().paused = false
	get_tree().quit()

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	if state == "castle_interior":
		interaction_timer -= delta
		if interaction_timer <= 0.0:
			interaction_timer = 0.1
			_refresh_castle_interactable()
		return
	if state not in ["playing", "boss"]:
		return
	elapsed_time += delta
	if not automated_test and OS.get_cmdline_user_args().is_empty():
		run_save_timer -= delta
		if run_save_timer <= 0.0:
			run_save_timer = 5.0
			_save_run_snapshot()
	if state == "playing":
		phase_elapsed += delta
		var duration := NIGHT_DURATION if is_night else DAY_DURATION
		if phase_elapsed >= duration:
			_toggle_day_night()
		spawn_timer -= delta
		if spawn_timer <= 0.0:
			_maintain_field_population()
			spawn_timer = _current_spawn_interval()
		interaction_timer -= delta
		if interaction_timer <= 0.0:
			interaction_timer = 0.12
			_refresh_interactable()
			_check_trial_camps()
	_update_world_lighting(delta)
	spatial_refresh_timer -= delta
	if spatial_refresh_timer <= 0.0:
		spatial_refresh_timer = 0.12
		_rebuild_enemy_spatial()
	hud_refresh_timer -= delta
	if hud_refresh_timer <= 0.0:
		hud_refresh_timer = 0.1
		_update_hud()
	# 되감기 릴만 매 프레임 갱신합니다(스타일박스는 만들지 않고 위치·스케일·알파만 씁니다).
	_update_cycle_rewind()

func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if not event is InputEventKey or not event.pressed or event.echo:
		return
	var key_event := event as InputEventKey
	if state == "onboarding":
		if key_event.keycode in [KEY_LEFT, KEY_A]:
			_show_onboarding(maxi(0, onboarding_page - 1))
		elif key_event.keycode in [KEY_RIGHT, KEY_D] and onboarding_page < ONBOARDING_PAGE_COUNT - 1:
			_show_onboarding(onboarding_page + 1)
		elif key_event.keycode in [KEY_SPACE, KEY_ENTER]:
			if onboarding_page >= ONBOARDING_PAGE_COUNT - 1:
				_finish_onboarding()
			else:
				_show_onboarding(onboarding_page + 1)
		elif key_event.keycode == KEY_ESCAPE:
			_show_character_select()
		return
	if state == "character_select":
		if key_event.keycode in [KEY_LEFT, KEY_A]:
			_cycle_character(-1)
		elif key_event.keycode in [KEY_RIGHT, KEY_D]:
			_cycle_character(1)
		elif key_event.keycode in [KEY_SPACE, KEY_ENTER] and lobby_character_index == 0:
			_confirm_character_selection("swordsman")
		elif key_event.keycode == KEY_ESCAPE:
			_show_menu()
		return
	if state == "settings":
		if key_event.keycode == KEY_ESCAPE:
			_show_menu()
		return
	if state in ["choice", "advancement_choice", "item_choice"]:
		_handle_choice_keyboard(key_event)
		return
	if state in ["factory_menu", "factory_place", "factory_upgrade"]:
		_handle_factory_keyboard(key_event)
		return
	# 마우스 없이도 빠져나올 수 있어야 하는 모달들 (P1-5).
	if state == "camp":
		if key_event.keycode == KEY_ESCAPE:
			_close_base_camp()
		return
	if state == "evolution":
		if key_event.keycode in [KEY_SPACE, KEY_ENTER]:
			_accept_evolution()
		return
	if state == "boss_preview":
		if key_event.keycode == KEY_ESCAPE:
			_cancel_boss_preview()
		elif key_event.keycode in [KEY_SPACE, KEY_ENTER]:
			_begin_boss_battle()
		return
	if key_event.keycode == KEY_ESCAPE:
		if state == "factory_menu":
			_close_factory_menu()
		elif state in ["won", "lost"]:
			_show_menu()
		elif state in ["playing", "boss", "castle_interior"]:
			_show_factory_menu("edit")
		return
	if state == "menu" and key_event.keycode in [KEY_SPACE, KEY_ENTER]:
		_show_character_select()
	elif state in ["playing", "castle_interior"] and (event.is_action_pressed("interact") or key_event.keycode == KEY_E or key_event.physical_keycode == KEY_E):
		if state == "castle_interior":
			_refresh_castle_interactable()
		else:
			_refresh_interactable()
		_interact_with_world()
	elif state in ["won", "lost"] and key_event.keycode == KEY_R:
		_start_game()
	elif state in ["won", "lost"] and key_event.keycode == KEY_L:
		_show_menu()

func _ensure_input_actions() -> void:
	var actions := {
		"move_left": [KEY_A, KEY_LEFT],
		"move_right": [KEY_D, KEY_RIGHT],
		"move_up": [KEY_W, KEY_UP],
		"move_down": [KEY_S, KEY_DOWN],
		"dash": [KEY_SHIFT],
		"interact": [KEY_E]
	}
	for action_name: String in actions:
		if not InputMap.has_action(action_name):
			InputMap.add_action(action_name)
		for key_code: int in actions[action_name]:
			var input_event := InputEventKey.new()
			input_event.keycode = key_code
			if not InputMap.action_has_event(action_name, input_event):
				InputMap.action_add_event(action_name, input_event)
			if action_name == "interact":
				var physical_event := InputEventKey.new()
				physical_event.physical_keycode = key_code
				if not InputMap.action_has_event(action_name, physical_event):
					InputMap.action_add_event(action_name, physical_event)

# 필드 HUD 패널 사각형 (P1-3)
# 낮/밤 표시와 경험치 줄은 예전에 배경 없이 초원 위에 얹혀 있었습니다.
# 좌표를 상수로 올려 두면 _update_hud()의 게이지 폭 계산이 패널 폭과 항상 함께 움직입니다.
const HUD_PHASE_PANEL := Rect2(430.0, 16.0, 420.0, 80.0)
const HUD_PHASE_BAR := Rect2(14.0, 62.0, 392.0, 9.0)
const HUD_XP_PANEL := Rect2(334.0, 652.0, 612.0, 52.0)
const HUD_XP_BAR := Rect2(46.0, 32.0, 552.0, 11.0)

func _build_ui() -> void:
	ui_layer = CanvasLayer.new()
	ui_layer.layer = 20
	add_child(ui_layer)
	ui_root = Control.new()
	ui_root.name = "UIRoot"
	ui_root.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	ui_root.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	ui_root.theme = _build_ui_theme()
	ui_layer.add_child(ui_root)
	hud = Control.new()
	hud.name = "HUD"
	hud.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	hud.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	ui_root.add_child(hud)

	# 왼쪽 위 신상 패널: 직업/레벨 → 체력 게이지 → 보호막. 체력 수치는 그동안 화면에
	# 어디에도 없었으므로(⑰ "체력 표시 포함") 게이지와 숫자를 여기서 처음으로 보여준다.
	var health_panel := _hud_panel(Vector2(18.0, 16.0), Vector2(345.0, 112.0), GamePalette.YELLOW)
	class_text = _label("왕국 검사  LV 1", 15, GamePalette.YELLOW)
	class_text.position = Vector2(14.0, 7.0)
	class_text.size = Vector2(310.0, 22.0)
	health_panel.add_child(class_text)
	var health_track := ColorRect.new()
	health_track.position = Vector2(14.0, 33.0)
	health_track.size = Vector2(313.0, 18.0)
	health_track.color = UI_CHIP_BG
	health_track.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	health_panel.add_child(health_track)
	health_fill = ColorRect.new()
	health_fill.position = Vector2(2.0, 2.0)
	health_fill.size = Vector2(309.0, 14.0)
	health_fill.color = GamePalette.GREEN
	health_fill.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	health_track.add_child(health_fill)
	health_text = _label("100 / 100", UI_BODY_SIZE, GamePalette.TEXT)
	health_text.position = Vector2(14.0, 34.0)
	health_text.size = Vector2(313.0, 17.0)
	health_text.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	health_text.vertical_alignment = VERTICAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	health_panel.add_child(health_text)
	shield_text = _label("수호 0  ·  부활 0", UI_LABEL_SIZE, GamePalette.BLUE)
	shield_text.position = Vector2(14.0, 55.0)
	shield_text.size = Vector2(313.0, 18.0)
	health_panel.add_child(shield_text)
	# 예전에는 마물 수·골드가 패널 밖 초원 위에 떠 있어 잘 읽히지 않았습니다.
	# 같은 신상 패널 안으로 넣고 얇은 구분선으로 층을 나눕니다 (⑰).
	var health_divider := ColorRect.new()
	health_divider.position = Vector2(14.0, 77.0)
	health_divider.size = Vector2(313.0, 1.0)
	health_divider.color = UI_EDGE_SOFT
	health_divider.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	health_panel.add_child(health_divider)

	# 영구 비표시였던 fate_hud_panel이 쓰던 좌표를 그대로 물려받습니다 (P1-3).
	var quest_panel := _hud_panel(Vector2(929.0, 16.0), Vector2(333.0, 72.0), GamePalette.MAGENTA)

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 필드 HUD 딜싸이클 (이전 · 현재 · 다음)
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 전투 중에 시야를 가리지 않도록 텍스트 목록 대신 공장 레일과 같은 시각 언어의
# 카드 칩 미니어처를 사용합니다. 세 칩은 하나의 스파인 라인 위에 놓이고
# 현재 칸만 크고 밝게, 이전/다음은 작고 흐리게 그립니다. 패널 사각형(334,568,612×78)은
# 예전 텍스트 버전과 같은 자리를 유지해 다른 HUD 요소가 밀리지 않습니다.
const CYCLE_HUD_RECT := Rect2(334.0, 568.0, 612.0, 78.0)
const CYCLE_CHIP_SIDE := 44.0
const CYCLE_CHIP_MAIN := 64.0
const CYCLE_HUD_LINE_Y := 36.0
# 칩 3개는 등간격(이전 86 · 현재 216 · 다음 366)으로 붙여 놓고, 정보 열은 패널 오른쪽
# 끝(438~598)으로 밀어 냅니다. 예전에는 정보 열이 가운데(258~428)에 끼어 있어 "다음" 칩만
# 508에 홀로 떨어져 보였습니다 (P1-4).
const CYCLE_HUD_BAR_W := 160.0
const CYCLE_HUD_INFO_X := 438.0
const CYCLE_HUD_NEXT_X := 344.0

# RELOAD 되감기 릴 (3차 피드백 ⑯)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 사용자 요구 원문: "게이지 타이머가 돌아가면서 딜싸이클이 되감아지는 형태의 애니메이션".
# 예전 표현(가운데 칩을 RELOAD 아이콘으로 바꾸고 패널 전체를 주황 박스로 강조)은 지우고
# 다음 세 요소로 대체한다.
#   ① 재생 헤드(평상시 현재 칸 자리, x=CYCLE_REWIND_PLAYHEAD_X) 주위의 원형 스윕 게이지.
#      남은 RELOAD가 12시부터 시계방향으로 비워진다.
#   ② 같은 자리를 지나가는 칩 릴. 칸 번호가 마지막 칸 → 01로 줄어들면서
#      칩들이 미끄러지고, 재생 헤드에서 멀어질수록 작아지고 흐려진다(잔상 대신 초점 렌즈).
#      감속(ease out)이 걸려 RELOAD가 끝나는 순간 칸 01이 재생 헤드에 정확히 멈춘다.
#   ③ 오른쪽 정보 열의 남은 초 텍스트 + 되감기 바(오른쪽에서 왼쪽으로 줄어듦).
# 박스 프레임 강조는 쓰지 않고, 스타일박스는 빌드 때 한 번만 만든다.
const CYCLE_REWIND_PLAYHEAD_X := 216.0
const CYCLE_REWIND_CHIP_SIDE := 56.0
const CYCLE_REWIND_PITCH := 78.0
const CYCLE_REWIND_CHIPS := 7
const CYCLE_REWIND_FADE_SPAN := 1.4
const CYCLE_REWIND_GAUGE_R := 33.0
const CYCLE_REWIND_GAUGE_W := 4.0
const CYCLE_REWIND_INFO_X := 372.0
const CYCLE_REWIND_INFO_W := 226.0
# 1.0이면 칸 번호가 줄어드는 동안 칩이 오른쪽으로 흘러간다(테이프를 되감는 방향).
# -1.0으로 바꾸면 같은 카운트다운을 반대 방향으로 흘려보낸다.
const CYCLE_REWIND_TRAVEL := 1.0

# 원형 스윕 게이지. 남은 비율만 링 + 옅은 부채꼴로 그린다.
class CycleSweepGauge:
	extends Control

	var ratio := 1.0
	var radius := 33.0
	var thickness := 5.0
	var ring_color := Color("e78a45")
	var track_color := Color("232c3d")

	func _draw() -> void:
		var center := size * 0.5
		draw_arc(center, radius, 0.0, TAU, 48, track_color, thickness, false)
		var visible_ratio := clampf(ratio, 0.0, 1.0)
		if visible_ratio <= 0.004:
			return
		var start := -PI * 0.5
		var end := start + TAU * visible_ratio
		var steps := maxi(3, int(ceil(48.0 * visible_ratio)))
		var sector := PackedVector2Array()
		sector.append(center)
		for step in steps + 1:
			sector.append(center + Vector2.from_angle(lerpf(start, end, float(step) / float(steps))) * radius)
		draw_colored_polygon(sector, Color(ring_color, 0.07))
		draw_arc(center, radius, start, end, 48, Color(ring_color, 0.88), thickness, false)
		# 12시 기준점. 되감기가 어디서 끝나는지 눈으로 고정해 준다.
		draw_rect(Rect2(center + Vector2(-1.0, -radius - thickness), Vector2(2.0, thickness * 2.0)), ring_color, true)

func _build_cycle_hud() -> void:
	cycle_hud_panel = Panel.new()
	cycle_hud_panel.position = CYCLE_HUD_RECT.position
	cycle_hud_panel.size = CYCLE_HUD_RECT.size
	cycle_hud_panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(Color("0f1521ed"), UI_EDGE_SOFT, 0, UI_BORDER_FRAME))
	hud.add_child(cycle_hud_panel)
	# 공장 레일과 같은 연속 스파인 라인. 칩과 게이지가 이 선 위에 인라인으로 올라갑니다.
	var spine := ColorRect.new()
	spine.position = Vector2(10.0, CYCLE_HUD_LINE_Y - 2.0)
	spine.size = Vector2(CYCLE_HUD_RECT.size.x - 20.0, 4.0)
	spine.color = FACTORY_RAIL_SPINE_BUILT.darkened(0.18)
	spine.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	cycle_hud_panel.add_child(spine)
	# 되감기 중에만 켜지는 속도 잔상. 스파인 위에 겹쳐 흐르는 느낌만 준다.
	cycle_rewind_streak = ColorRect.new()
	cycle_rewind_streak.position = Vector2(CYCLE_REWIND_PLAYHEAD_X - CYCLE_REWIND_PITCH * CYCLE_REWIND_FADE_SPAN, CYCLE_HUD_LINE_Y - 2.0)
	cycle_rewind_streak.size = Vector2(CYCLE_REWIND_PITCH * CYCLE_REWIND_FADE_SPAN * 2.0, 4.0)
	cycle_rewind_streak.color = Color(GamePalette.ORANGE, 0.0)
	cycle_rewind_streak.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	cycle_hud_panel.add_child(cycle_rewind_streak)

	cycle_chip_previous = _build_cycle_chip(Vector2(64.0, CYCLE_HUD_LINE_Y - CYCLE_CHIP_SIDE * 0.5), CYCLE_CHIP_SIDE, false)
	var previous_caption := _label("이전", 10, GamePalette.MUTED.darkened(0.2))
	previous_caption.position = Vector2(54.0, 58.0)
	previous_caption.size = Vector2(64.0, 14.0)
	previous_caption.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	cycle_hud_panel.add_child(previous_caption)

	cycle_chip_current = _build_cycle_chip(Vector2(184.0, CYCLE_HUD_LINE_Y - CYCLE_CHIP_MAIN * 0.5), CYCLE_CHIP_MAIN, true)
	cycle_chip_next = _build_cycle_chip(Vector2(CYCLE_HUD_NEXT_X, CYCLE_HUD_LINE_Y - CYCLE_CHIP_SIDE * 0.5), CYCLE_CHIP_SIDE, false)
	var next_caption := _label("다음", 10, GamePalette.MUTED.darkened(0.2))
	next_caption.position = Vector2(CYCLE_HUD_NEXT_X - 10.0, 58.0)
	next_caption.size = Vector2(64.0, 14.0)
	next_caption.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	cycle_hud_panel.add_child(next_caption)

	cycle_slot_text = _label("칸 01", UI_LABEL_SIZE, GamePalette.YELLOW)
	cycle_slot_text.position = Vector2(CYCLE_HUD_INFO_X, 8.0)
	cycle_slot_text.size = Vector2(CYCLE_HUD_BAR_W, 20.0)
	cycle_hud_panel.add_child(cycle_slot_text)
	# 진행 게이지는 스파인 라인 위에 그대로 얹혀 레일이 굵어진 것처럼 보입니다.
	var deal_progress_back := ColorRect.new()
	deal_progress_back.position = Vector2(CYCLE_HUD_INFO_X, CYCLE_HUD_LINE_Y - 5.0)
	deal_progress_back.size = Vector2(CYCLE_HUD_BAR_W, 10.0)
	deal_progress_back.color = UI_CHIP_BG
	cycle_hud_panel.add_child(deal_progress_back)
	cycle_progress_fill = ColorRect.new()
	cycle_progress_fill.position = Vector2(2.0, 2.0)
	cycle_progress_fill.size = Vector2(0.0, 6.0)
	cycle_progress_fill.color = GamePalette.CYAN
	deal_progress_back.add_child(cycle_progress_fill)
	cycle_status_text = _label("0.00 / 0.00초", 10, GamePalette.CYAN)
	cycle_status_text.position = Vector2(CYCLE_HUD_INFO_X, 48.0)
	cycle_status_text.size = Vector2(CYCLE_HUD_BAR_W, 18.0)
	cycle_hud_panel.add_child(cycle_status_text)
	cycle_run_nodes = [cycle_chip_previous, cycle_chip_current, cycle_chip_next, previous_caption, next_caption, cycle_slot_text, deal_progress_back, cycle_status_text]

	_build_cycle_rewind_reel()

func _build_cycle_rewind_reel() -> void:
	cycle_rewind_reel = Control.new()
	cycle_rewind_reel.position = Vector2(6.0, 2.0)
	cycle_rewind_reel.size = Vector2(CYCLE_HUD_RECT.size.x - 12.0, CYCLE_HUD_RECT.size.y - 4.0)
	cycle_rewind_reel.clip_contents = true
	cycle_rewind_reel.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	cycle_hud_panel.add_child(cycle_rewind_reel)
	cycle_rewind_chips.clear()
	for _index in CYCLE_REWIND_CHIPS:
		cycle_rewind_chips.append(_build_cycle_rewind_chip())

	cycle_rewind_gauge = CycleSweepGauge.new()
	var gauge_side := (CYCLE_REWIND_GAUGE_R + CYCLE_REWIND_GAUGE_W) * 2.0 + 4.0
	cycle_rewind_gauge.position = Vector2(CYCLE_REWIND_PLAYHEAD_X - gauge_side * 0.5, CYCLE_HUD_LINE_Y - gauge_side * 0.5)
	cycle_rewind_gauge.size = Vector2(gauge_side, gauge_side)
	cycle_rewind_gauge.radius = CYCLE_REWIND_GAUGE_R
	cycle_rewind_gauge.thickness = CYCLE_REWIND_GAUGE_W
	cycle_rewind_gauge.ring_color = GamePalette.ORANGE
	cycle_rewind_gauge.track_color = UI_EDGE_SOFT
	cycle_rewind_gauge.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	cycle_hud_panel.add_child(cycle_rewind_gauge)

	var rewind_caption := _label("RELOAD 되감기", 10, GamePalette.ORANGE)
	rewind_caption.position = Vector2(CYCLE_REWIND_INFO_X, 9.0)
	rewind_caption.size = Vector2(CYCLE_REWIND_INFO_W, 16.0)
	rewind_caption.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT
	cycle_hud_panel.add_child(rewind_caption)
	cycle_rewind_time_text = _label("0.00초", 22, GamePalette.TEXT)
	cycle_rewind_time_text.position = Vector2(CYCLE_REWIND_INFO_X, 26.0)
	cycle_rewind_time_text.size = Vector2(CYCLE_REWIND_INFO_W, 30.0)
	cycle_rewind_time_text.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT
	cycle_hud_panel.add_child(cycle_rewind_time_text)
	var rewind_bar_back := ColorRect.new()
	rewind_bar_back.position = Vector2(CYCLE_REWIND_INFO_X, 60.0)
	rewind_bar_back.size = Vector2(CYCLE_REWIND_INFO_W, 7.0)
	rewind_bar_back.color = UI_CHIP_BG
	rewind_bar_back.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	cycle_hud_panel.add_child(rewind_bar_back)
	cycle_rewind_bar_fill = ColorRect.new()
	cycle_rewind_bar_fill.position = Vector2(1.0, 1.0)
	cycle_rewind_bar_fill.size = Vector2(CYCLE_REWIND_INFO_W - 2.0, 5.0)
	cycle_rewind_bar_fill.color = GamePalette.ORANGE
	cycle_rewind_bar_fill.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	rewind_bar_back.add_child(cycle_rewind_bar_fill)

	cycle_rewind_nodes = [cycle_rewind_reel, cycle_rewind_gauge, rewind_caption, cycle_rewind_time_text, rewind_bar_back]
	_set_cycle_rewind_visible(false)

func _build_cycle_rewind_chip() -> Panel:
	var chip := Panel.new()
	chip.size = Vector2(CYCLE_REWIND_CHIP_SIDE, CYCLE_REWIND_CHIP_SIDE)
	chip.pivot_offset = chip.size * 0.5
	chip.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	chip.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(UI_CHIP_BG, UI_EDGE_SOFT, 0, UI_BORDER_FRAME))
	cycle_rewind_reel.add_child(chip)
	var icon := SKILL_ICON_SCRIPT.new()
	icon.name = "Icon"
	icon.position = Vector2(6.0, 3.0)
	icon.size = Vector2(CYCLE_REWIND_CHIP_SIDE - 12.0, CYCLE_REWIND_CHIP_SIDE - 19.0)
	icon.setup("basic", GamePalette.STONE_LIGHT, false)
	chip.add_child(icon)
	var number := _label("칸 01", 10, GamePalette.MUTED)
	number.name = "Number"
	number.position = Vector2(2.0, CYCLE_REWIND_CHIP_SIDE - 17.0)
	number.size = Vector2(CYCLE_REWIND_CHIP_SIDE - 4.0, 15.0)
	number.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	chip.add_child(number)
	return chip

func _set_cycle_rewind_visible(active: bool) -> void:
	for node: Control in cycle_rewind_nodes:
		if is_instance_valid(node):
			node.visible = active
	for node: Control in cycle_run_nodes:
		if is_instance_valid(node):
			node.visible = not active
	if is_instance_valid(cycle_rewind_streak) and not active:
		cycle_rewind_streak.color = Color(GamePalette.ORANGE, 0.0)

func _update_cycle_rewind() -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(cycle_rewind_reel) or not is_instance_valid(cycle_hud_panel) or factory == null:
		return
	var rewinding := cycle_hud_panel.visible and not inside_castle and is_instance_valid(player_cycle) and player_cycle.reloading and not factory.slots.is_empty()
	if rewinding != cycle_rewind_active:
		cycle_rewind_active = rewinding
		_set_cycle_rewind_visible(rewinding)
		if not rewinding:
			# 되감기가 끝난 프레임에 바로 칸 01 상태로 이어지도록 즉시 갱신합니다.
			_update_deal_cycle_hud()
	if not rewinding:
		return
	var slot_count := maxi(1, factory.slots.size())
	var phase := clampf(player_cycle.progress_ratio(), 0.0, 1.0)
	# 뒤로 갈수록 느려지는 감속. 칩이 적을 때도 최소 3칸 이상 흘러가도록 한 바퀴를 더 돈다.
	var loops := maxi(1, ceili(4.0 / float(slot_count)))
	var travel := float(slot_count * loops) - 1.0
	var eased := 1.0 - pow(1.0 - phase, 2.4)
	var virtual_slot := (1.0 - eased) * travel
	var base := floori(virtual_slot)
	var fraction := virtual_slot - float(base)
	var offset := -fraction * CYCLE_REWIND_PITCH * CYCLE_REWIND_TRAVEL
	var half := int((CYCLE_REWIND_CHIPS - 1) / 2)
	for chip_index in cycle_rewind_chips.size():
		var chip := cycle_rewind_chips[chip_index]
		if not is_instance_valid(chip):
			continue
		var column := chip_index - half
		var center_x := CYCLE_REWIND_PLAYHEAD_X + float(column) * CYCLE_REWIND_PITCH + offset
		var closeness := clampf(1.0 - absf(center_x - CYCLE_REWIND_PLAYHEAD_X) / (CYCLE_REWIND_PITCH * CYCLE_REWIND_FADE_SPAN), 0.0, 1.0)
		chip.position = Vector2(center_x - cycle_rewind_reel.position.x - CYCLE_REWIND_CHIP_SIDE * 0.5, CYCLE_HUD_LINE_Y - cycle_rewind_reel.position.y - CYCLE_REWIND_CHIP_SIDE * 0.5)
		chip.scale = Vector2.ONE * lerpf(0.7, 1.0, closeness)
		chip.modulate = Color(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, pow(closeness, 0.85))
		chip.rotation = deg_to_rad(lerpf(7.0, 0.0, closeness) * signf(center_x - CYCLE_REWIND_PLAYHEAD_X))
		_apply_cycle_rewind_chip(chip, wrapi(base + column, 0, slot_count))
	cycle_rewind_gauge.ratio = 1.0 - phase
	cycle_rewind_gauge.queue_redraw()
	cycle_rewind_time_text.text = "%.2f초" % player_cycle.reload_remaining
	cycle_rewind_bar_fill.size.x = (CYCLE_REWIND_INFO_W - 2.0) * (1.0 - phase)
	# 릴이 빠를 때만 잔상이 진해집니다(감속과 함께 자연히 사라짐).
	cycle_rewind_streak.color = Color(GamePalette.ORANGE, clampf(pow(1.0 - phase, 1.6) * 0.16, 0.0, 0.16))

func _apply_cycle_rewind_chip(chip: Panel, slot_index: int) -> void:
	var card := _cycle_slot_lead_card(slot_index)
	var color := GamePalette.STONE_LIGHT if card.is_empty() else _factory_card_color(card)
	var icon := chip.get_node_or_null("Icon") as PixelSkillIcon
	if is_instance_valid(icon):
		var visual_id := "basic" if card.is_empty() else String(card.get("id", "basic"))
		if icon.skill_id != visual_id or icon.icon_color != color:
			icon.setup(visual_id, color, false)
	var number := chip.get_node_or_null("Number") as Label
	if is_instance_valid(number):
		number.text = "칸 %02d" % (slot_index + 1)

func _build_cycle_chip(chip_position: Vector2, chip_side: float, emphasised: bool) -> Panel:
	var chip := Panel.new()
	chip.position = chip_position
	chip.size = Vector2(chip_side, chip_side)
	chip.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	chip.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(UI_CHIP_BG, UI_EDGE_SOFT, 0, UI_BORDER_CARD if emphasised else UI_BORDER_FRAME))
	cycle_hud_panel.add_child(chip)
	var icon_side := chip_side - (16.0 if emphasised else 10.0)
	var icon := SKILL_ICON_SCRIPT.new()
	icon.name = "Icon"
	icon.position = Vector2((chip_side - icon_side) * 0.5, 4.0)
	icon.size = Vector2(icon_side, icon_side)
	icon.setup("basic", GamePalette.STONE_LIGHT, false)
	chip.add_child(icon)
	var badge := _label("", 11, GamePalette.YELLOW)
	badge.name = "Badge"
	badge.position = Vector2(chip_side - 28.0, chip_side - 21.0)
	badge.size = Vector2(24.0, 16.0)
	badge.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT
	badge.visible = false
	chip.add_child(badge)
	return chip

func _cycle_slot_lead_card(slot_index: int) -> Dictionary:
	# 동시 레인이 있는 칸은 대표 카드 하나만 칩에 표시하고 레인 수는 뱃지로 알립니다.
	if factory == null or slot_index < 0 or slot_index >= factory.slots.size():
		return {}
	for lane in factory.slots[slot_index].get("lanes", []):
		if lane is Dictionary and not (lane as Dictionary).is_empty():
			return lane as Dictionary
	return {}

func _apply_cycle_chip(chip: Panel, slot_index: int, emphasised: bool) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(chip) or factory == null:
		return
	var card := _cycle_slot_lead_card(slot_index)
	var lane_count := 1
	if slot_index >= 0 and slot_index < factory.slots.size():
		lane_count = maxi(1, (factory.slots[slot_index].get("lanes", []) as Array).size())
	var color := GamePalette.STONE_LIGHT if card.is_empty() else _factory_card_color(card)
	var icon := chip.get_node_or_null("Icon") as PixelSkillIcon
	var badge := chip.get_node_or_null("Badge") as Label
	if is_instance_valid(icon):
		var visual_id := String(card.get("id", "basic")) if not card.is_empty() else "basic"
		if icon.skill_id != visual_id or icon.icon_color != color:
			icon.setup(visual_id, color, false)
	if is_instance_valid(badge):
		badge.visible = lane_count > 1
		badge.text = "×%d" % lane_count
	# 스타일박스는 색·강조가 바뀔 때만 새로 만듭니다(HUD는 초당 10회 갱신됩니다).
	var style_key := "%s|%d" % [color.to_html(false), int(emphasised)]
	if String(chip.get_meta("cycle_style_key", "")) != style_key:
		chip.set_meta("cycle_style_key", style_key)
		var border := color.darkened(0.15) if emphasised else color.darkened(0.55)
		var background := color.darkened(0.74) if emphasised else color.darkened(0.86)
		chip.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(background, border, 0, UI_BORDER_CARD if emphasised else UI_BORDER_FRAME))
	chip.modulate = Color(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0 if emphasised else 0.6)

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 딜싸이클 공장 UI
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 전체 레일은 항상 최대 10칸 자리를 그립니다. 건설된 칸만 색이 채워지고
# 나머지는 무채색 자리표시자로 남아 앞으로 몇 칸을 더 열 수 있는지 보여줍니다.
# 모든 셀은 같은 높이(FACTORY_RAIL_ROW_H)를 쓰고 다리는 카드 세로 중앙선
# (FACTORY_RAIL_LINE_Y)에 인라인으로 놓여 레일이 한 줄로 이어져 보입니다.
const FACTORY_RAIL_CARD_W := 200.0
const FACTORY_RAIL_CARD_INSET := 5.0
const FACTORY_RAIL_BRIDGE_W := 70.0
const FACTORY_RAIL_SPINE_BUILT := Color("46707f")
const FACTORY_RAIL_SPINE_GHOST := Color("242b37")
const FACTORY_RAIL_HEADER_TOP := 4.0
const FACTORY_RAIL_HEADER_H := 26.0
const FACTORY_RAIL_LANE_TOP := 34.0
const FACTORY_RAIL_LANE_AREA := 266.0
const FACTORY_RAIL_LANE_GAP := 8.0
const FACTORY_RAIL_ROW_H := 302.0
const FACTORY_RAIL_LINE_Y := 167.0
const FACTORY_RAIL_PANEL_TOP := 173.0
const FACTORY_RAIL_PANEL_H := 342.0
# 공장 레일 카드 = 선택 모달·요약에서 재사용하는 표준 카드 블록 크기 (3차 피드백 ⑳).
const FACTORY_CARD_BLOCK_SIZE := Vector2(FACTORY_RAIL_CARD_W - FACTORY_RAIL_CARD_INSET * 2.0, 142.0)
# 다리 스프라이트를 스파인 위에 얹을 때 쓰는 사각형. 아틀라스 프레임을 벗긴 뒤의
# 실제 다리 비율(약 2:1)에 맞춘 크기라 세로로 눌리지 않습니다 (3차 피드백 ⑫).
const FACTORY_RAIL_BRIDGE_SPRITE := Vector2(58.0, 30.0)

func _show_factory_menu(mode: String = "edit", acquired_card: Dictionary = {}, return_state_override: String = "") -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(player) or factory == null:
		return
	if mode == "edit":
		if not return_state_override.is_empty():
			factory_return_state = return_state_override
		elif state != "factory_menu":
			factory_return_state = state
	elif not return_state_override.is_empty():
		factory_return_state = return_state_override
	factory_mode = mode
	pending_factory_card = acquired_card.duplicate(true)
	factory_lane_buttons.clear()
	factory_lane_coordinates.clear()
	factory_inventory_buttons.clear()
	factory_inventory_indices.clear()
	factory_focus_zone = "rail"
	factory_focus_index = 0
	factory_selected_inventory = -1
	state = "factory_menu" if mode == "edit" else "factory_place" if mode == "place" else "factory_upgrade" if mode.begins_with("upgrade") else "factory_build"
	get_tree().paused = true
	_clear_overlay()
	overlay = Control.new()
	overlay.name = "DealCycleFactory"
	overlay.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	overlay.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	ui_root.add_child(overlay)
	var dim := ColorRect.new()
	dim.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	dim.color = Color("07101bea")
	overlay.add_child(dim)
	var panel := Panel.new()
	panel.position = Vector2(20.0, 16.0)
	panel.size = Vector2(1240.0, 688.0)
	panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(UI_MODAL_BG, UI_EDGE, 0, UI_BORDER_MODAL))
	overlay.add_child(panel)

	var title_text := "딜싸이클 공장 · 전체 레일"
	var subtitle_text := "카드를 끌어 놓아 교체 · 빈칸은 기본 베기 · 아이템 카드는 주변 또는 전체 스킬을 강화"
	if mode == "place":
		title_text = "새 카드 입고 · 교체할 칸 선택"
		subtitle_text = "%s 카드를 배치하면 즉시 필드로 돌아갑니다." % _factory_card_name(acquired_card)
	elif mode == "build":
		title_text = "공장 증설 완료"
		subtitle_text = "딜싸이클 업그레이드로 레일 부품을 한 조각 추가했습니다."
	elif mode.begins_with("upgrade"):
		title_text = "레일 강화술 · 적용할 칸 선택"
		subtitle_text = _factory_upgrade_description(pending_factory_upgrade)
	var title_accent := ColorRect.new()
	title_accent.position = Vector2(28.0, 18.0)
	title_accent.size = Vector2(4.0, 34.0)
	title_accent.color = GamePalette.YELLOW if mode == "edit" else GamePalette.ORANGE
	panel.add_child(title_accent)
	var title := _label(title_text, UI_TITLE_SIZE, GamePalette.TEXT)
	title.position = Vector2(44.0, 16.0)
	title.size = Vector2(800.0, 40.0)
	panel.add_child(title)
	var status := _label("칸 %d/10 · 공장 부품 %d/14 · 분열 %d회 남음" % [factory.slots.size(), factory.construction_count, maxi(0, FactoryDeck.MAX_SPLIT_UPGRADES - factory.split_upgrades_used)], UI_LABEL_SIZE, GamePalette.CYAN)
	status.position = Vector2(825.0, 16.0)
	status.size = Vector2(375.0, 22.0)
	status.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT
	panel.add_child(status)
	# 레일 끝 마감 패널은 3차 피드백⑬으로 제거했습니다. 총 RELOAD는 이 머리말 표기가 유일한 자리입니다.
	var reload_readout := _label("한 바퀴 RELOAD %.2f초" % factory.total_reload(), UI_HEADING_SIZE, GamePalette.ORANGE)
	reload_readout.position = Vector2(825.0, 36.0)
	reload_readout.size = Vector2(375.0, 24.0)
	reload_readout.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT
	panel.add_child(reload_readout)
	var subtitle := _label(subtitle_text, UI_BODY_SIZE, GamePalette.YELLOW if mode != "edit" else GamePalette.MUTED)
	subtitle.position = Vector2(44.0, 60.0)
	subtitle.size = Vector2(1156.0, 26.0)
	subtitle.autowrap_mode = TextServer.AUTOWRAP_WORD_SMART
	panel.add_child(subtitle)
	if mode != "place":
		var rail_guide := _label("회색 칸은 아직 건설되지 않은 자리 · 좌우 스크롤로 최대 10칸까지 미리 확인", UI_CAPTION_SIZE, GamePalette.MUTED.darkened(0.22))
		rail_guide.position = Vector2(28.0, 126.0)
		rail_guide.size = Vector2(1184.0, 24.0)
		rail_guide.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
		panel.add_child(rail_guide)

	# 레일 패널은 1280×720 화면의 세로·가로 정중앙에 놓입니다.
	var rail_panel := Panel.new()
	rail_panel.position = Vector2(28.0, FACTORY_RAIL_PANEL_TOP)
	rail_panel.size = Vector2(1184.0, FACTORY_RAIL_PANEL_H)
	rail_panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(UI_PANEL_BG, UI_EDGE_SOFT, 0, UI_BORDER_FRAME))
	panel.add_child(rail_panel)
	var rail_scroll := ScrollContainer.new()
	rail_scroll.position = Vector2(12.0, 12.0)
	rail_scroll.size = Vector2(1160.0, FACTORY_RAIL_PANEL_H - 24.0)
	rail_scroll.horizontal_scroll_mode = ScrollContainer.SCROLL_MODE_AUTO
	rail_scroll.vertical_scroll_mode = ScrollContainer.SCROLL_MODE_DISABLED
	rail_panel.add_child(rail_scroll)
	factory_rail_scroll = rail_scroll
	var rail := HBoxContainer.new()
	var content_width := float(FactoryDeck.MAX_SLOTS) * FACTORY_RAIL_CARD_W + float(FactoryDeck.MAX_SLOTS - 1) * FACTORY_RAIL_BRIDGE_W
	rail.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(maxf(content_width, rail_scroll.size.x), FACTORY_RAIL_ROW_H)
	rail.alignment = BoxContainer.ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	rail.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 0)
	rail_scroll.add_child(rail)
	for slot_index in FactoryDeck.MAX_SLOTS:
		if slot_index < factory.slots.size():
			_build_factory_rail_slot(rail, slot_index)
		else:
			_build_factory_rail_ghost_slot(rail, slot_index)
		if slot_index >= FactoryDeck.MAX_SLOTS - 1:
			continue
		var bridge_state := "ghost"
		if slot_index < factory.slots.size() - 1:
			bridge_state = "built"
		elif slot_index == factory.slots.size() - 1 and factory.trailing_bridge:
			bridge_state = "building"
		_build_factory_rail_bridge(rail, bridge_state)

	if mode == "edit":
		_build_factory_inventory(panel)
		var hint := _label("마우스로 드래그 교체 · TAB 레일/보관함 전환 · 방향키 이동 · SPACE 선택/배치", UI_LABEL_SIZE, GamePalette.CYAN)
		hint.position = Vector2(30.0, 657.0)
		hint.size = Vector2(900.0, 24.0)
		panel.add_child(hint)
		var close := _button("ESC · 공장 닫기", GamePalette.YELLOW, Vector2(214.0, 36.0))
		close.position = Vector2(992.0, 650.0)
		close.pressed.connect(_close_factory_menu)
		panel.add_child(close)
	elif mode == "place":
		var pending_panel := Panel.new()
		pending_panel.position = Vector2(28.0, 92.0)
		pending_panel.size = Vector2(1184.0, 74.0)
		var pending_color := _factory_card_color(acquired_card)
		pending_panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(UI_PANEL_BG, pending_color.darkened(0.32), 0, UI_BORDER_FRAME))
		panel.add_child(pending_panel)
		_build_pending_card_summary(pending_panel, acquired_card)
		var place_hint := _label("방향키로 칸 선택 · SPACE로 배치 · 교체된 카드는 보관함으로 이동", UI_HEADING_SIZE - 2, GamePalette.YELLOW)
		place_hint.position = Vector2(180.0, 560.0)
		place_hint.size = Vector2(870.0, 30.0)
		place_hint.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
		panel.add_child(place_hint)
		# 아이템 카드는 보관함 직행인데 스킬 카드만 레일에 강제 배치되던 규칙 차이를 없앱니다.
		var store := _button("ESC · 보관함에 넣기", GamePalette.CYAN, Vector2(214.0, 36.0))
		store.position = Vector2(992.0, 650.0)
		store.pressed.connect(_store_pending_factory_card)
		panel.add_child(store)
	elif mode == "build":
		var built_piece := String(acquired_card.get("piece", "bridge"))
		var built_name := "연결 다리" if built_piece == "bridge" else "새 딜싸이클 칸"
		var build_icon := GENERATED_UI_ICON_SCRIPT.new()
		build_icon.position = Vector2(566.0, 528.0)
		build_icon.size = Vector2(108.0, 108.0)
		build_icon.pivot_offset = build_icon.size * 0.5
		build_icon.scale = Vector2(0.18, 0.18)
		build_icon.modulate = Color(1.0, 0.66, 0.18, 0.0)
		build_icon.setup("bridge" if built_piece == "bridge" else "slot_active")
		panel.add_child(build_icon)
		var build_result := _label("%s 생성! 딜싸이클 끝에 설치 중…" % built_name, 20, GamePalette.ORANGE)
		build_result.position = Vector2(180.0, 640.0)
		build_result.size = Vector2(870.0, 40.0)
		build_result.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
		panel.add_child(build_result)
		var build_tween := build_icon.create_tween()
		build_tween.set_pause_mode(Tween.TWEEN_PAUSE_PROCESS)
		build_tween.set_trans(Tween.TRANS_BACK).set_ease(Tween.EASE_OUT)
		build_tween.parallel().tween_property(build_icon, "scale", Vector2.ONE, 0.52)
		build_tween.parallel().tween_property(build_icon, "modulate", Color.WHITE, 0.28)
		build_tween.tween_property(build_icon, "scale", Vector2(1.08, 1.08), 0.16)
		build_tween.tween_property(build_icon, "scale", Vector2.ONE, 0.16)
		call_deferred("_auto_close_factory_build")
	else:
		var upgrade_hint := _label("강화할 칸을 선택하세요 · 같은 칸을 두 번 분열하면 카드 3장이 동시에 실행됩니다.", UI_HEADING_SIZE, GamePalette.ORANGE)
		upgrade_hint.position = Vector2(145.0, 548.0)
		upgrade_hint.size = Vector2(940.0, 52.0)
		upgrade_hint.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
		panel.add_child(upgrade_hint)
		if mode.begins_with("upgrade"):
			# 적용 가능한 칸이 없을 때 갇히지 않도록 항상 환불 취소 경로를 둡니다.
			var cancel := _button("ESC · 강화 취소 (%d G 환불)" % factory_upgrade_refund, GamePalette.MUTED, Vector2(214.0, 36.0))
			cancel.position = Vector2(992.0, 650.0)
			cancel.pressed.connect(_cancel_factory_upgrade)
			panel.add_child(cancel)
	_animate_modal(panel, Vector2(0.0, 16.0))
	_update_factory_focus()

func _factory_rail_cell(width: float, spine_color: Color) -> Control:
	var cell := Control.new()
	cell.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(width, FACTORY_RAIL_ROW_H)
	cell.size = Vector2(width, FACTORY_RAIL_ROW_H)
	cell.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_PASS
	# 모든 셀을 가로지르는 레일 본선입니다. 카드 사이 틈으로 이어져 보이게 가장 먼저 깔립니다.
	var spine := ColorRect.new()
	spine.position = Vector2(0.0, FACTORY_RAIL_LINE_Y - 3.0)
	spine.size = Vector2(width, 6.0)
	spine.color = spine_color
	spine.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	cell.add_child(spine)
	return cell

func _factory_lane_card_height(lane_count: int) -> float:
	if lane_count <= 1:
		return 142.0
	var usable := FACTORY_RAIL_LANE_AREA - FACTORY_RAIL_LANE_GAP * float(lane_count - 1)
	return floorf(usable / float(lane_count))

func _build_factory_rail_slot(rail: HBoxContainer, slot_index: int, interactive: bool = true, deck: FactoryDeck = null) -> void:
	# interactive=false는 결과 모달·마왕 딜싸이클 미리보기처럼 읽기 전용으로 같은 레일을 그릴 때
	# 사용합니다. 이 경우 포커스 배열 등록·클릭·드래그를 모두 건너뛰어 공장 화면의 상태를 오염시키지
	# 않습니다. deck을 주면 플레이어 공장 대신 그 덱(예: boss_factory)을 그립니다.
	var source_deck: FactoryDeck = deck if deck != null else factory
	var slot: Dictionary = source_deck.slots[slot_index]
	var lanes: Array = slot.get("lanes", [])
	var cell := _factory_rail_cell(FACTORY_RAIL_CARD_W, FACTORY_RAIL_SPINE_BUILT)
	rail.add_child(cell)
	var upgrade_marks: Array[String] = []
	if int(slot.get("repeat", 1)) > 1: upgrade_marks.append("×2")
	if float(slot.get("duration_mul", 1.0)) < 0.99: upgrade_marks.append("속도")
	if float(slot.get("reload_mul", 1.0)) < 0.99: upgrade_marks.append("RELOAD")
	var header_row := HBoxContainer.new()
	header_row.position = Vector2(0.0, FACTORY_RAIL_HEADER_TOP)
	header_row.size = Vector2(FACTORY_RAIL_CARD_W, FACTORY_RAIL_HEADER_H)
	header_row.alignment = BoxContainer.ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	header_row.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	header_row.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 4)
	cell.add_child(header_row)
	var slot_icon := GENERATED_UI_ICON_SCRIPT.new()
	slot_icon.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(24.0, 24.0)
	slot_icon.setup("split" if lanes.size() > 1 else "slot_active")
	header_row.add_child(slot_icon)
	var slot_header := _label("칸 %02d  %s" % [slot_index + 1, " · ".join(upgrade_marks)], UI_LABEL_SIZE, GamePalette.YELLOW)
	slot_header.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(112.0, 24.0)
	slot_header.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	header_row.add_child(slot_header)
	var lane_height := _factory_lane_card_height(lanes.size())
	var stack_height := lane_height * float(lanes.size()) + FACTORY_RAIL_LANE_GAP * float(lanes.size() - 1)
	var lane_y := FACTORY_RAIL_LANE_TOP + (FACTORY_RAIL_LANE_AREA - stack_height) * 0.5
	for lane_index in lanes.size():
		var card: Dictionary = lanes[lane_index] if lanes[lane_index] is Dictionary else {}
		var button := _factory_lane_button(card, lane_index, lanes.size())
		button.position = Vector2(FACTORY_RAIL_CARD_INSET, lane_y + float(lane_index) * (lane_height + FACTORY_RAIL_LANE_GAP))
		if interactive:
			button.pressed.connect(_factory_lane_pressed.bind(slot_index, lane_index))
			if factory_mode == "edit":
				button.set("drag_payload", {"zone":"rail", "slot":slot_index, "lane":lane_index, "has_card":not card.is_empty(), "name":_factory_card_name(card)})
				button.connect("factory_card_dropped", _on_factory_card_dropped)
		else:
			button.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
			button.focus_mode = Control.FOCUS_NONE
		cell.add_child(button)
		if interactive:
			factory_lane_buttons.append(button)
			factory_lane_coordinates.append(Vector2i(slot_index, lane_index))

func _build_factory_rail_ghost_slot(rail: HBoxContainer, slot_index: int) -> void:
	# 미건설 자리표시자입니다. 포커스·드래그 대상이 아니며 클릭할 수 없습니다.
	var cell := _factory_rail_cell(FACTORY_RAIL_CARD_W, FACTORY_RAIL_SPINE_GHOST)
	rail.add_child(cell)
	var header := _label("칸 %02d" % [slot_index + 1], UI_LABEL_SIZE, Color("5a6472"))
	header.position = Vector2(0.0, FACTORY_RAIL_HEADER_TOP)
	header.size = Vector2(FACTORY_RAIL_CARD_W, FACTORY_RAIL_HEADER_H)
	header.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	cell.add_child(header)
	var ghost_height := _factory_lane_card_height(1)
	var ghost_top := FACTORY_RAIL_LANE_TOP + (FACTORY_RAIL_LANE_AREA - ghost_height) * 0.5
	var ghost_width := FACTORY_RAIL_CARD_W - FACTORY_RAIL_CARD_INSET * 2.0
	var ghost := Panel.new()
	ghost.position = Vector2(FACTORY_RAIL_CARD_INSET, ghost_top)
	ghost.size = Vector2(ghost_width, ghost_height)
	ghost.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	ghost.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(Color("0a101adc"), Color("303948"), 0, UI_BORDER_FRAME))
	cell.add_child(ghost)
	var ghost_icon := GENERATED_UI_ICON_SCRIPT.new()
	ghost_icon.position = Vector2((FACTORY_RAIL_CARD_W - 42.0) * 0.5, ghost_top + 20.0)
	ghost_icon.size = Vector2(42.0, 42.0)
	ghost_icon.modulate = Color(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.24)
	ghost_icon.setup("slot_empty")
	cell.add_child(ghost_icon)
	var note := _label("미건설", UI_BODY_SIZE, Color("6b7684"))
	note.position = Vector2(FACTORY_RAIL_CARD_INSET, ghost_top + 72.0)
	note.size = Vector2(ghost_width, 22.0)
	note.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	cell.add_child(note)
	var unlock := _label("공장 부품으로 개방", 10, Color("515b69"))
	unlock.position = Vector2(FACTORY_RAIL_CARD_INSET, ghost_top + 98.0)
	unlock.size = Vector2(ghost_width, 18.0)
	unlock.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	cell.add_child(unlock)

func _build_factory_rail_bridge(rail: HBoxContainer, bridge_state: String) -> void:
	var line_color := FACTORY_RAIL_SPINE_GHOST
	if bridge_state == "built":
		line_color = FACTORY_RAIL_SPINE_BUILT
	elif bridge_state == "building":
		line_color = GamePalette.ORANGE.darkened(0.2)
	var cell := _factory_rail_cell(FACTORY_RAIL_BRIDGE_W, line_color)
	rail.add_child(cell)
	# 3차 피드백⑫: 다리는 어두운 사각 패널 없이 스프라이트만 스파인 위에 얹습니다.
	# 프레임 제거는 generated_ui_icon.gd의 런타임 트리밍이 담당하고, 여기서는
	# 다리의 실제 가로:세로 비율에 맞는 사각형만 잡아 줍니다. 고스트는 딤 처리만 다릅니다.
	var icon := GENERATED_UI_ICON_SCRIPT.new()
	icon.position = Vector2((FACTORY_RAIL_BRIDGE_W - FACTORY_RAIL_BRIDGE_SPRITE.x) * 0.5, FACTORY_RAIL_LINE_Y - FACTORY_RAIL_BRIDGE_SPRITE.y * 0.5)
	icon.size = FACTORY_RAIL_BRIDGE_SPRITE
	icon.setup("bridge")
	if bridge_state == "built":
		icon.modulate = Color.WHITE
	elif bridge_state == "building":
		icon.modulate = GamePalette.ORANGE
	else:
		icon.modulate = Color(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.22)
	cell.add_child(icon)
	if bridge_state != "building":
		return
	var under_construction := _label("연결 다리", 10, GamePalette.ORANGE)
	under_construction.position = Vector2(-10.0, FACTORY_RAIL_LINE_Y + 26.0)
	under_construction.size = Vector2(FACTORY_RAIL_BRIDGE_W + 20.0, 18.0)
	under_construction.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	cell.add_child(under_construction)
	var pulse := icon.create_tween().set_loops()
	pulse.set_pause_mode(Tween.TWEEN_PAUSE_PROCESS)
	pulse.tween_property(icon, "modulate:a", 0.42, 0.45)
	pulse.tween_property(icon, "modulate:a", 1.0, 0.45)

func _build_factory_inventory(panel: Control) -> void:
	var inventory_panel := Panel.new()
	inventory_panel.position = Vector2(28.0, 517.0)
	inventory_panel.size = Vector2(1184.0, 130.0)
	inventory_panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(UI_PANEL_BG, UI_EDGE_SOFT, 0, UI_BORDER_FRAME))
	panel.add_child(inventory_panel)
	var heading_accent := ColorRect.new()
	heading_accent.position = Vector2(12.0, 8.0)
	heading_accent.size = Vector2(3.0, 16.0)
	heading_accent.color = GamePalette.CYAN
	inventory_panel.add_child(heading_accent)
	var heading := _label("카드 보관함 · %d장" % factory.inventory.size(), UI_BODY_SIZE, GamePalette.CYAN)
	heading.position = Vector2(23.0, 6.0)
	heading.size = Vector2(1100.0, 21.0)
	inventory_panel.add_child(heading)
	var scroll := ScrollContainer.new()
	scroll.position = Vector2(10.0, 30.0)
	scroll.size = Vector2(1164.0, 94.0)
	scroll.horizontal_scroll_mode = ScrollContainer.SCROLL_MODE_AUTO
	scroll.vertical_scroll_mode = ScrollContainer.SCROLL_MODE_DISABLED
	inventory_panel.add_child(scroll)
	var row := HBoxContainer.new()
	row.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 7)
	scroll.add_child(row)
	if factory.inventory.is_empty():
		var empty := _label("아직 보관한 카드가 없습니다. 레벨업·상점·보물상자에서 카드를 얻으세요.", 15, GamePalette.MUTED)
		empty.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(900.0, 76.0)
		empty.vertical_alignment = VERTICAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
		row.add_child(empty)
		return
	for index in factory.inventory.size():
		var card: Dictionary = factory.inventory[index]
		var button := _factory_card_button(card, Vector2(190.0, 92.0))
		button.pressed.connect(_factory_inventory_pressed.bind(index))
		button.set("drag_payload", {"zone":"inventory", "index":index, "has_card":true, "name":_factory_card_name(card)})
		button.connect("factory_card_dropped", _on_factory_card_dropped)
		row.add_child(button)
		factory_inventory_buttons.append(button)
		factory_inventory_indices.append(index)

func _factory_lane_button(card: Dictionary, lane_index: int, lane_count: int) -> Button:
	var prefix := "" if lane_count == 1 else String.chr(65 + lane_index)
	return _factory_card_button(card, Vector2(FACTORY_RAIL_CARD_W - FACTORY_RAIL_CARD_INSET * 2.0, _factory_lane_card_height(lane_count)), prefix)

func _factory_card_button(card: Dictionary, card_size: Vector2, lane_prefix: String = "") -> Button:
	var color := GamePalette.STONE_LIGHT if card.is_empty() else _factory_card_color(card)
	var button: Button = FACTORY_DRAG_BUTTON_SCRIPT.new()
	_style_button(button, "", color, card_size)
	button.tooltip_text = _factory_card_detail(card)
	_paint_card_block(button, card, card_size, lane_prefix)
	return button

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 표준 카드 블록 (2026-08-04 3차 피드백 ⑳)
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 사용자 요구 원문: "새로 얻은 카드와 기존 카드의 형태가 달라서 지속시간하고 RELOAD 비교가
# 힘들어. 같은 형태의 블록으로 만들어줘."
# 공장 레일 카드·보관함 카드·선택 모달 카드가 모두 이 함수 하나로 그려지므로
# 아이콘 위치, 이름·랭크 줄, 지속/R 칩의 크기·글꼴·색이 픽셀 단위로 같다.
# 선택 모달은 이 블록을 그대로 얹고 설명·보유 장수 같은 부가 정보만 바깥에 붙인다.
func _paint_card_block(host: Control, card: Dictionary, card_size: Vector2, lane_prefix: String = "") -> void:
	var color := GamePalette.STONE_LIGHT if card.is_empty() else _factory_card_color(card)
	var is_item := not card.is_empty() and String(card.get("kind", "skill")) == "item"
	var visual_card := card.duplicate(true)
	if visual_card.is_empty():
		visual_card = DealCardLibrary.basic_instance()
	var icon_id := String(visual_card.get("id", "basic"))
	# 카드 높이에 따라 세 단계 밀도로 배치합니다. 어느 단계에서도 글자가 겹치지 않습니다.
	var tight := card_size.y < 90.0
	var wide := card_size.y >= 132.0
	var icon_size := 38.0 if tight else (52.0 if wide else 48.0)
	var text_left := icon_size + 14.0
	var text_width := card_size.x - text_left - 8.0
	var name_height := 20.0 if tight else (44.0 if wide else 38.0)
	var meter_height := 14.0 if tight else 18.0
	var meter_y := card_size.y - meter_height - 6.0
	var icon := SKILL_ICON_SCRIPT.new()
	icon.position = Vector2(8.0, 8.0)
	icon.size = Vector2(icon_size, icon_size)
	icon.setup(icon_id, color)
	host.add_child(icon)

	var name_text := _factory_card_name(card)
	if not lane_prefix.is_empty():
		name_text = "%s · %s" % [lane_prefix, name_text]
	var name_label := _label(name_text, 11 if tight else (14 if wide else 13), GamePalette.TEXT)
	name_label.position = Vector2(text_left, 6.0)
	name_label.size = Vector2(text_width, name_height)
	if tight:
		name_label.text_overrun_behavior = TextServer.OVERRUN_TRIM_ELLIPSIS
	else:
		name_label.autowrap_mode = TextServer.AUTOWRAP_WORD_SMART
	host.add_child(name_label)

	if is_item:
		var item := ItemLibrary.by_id(String(card.get("id", "")))
		var item_tag := _label("아이템 · 공격 없음", 9 if tight else UI_CAPTION_SIZE, color.lightened(0.18))
		item_tag.position = Vector2(text_left, 6.0 + name_height)
		item_tag.size = Vector2(text_width, 18.0)
		host.add_child(item_tag)
		if not tight:
			var scope := _label(ItemLibrary.effect_scope(item), 10, GamePalette.CYAN)
			scope.position = Vector2(8.0, meter_y - 19.0)
			scope.size = Vector2(card_size.x - 16.0, 17.0)
			scope.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
			host.add_child(scope)
		var item_effect := _label(ItemLibrary.compact_effect(item), 9 if tight else 10, GamePalette.MUTED)
		item_effect.position = Vector2(8.0, meter_y)
		item_effect.size = Vector2(card_size.x - 16.0, meter_height + 2.0)
		item_effect.text_overrun_behavior = TextServer.OVERRUN_TRIM_ELLIPSIS
		item_effect.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
		host.add_child(item_effect)
	else:
		var ranked := DealCardLibrary.ranked(visual_card)
		var rank_text := "빈칸 · 기본 공격" if card.is_empty() else "스킬 · R%d" % int(card.get("rank", 1))
		var rank_label := _label(rank_text, 9 if tight else UI_CAPTION_SIZE, color.lightened(0.18))
		rank_label.position = Vector2(text_left, 6.0 + name_height)
		rank_label.size = Vector2(text_width, 18.0)
		host.add_child(rank_label)
		var half_width := (card_size.x - 22.0) * 0.5
		var meter_font := 8 if tight else 10
		_add_metric_bar(host, Vector2(8.0, meter_y), Vector2(half_width, meter_height), "지속 %.2f" % float(ranked.get("duration", 0.0)), float(ranked.get("duration", 0.0)), 2.8, GamePalette.CYAN, meter_font)
