		_add_metric_bar(host, Vector2(14.0 + half_width, meter_y), Vector2(half_width, meter_height), "R %.2f" % float(ranked.get("reload", 0.0)), float(ranked.get("reload", 0.0)), 1.8, GamePalette.ORANGE, meter_font)

func _card_block_panel(card: Dictionary) -> Panel:
	# 클릭·드래그가 필요 없는 화면(선택 모달 등)에서 쓰는 읽기 전용 카드 블록입니다.
	# 스타일박스는 _style_button의 normal과 같은 공식을 써서 레일 카드와 겉모습이 같습니다.
	var color := GamePalette.STONE_LIGHT if card.is_empty() else _factory_card_color(card)
	var block := Panel.new()
	block.size = FACTORY_CARD_BLOCK_SIZE
	block.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	block.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(color.darkened(0.76), color.darkened(0.14), 0, UI_BORDER_CARD))
	_paint_card_block(block, card, FACTORY_CARD_BLOCK_SIZE)
	return block

func _on_factory_card_dropped(source: Dictionary, target: Dictionary) -> void:
	if state != "factory_menu" or factory_mode != "edit" or factory == null or source == target:
		return
	var source_zone := String(source.get("zone", ""))
	var target_zone := String(target.get("zone", ""))
	if source_zone == "rail" and target_zone == "rail":
		var source_slot := int(source.get("slot", -1))
		var source_lane := int(source.get("lane", -1))
		var target_slot := int(target.get("slot", -1))
		var target_lane := int(target.get("lane", -1))
		var first := factory.get_card(source_slot, source_lane)
		var second := factory.get_card(target_slot, target_lane)
		factory.place_card(source_slot, source_lane, second)
		factory.place_card(target_slot, target_lane, first)
	elif source_zone == "inventory" and target_zone == "rail":
		var inventory_index := int(source.get("index", -1))
		var moving := factory.remove_inventory_at(inventory_index)
		if moving.is_empty():
			return
		var replaced := factory.place_card(int(target.get("slot", -1)), int(target.get("lane", -1)), moving)
		factory.add_inventory(replaced)
	elif source_zone == "rail" and target_zone == "inventory":
		var removed := factory.clear_lane(int(source.get("slot", -1)), int(source.get("lane", -1)))
		factory.add_inventory(removed)
	elif source_zone == "inventory" and target_zone == "inventory":
		var from_index := int(source.get("index", -1))
		var to_index := int(target.get("index", -1))
		if from_index < 0 or to_index < 0 or from_index >= factory.inventory.size() or to_index >= factory.inventory.size():
			return
		var held: Dictionary = factory.inventory[from_index]
		factory.inventory[from_index] = factory.inventory[to_index]
		factory.inventory[to_index] = held
	else:
		return
	_reset_player_cycle()
	play_sound("choice", -5.0)
	_show_factory_menu("edit", {}, factory_return_state)

func _build_pending_card_summary(parent: Control, card: Dictionary) -> void:
	# 레일이 화면 중앙을 차지하므로 입고 카드 요약은 제목 아래 가로 스트립으로 놓습니다.
	var color := _factory_card_color(card)
	var icon := SKILL_ICON_SCRIPT.new()
	icon.position = Vector2(14.0, 8.0)
	icon.size = Vector2(58.0, 58.0)
	icon.setup(String(card.get("id", "basic")), color)
	parent.add_child(icon)
	var is_item := String(card.get("kind", "skill")) == "item"
	if is_item:
		var item := ItemLibrary.by_id(String(card.get("id", "")))
		var item_type := _label("아이템 카드 · %s · %s" % [ItemLibrary.rarity_name(String(item.get("rarity", "common"))), ItemLibrary.part_name(String(item.get("slot", "")))], UI_CAPTION_SIZE, color)
		item_type.position = Vector2(84.0, 9.0)
		item_type.size = Vector2(420.0, 18.0)
		parent.add_child(item_type)
		var item_name := _label(String(item.get("name", "아이템")), 20, GamePalette.TEXT)
		item_name.position = Vector2(84.0, 28.0)
		item_name.size = Vector2(420.0, 32.0)
		item_name.text_overrun_behavior = TextServer.OVERRUN_TRIM_ELLIPSIS
		parent.add_child(item_name)
		var attack_notice := _label("공격 없음 · 놓인 칸은 즉시 통과", UI_CAPTION_SIZE, GamePalette.YELLOW)
		attack_notice.position = Vector2(520.0, 9.0)
		attack_notice.size = Vector2(340.0, 18.0)
		parent.add_child(attack_notice)
		var effect := _label("적용 범위 %s · %s" % [ItemLibrary.effect_scope(item), item.get("desc", "")], UI_CAPTION_SIZE, GamePalette.CYAN)
		effect.position = Vector2(520.0, 28.0)
		effect.size = Vector2(640.0, 38.0)
		effect.autowrap_mode = TextServer.AUTOWRAP_WORD_SMART
		parent.add_child(effect)
	else:
		var ranked := DealCardLibrary.ranked(card)
		var skill_type := _label("스킬 카드 · %s · R%d" % [DealCardLibrary.combat_tags(ranked), int(card.get("rank", 1))], UI_CAPTION_SIZE, color)
		skill_type.position = Vector2(84.0, 9.0)
		skill_type.size = Vector2(420.0, 18.0)
		skill_type.text_overrun_behavior = TextServer.OVERRUN_TRIM_ELLIPSIS
		parent.add_child(skill_type)
		var skill_name := _label(String(ranked.get("name", "기술")), 20, GamePalette.TEXT)
		skill_name.position = Vector2(84.0, 28.0)
		skill_name.size = Vector2(420.0, 32.0)
		skill_name.text_overrun_behavior = TextServer.OVERRUN_TRIM_ELLIPSIS
		parent.add_child(skill_name)
		var description := _label(String(ranked.get("desc", "")), UI_CAPTION_SIZE, GamePalette.MUTED)
		description.position = Vector2(516.0, 8.0)
		description.size = Vector2(346.0, 38.0)
		description.autowrap_mode = TextServer.AUTOWRAP_WORD_SMART
		parent.add_child(description)
		var damage := _label("피해계수 %.2f" % float(ranked.get("damage", 0.0)), UI_CAPTION_SIZE, GamePalette.YELLOW)
		damage.position = Vector2(516.0, 46.0)
		damage.size = Vector2(346.0, 20.0)
		parent.add_child(damage)
		_add_metric_bar(parent, Vector2(880.0, 11.0), Vector2(280.0, 22.0), "지속시간  %.2f초" % float(ranked.get("duration", 0.0)), float(ranked.get("duration", 0.0)), 2.8, GamePalette.CYAN, UI_CAPTION_SIZE)
		_add_metric_bar(parent, Vector2(880.0, 41.0), Vector2(280.0, 22.0), "RELOAD  %.2f초" % float(ranked.get("reload", 0.0)), float(ranked.get("reload", 0.0)), 1.8, GamePalette.ORANGE, UI_CAPTION_SIZE)

func _add_metric_bar(parent: Control, bar_position: Vector2, bar_size: Vector2, title: String, value: float, visual_max: float, color: Color, font_size: int = 10) -> void:
	var track := ColorRect.new()
	track.position = bar_position
	track.size = bar_size
	track.color = Color(UI_CHIP_BG, 0.88)
	track.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	parent.add_child(track)
	var fill := ColorRect.new()
	fill.position = Vector2(2.0, 2.0)
	fill.size = Vector2((bar_size.x - 4.0) * clampf(value / maxf(visual_max, 0.01), 0.0, 1.0), maxf(1.0, bar_size.y - 4.0))
	# 채움 밴드 위에 아이콘·숫자를 얹으므로 한 단계 어둡고 옅게 깔아 글자가 먼저 읽히게 합니다 (⑰).
	fill.color = Color(color.darkened(0.32), 0.62)
	fill.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	track.add_child(fill)
	# 아이콘은 프레임을 벗긴 뒤 글리프가 칸을 꽉 채우므로 예전보다 작게 잡아야
	# 글자와 겹치지 않습니다 (⑫ 트리밍 + ⑰ 간격 리듬).
	var metric_icon := GENERATED_UI_ICON_SCRIPT.new()
	var metric_size := minf(bar_size.y - 4.0, 14.0)
	metric_icon.position = Vector2(3.0, (bar_size.y - metric_size) * 0.5)
	metric_icon.size = Vector2(metric_size, metric_size)
	metric_icon.setup("reload" if title.to_upper().contains("RELOAD") or title.begins_with("R ") else "duration")
	track.add_child(metric_icon)
	# 가운데 정렬이면 짧은 칩에서 글자 시작점이 아이콘 쪽으로 밀려 "지" 글자가 아이콘에
	# 파묻혔습니다. 아이콘 오른쪽에서 왼쪽 정렬로 시작해 항상 2px 이상 띄웁니다 (P1-14).
	var text_label := _label(title, font_size, GamePalette.TEXT)
	text_label.position = Vector2(metric_size + 9.0, -2.0 if bar_size.y <= 15.0 else 0.0)
	text_label.size = Vector2(maxf(1.0, bar_size.x - metric_size - 12.0), bar_size.y)
	text_label.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT
	text_label.vertical_alignment = VERTICAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	track.add_child(text_label)

func _factory_card_name(card: Dictionary) -> String:
	if card.is_empty():
		return "기본 베기"
	if String(card.get("kind", "skill")) == "item":
		return String(ItemLibrary.by_id(String(card.get("id", ""))).get("name", "아이템"))
	return String(DealCardLibrary.by_id(String(card.get("id", ""))).get("name", "기술"))

func _factory_card_color(card: Dictionary) -> Color:
	if card.is_empty():
		return GamePalette.STONE_LIGHT
	if String(card.get("kind", "skill")) == "item":
		var item := ItemLibrary.by_id(String(card.get("id", "")))
		return ItemLibrary.rarity_color(String(item.get("rarity", "common")))
	var definition := DealCardLibrary.by_id(String(card.get("id", "")))
	return Color(String(definition.get("color", "f4d35e")))

func _factory_card_detail(card: Dictionary) -> String:
	if String(card.get("kind", "skill")) == "item":
		var item := ItemLibrary.by_id(String(card.get("id", "")))
		return "%s · %s · 아이템 카드\n공격 없음 · %s\n%s" % [ItemLibrary.rarity_name(String(item.get("rarity", "common"))), _item_slot_korean(String(item.get("slot", ""))), ItemLibrary.effect_scope(item), item.get("desc", "")]
	var definition := DealCardLibrary.ranked(card)
	return "%s  R%d\n%s\n지속시간 %.2f초 · RELOAD %.2f초 · 피해계수 %.2f" % [definition.get("name", "기술"), int(card.get("rank", 1)), definition.get("desc", ""), float(definition.get("duration", 0.0)), float(definition.get("reload", 0.0)), float(definition.get("damage", 0.0))]

func _decorate_shop_offer(button: Button, card: Dictionary, offer: Dictionary) -> void:
	var color := _factory_card_color(card)
	var icon := SKILL_ICON_SCRIPT.new()
	icon.position = Vector2(86.0, 18.0)
	icon.size = Vector2(80.0, 80.0)
	icon.setup(String(card.get("id", "basic")), color)
	button.add_child(icon)
	var is_item := String(card.get("kind", "skill")) == "item"
	var type_text := "아이템 카드 · 공격 없음" if is_item else "스킬 카드 · R%d" % int(card.get("rank", 1))
	var type_label := _label(type_text, UI_CAPTION_SIZE, color.lightened(0.18))
	type_label.position = Vector2(12.0, 106.0)
	type_label.size = Vector2(228.0, 20.0)
	type_label.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	button.add_child(type_label)
	var name_label := _label(String(offer.get("name", _factory_card_name(card))), UI_HEADING_SIZE, GamePalette.TEXT)
	name_label.position = Vector2(14.0, 130.0)
	name_label.size = Vector2(224.0, 48.0)
	name_label.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	name_label.vertical_alignment = VERTICAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	name_label.autowrap_mode = TextServer.AUTOWRAP_WORD_SMART
	button.add_child(name_label)
	var description := _label(String(offer.get("desc", "")), UI_LABEL_SIZE, GamePalette.MUTED)
	description.position = Vector2(15.0, 180.0)
	description.size = Vector2(222.0, 66.0)
	description.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	description.vertical_alignment = VERTICAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	description.autowrap_mode = TextServer.AUTOWRAP_WORD_SMART
	button.add_child(description)
	if is_item:
		var item := ItemLibrary.by_id(String(card.get("id", "")))
		var scope := _label("적용  %s" % ItemLibrary.effect_scope(item), UI_CAPTION_SIZE, GamePalette.CYAN)
		scope.position = Vector2(14.0, 256.0)
		scope.size = Vector2(224.0, 42.0)
		scope.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
		scope.autowrap_mode = TextServer.AUTOWRAP_WORD_SMART
		button.add_child(scope)
	else:
		var ranked := DealCardLibrary.ranked(card)
		_add_metric_bar(button, Vector2(16.0, 252.0), Vector2(220.0, 21.0), "지속시간  %.2f초" % float(ranked.get("duration", 0.0)), float(ranked.get("duration", 0.0)), 2.8, GamePalette.CYAN, 10)
		_add_metric_bar(button, Vector2(16.0, 280.0), Vector2(220.0, 21.0), "RELOAD  %.2f초" % float(ranked.get("reload", 0.0)), float(ranked.get("reload", 0.0)), 1.8, GamePalette.ORANGE, 10)
	var price := _label("%d G" % int(offer.get("price", 0)), UI_HEADING_SIZE, GamePalette.YELLOW)
	price.position = Vector2(14.0, 319.0)
	price.size = Vector2(224.0, 28.0)
	price.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	button.add_child(price)

func _item_slot_korean(slot_id: String) -> String:
	return ItemLibrary.part_name(slot_id)

func _factory_inventory_pressed(inventory_index: int) -> void:
	if state != "factory_menu" or inventory_index < 0 or inventory_index >= factory.inventory.size():
		return
	factory_selected_inventory = inventory_index
	factory_focus_zone = "inventory"
	factory_focus_index = factory_inventory_indices.find(inventory_index)
	_update_factory_focus()

func _factory_lane_pressed(slot_index: int, lane_index: int) -> void:
	if factory == null:
		return
	if factory_mode == "place":
		var previous := factory.place_card(slot_index, lane_index, pending_factory_card)
		factory.add_inventory(previous)
		var placed_name := _factory_card_name(pending_factory_card)
		pending_factory_card.clear()
		_reset_player_cycle()
		_finish_factory_return()
		_show_banner("%s 배치 완료 · 딜싸이클 재가동" % placed_name, GamePalette.GREEN, 2.4)
		_check_first_advancement()
		return
	if factory_mode.begins_with("upgrade"):
		var success := false
		if pending_factory_upgrade == "split":
			success = factory.split_slot(slot_index)
		else:
			success = factory.upgrade_slot(slot_index, pending_factory_upgrade)
		if success:
			_reset_player_cycle()
			_finish_factory_return()
			_show_banner("%d번 칸 강화 완료 · %s" % [slot_index + 1, _factory_upgrade_description(pending_factory_upgrade)], GamePalette.ORANGE, 2.8)
		else:
			_show_banner("이 칸에는 더 적용할 수 없습니다", GamePalette.RED, 1.8)
		return
	if factory_mode != "edit":
		return
	if factory_selected_inventory >= 0 and factory_selected_inventory < factory.inventory.size():
		var selected := factory.remove_inventory_at(factory_selected_inventory)
		var old := factory.place_card(slot_index, lane_index, selected)
		factory.add_inventory(old)
		factory_selected_inventory = -1
	else:
		var removed := factory.clear_lane(slot_index, lane_index)
		if removed.is_empty():
			return
		factory.add_inventory(removed)
	_reset_player_cycle()
	_show_factory_menu("edit", {}, factory_return_state)

func _handle_factory_keyboard(key_event: InputEventKey) -> void:
	if key_event.keycode == KEY_ESCAPE:
		# 예전에는 edit 모드에만 ESC 탈출구가 있었습니다. upgrade 모드에서 적용 가능한 칸이
		# 하나도 없으면 화면을 빠져나갈 방법이 전혀 없어 강제 종료뿐이었습니다.
		if factory_mode == "edit":
			_close_factory_menu()
		elif factory_mode.begins_with("upgrade"):
			_cancel_factory_upgrade()
		elif factory_mode == "place":
			_store_pending_factory_card()
		return
	if key_event.keycode == KEY_TAB and factory_mode == "edit" and not factory_inventory_buttons.is_empty():
		factory_focus_zone = "inventory" if factory_focus_zone == "rail" else "rail"
		factory_focus_index = 0
		_update_factory_focus()
		return
	var direction := 0
	if key_event.keycode in [KEY_LEFT, KEY_UP, KEY_A, KEY_W]: direction = -1
	if key_event.keycode in [KEY_RIGHT, KEY_DOWN, KEY_D, KEY_S]: direction = 1
	if direction != 0:
		var size := factory_lane_buttons.size() if factory_focus_zone == "rail" else factory_inventory_buttons.size()
		if size > 0:
			factory_focus_index = wrapi(factory_focus_index + direction, 0, size)
			_update_factory_focus()
		return
	if key_event.keycode not in [KEY_SPACE, KEY_ENTER]:
		return
	if factory_focus_zone == "inventory" and not factory_inventory_indices.is_empty():
		_factory_inventory_pressed(factory_inventory_indices[clampi(factory_focus_index, 0, factory_inventory_indices.size() - 1)])
	elif not factory_lane_coordinates.is_empty():
		var coordinate := factory_lane_coordinates[clampi(factory_focus_index, 0, factory_lane_coordinates.size() - 1)]
		_factory_lane_pressed(coordinate.x, coordinate.y)

func _update_factory_focus() -> void:
	for index in factory_lane_buttons.size():
		factory_lane_buttons[index].modulate = Color.WHITE if factory_focus_zone == "rail" and index == factory_focus_index else Color(0.72, 0.74, 0.8, 0.92)
	for index in factory_inventory_buttons.size():
		var selected := factory_selected_inventory == factory_inventory_indices[index]
		factory_inventory_buttons[index].modulate = Color.WHITE if selected or (factory_focus_zone == "inventory" and index == factory_focus_index) else Color(0.68, 0.7, 0.78, 0.9)
	# 레일이 10칸으로 늘어나 화면보다 길기 때문에 포커스가 항상 보이게 스크롤을 따라가게 합니다.
	if factory_focus_zone != "rail" or not is_instance_valid(factory_rail_scroll):
		return
	if factory_focus_index < 0 or factory_focus_index >= factory_lane_buttons.size():
		return
	factory_rail_scroll.ensure_control_visible(factory_lane_buttons[factory_focus_index])

func _factory_upgrade_description(upgrade_type: String) -> String:
	match upgrade_type:
		"split": return "선택한 칸에 동시 실행 레일 +1"
		"repeat": return "선택한 칸의 내용을 2번 실행"
		"duration": return "선택한 칸의 지속시간 14% 단축"
		"reload": return "선택한 칸의 RELOAD 18% 단축"
		_: return "레일 부품 강화"

func _factory_shape_text() -> String:
	# 예전에는 "칸-줄-칸-줄-칸" 같은 내부 표현이 그대로 버튼에 노출됐습니다.
	if factory == null:
		return "공장 없음"
	return "현재 %d칸 · 다음 다리 %s" % [factory.slots.size(), "건설 중" if factory.trailing_bridge else "없음"]

func _auto_close_factory_build() -> void:
	await get_tree().create_timer(1.8, true, false, true).timeout
	if state == "factory_build":
		_finish_factory_return()

func _close_factory_menu() -> void:
	if state != "factory_menu":
		return
	_finish_factory_return()

func _cancel_factory_upgrade() -> void:
	# 강화술사에게 이미 지불한 골드를 되돌려 주고 성 안으로 복귀합니다.
	if state != "factory_upgrade":
		return
	var refund := factory_upgrade_refund
	pending_factory_upgrade = ""
	if refund > 0:
		gold += refund
	_finish_factory_return()
	if refund > 0:
		_show_banner("강화를 취소했습니다 · %d G 환불" % refund, GamePalette.YELLOW, 2.4)
	else:
		_show_banner("강화를 취소했습니다", GamePalette.MUTED, 2.0)

func _store_pending_factory_card() -> void:
	# 레일에 놓지 않고 보관함으로 보냅니다. 아이템 카드 획득 흐름과 규칙이 같아집니다.
	if state != "factory_place" or factory == null:
		return
	var stored_name := _factory_card_name(pending_factory_card)
	factory.add_inventory(pending_factory_card)
	pending_factory_card.clear()
	_finish_factory_return()
	_show_banner("%s → 카드 보관함에 보관 · ESC 공장에서 배치" % stored_name, GamePalette.CYAN, 2.6)
	_check_first_advancement()

func _finish_factory_return() -> void:
	_clear_overlay()
	get_tree().paused = false
	state = factory_return_state
	factory_upgrade_refund = 0
	# 스킬 확정 → 즉시 배치 → 필드 복귀(factory_place), ESC 공장(factory_menu),
	# 슬롯 강화(factory_upgrade), 다리/슬롯 건설 연출(factory_build) 모두 이 경로로 나온다.
	_grant_modal_return_invulnerability()
	_update_hud()
	if not pending_boss_toast_cards.is_empty():
		var cards := pending_boss_toast_cards.duplicate(true)
		pending_boss_toast_cards.clear()
		_show_boss_growth_toast(cards)
	if state == "playing" and experience >= xp_target:
		call_deferred("_show_skill_choice", "level")

func _reset_player_cycle() -> void:
	if is_instance_valid(player_cycle):
		player_cycle.reset_cycle()

func _add_lobby_background(parent: Control) -> void:
	var art := TextureRect.new()
	art.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	art.texture = LOBBY_BACKGROUND
	art.expand_mode = TextureRect.EXPAND_IGNORE_SIZE
	art.stretch_mode = TextureRect.STRETCH_KEEP_ASPECT_COVERED
	art.texture_filter = CanvasItem.TEXTURE_FILTER_NEAREST
	art.modulate = Color(0.84, 0.88, 0.96, 0.96)
	art.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	parent.add_child(art)
	var shade := ColorRect.new()
	shade.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	shade.color = Color("07101b70")
	shade.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	parent.add_child(shade)

func _show_menu() -> void:
	get_tree().paused = false
	state = "menu"
	if is_instance_valid(gameplay_root):
		gameplay_root.free()
	gameplay_root = null
	world = null
	player = null
	boss = null
	hud.visible = false
	_load_progress()
	_clear_overlay()
	overlay = Control.new()
	overlay.name = "LobbyScene"
	overlay.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	overlay.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	ui_root.add_child(overlay)
	_add_lobby_background(overlay)
	var title := _label("딜싸이클 용사", 60, GamePalette.TEXT)
	title.position = Vector2(108.0, 92.0)
	title.size = Vector2(560.0, 82.0)
	title.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	title.add_theme_constant_override("outline_size", 9)
	title.add_theme_color_override("font_outline_color", Color("22152e"))
	overlay.add_child(title)
	var menu_panel := Panel.new()
	menu_panel.position = Vector2(190.0, 222.0)
	menu_panel.size = Vector2(400.0, 400.0)
	menu_panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(Color("0b1220dc"), GamePalette.STONE_LIGHT.darkened(0.2), 0, 2))
	overlay.add_child(menu_panel)
	var new_game := _button("게임 시작", GamePalette.YELLOW, Vector2(320.0, 62.0))
	new_game.position = Vector2(40.0, 42.0)
	new_game.pressed.connect(_show_character_select)
	menu_panel.add_child(new_game)
	var continue_text := "이어하기 · 플레이타임 %s" % _format_time(saved_run_playtime) if saved_run_available else "이어하기 · 저장된 모험 없음"
	var continue_button := _button(continue_text, GamePalette.CYAN, Vector2(320.0, 62.0))
	continue_button.position = Vector2(40.0, 126.0)
	continue_button.disabled = not saved_run_available
	continue_button.pressed.connect(_continue_saved_run)
	menu_panel.add_child(continue_button)
	var settings := _button("설정", GamePalette.MUTED, Vector2(320.0, 62.0))
	settings.position = Vector2(40.0, 210.0)
	settings.pressed.connect(_show_settings)
	menu_panel.add_child(settings)
	# 전체 화면에서는 창 닫기 버튼이 없고 ESC는 공장을 여는 키라, 로비에 종료 수단이
	# 없으면 Cmd+Q 말고는 게임을 끌 방법이 없습니다 (P1-10).
	var quit_button := _button("게임 종료", GamePalette.MUTED.darkened(0.2), Vector2(320.0, 62.0))
	quit_button.position = Vector2(40.0, 294.0)
	quit_button.pressed.connect(get_tree().quit)
	menu_panel.add_child(quit_button)
	_animate_modal(menu_panel, Vector2(-20.0, 0.0))
	new_game.grab_focus()

func _show_character_select() -> void:
	state = "character_select"
	hud.visible = false
	_clear_overlay()
	overlay = Control.new()
	overlay.name = "CharacterSelect"
	overlay.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	overlay.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	ui_root.add_child(overlay)
	_add_lobby_background(overlay)
	var ids := ["swordsman", "archer", "mage"]
	lobby_character_index = wrapi(lobby_character_index, 0, ids.size())
	var character_id := String(ids[lobby_character_index])
	var available := character_id == "swordsman"
	var names := {"swordsman":"왕국 검사", "archer":"방랑 궁사", "mage":"별빛 마법사"}
	var colors := {"swordsman":GamePalette.YELLOW, "archer":GamePalette.GREEN, "mage":GamePalette.MAGENTA}
	var heading := _label("캐릭터 선택", UI_TITLE_SIZE, GamePalette.TEXT)
	heading.position = Vector2(0.0, 24.0)
	heading.size = Vector2(1280.0, 46.0)
	heading.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	overlay.add_child(heading)
	var card := Panel.new()
	card.position = Vector2(430.0, 78.0)
	card.size = Vector2(420.0, 512.0)
	card.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(UI_MODAL_BG, UI_EDGE, 0, UI_BORDER_MODAL))
	overlay.add_child(card)
	var portrait_back := ColorRect.new()
	portrait_back.position = Vector2(18.0, 18.0)
	portrait_back.size = Vector2(384.0, 408.0)
	portrait_back.color = Color("0b1930")
	portrait_back.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	card.add_child(portrait_back)
	var portrait := TextureRect.new()
	portrait.position = Vector2(18.0, 18.0)
	portrait.size = Vector2(384.0, 408.0)
	portrait.texture = CHARACTER_CARD_TEXTURES[character_id]
	portrait.expand_mode = TextureRect.EXPAND_IGNORE_SIZE
	# 원화를 억지로 꽉 채우지 않고 전체를 맞춰 전사의 얼굴·검·발이 모두 보입니다.
	portrait.stretch_mode = TextureRect.STRETCH_KEEP_ASPECT_CENTERED
	portrait.texture_filter = CanvasItem.TEXTURE_FILTER_NEAREST
	portrait.modulate = Color.WHITE if available else Color(0.16, 0.18, 0.24, 0.78)
	portrait.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	card.add_child(portrait)
	if not available:
		var locked := ColorRect.new()
		locked.position = portrait.position
		locked.size = portrait.size
		locked.color = Color("070a13a8")
		locked.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
		card.add_child(locked)
		var soon := _label("다음 업데이트에서 개방", UI_TITLE_SIZE, colors[character_id])
		soon.position = Vector2(18.0, 190.0)
		soon.size = Vector2(384.0, 55.0)
		soon.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
		soon.vertical_alignment = VERTICAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
		card.add_child(soon)
	# 카드 성격은 3px 캐릭터색 테두리 대신 이름 왼쪽의 강조 바로만 표현합니다 (P1-1).
	var name_accent := ColorRect.new()
	name_accent.position = Vector2(20.0, 438.0)
	name_accent.size = Vector2(4.0, 32.0)
	name_accent.color = colors[character_id]
	card.add_child(name_accent)
	var name_label := _label(String(names[character_id]), UI_TITLE_SIZE, colors[character_id])
	name_label.position = Vector2(36.0, 436.0)
	name_label.size = Vector2(364.0, 36.0)
	name_label.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	card.add_child(name_label)
	var availability := _label("플레이 가능" if available else "다음 업데이트에서 개방", UI_BODY_SIZE, GamePalette.CYAN if available else GamePalette.MUTED)
	availability.position = Vector2(20.0, 476.0)
	availability.size = Vector2(380.0, 24.0)
	availability.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	card.add_child(availability)
	var left := _button("◀", GamePalette.CYAN, Vector2(82.0, 82.0))
	left.position = Vector2(315.0, 300.0)
	left.pressed.connect(_cycle_character.bind(-1))
	overlay.add_child(left)
	var right := _button("▶", GamePalette.CYAN, Vector2(82.0, 82.0))
	right.position = Vector2(883.0, 300.0)
	right.pressed.connect(_cycle_character.bind(1))
	overlay.add_child(right)
	var start := _button("이 캐릭터로 시작", colors[character_id], Vector2(310.0, 54.0))
	start.position = Vector2(485.0, 626.0)
	start.disabled = not available
	start.pressed.connect(_confirm_character_selection.bind(character_id))
	overlay.add_child(start)
	var back := _button("로비로", GamePalette.MUTED, Vector2(150.0, 40.0))
	back.position = Vector2(24.0, 656.0)
	back.pressed.connect(_show_menu)
	overlay.add_child(back)
	_animate_modal(card, Vector2(0.0, 22.0))

func _cycle_character(direction: int) -> void:
	lobby_character_index = wrapi(lobby_character_index + direction, 0, 3)
	_show_character_select()

func _confirm_character_selection(character_id: String) -> void:
	if character_id != "swordsman":
		return
	selected_character_id = character_id
	_start_game()

func _start_game() -> void:
	var args := OS.get_cmdline_user_args()
	var should_skip := automated_test or not args.is_empty() or onboarding_seen_session or _onboarding_hidden_today()
	if should_skip:
		_begin_run()
	else:
		onboarding_page = 0
		onboarding_skip_today = false
		_show_onboarding(0)

func _show_settings() -> void:
	state = "settings"
	_clear_overlay()
	overlay = Control.new()
	overlay.name = "Settings"
	overlay.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	overlay.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	ui_root.add_child(overlay)
	_add_lobby_background(overlay)
	var panel := Panel.new()
	panel.position = Vector2(330.0, 82.0)
	panel.size = Vector2(620.0, 550.0)
	panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(UI_MODAL_BG, UI_EDGE, 0, UI_BORDER_MODAL))
	overlay.add_child(panel)
	var accent := ColorRect.new()
	accent.position = Vector2(28.0, 26.0)
	accent.size = Vector2(4.0, 34.0)
	accent.color = GamePalette.CYAN
	panel.add_child(accent)
	var title := _label("설정", UI_TITLE_SIZE, GamePalette.TEXT)
	title.position = Vector2(44.0, 24.0)
	title.size = Vector2(400.0, 40.0)
	panel.add_child(title)
	var first_slider := _add_settings_slider(panel, "전체 음량", 92.0, master_volume_db, _set_master_volume)
	_add_settings_slider(panel, "효과음", 166.0, effects_volume_db, _set_effects_volume)
	var shake := CheckButton.new()
	shake.text = "피격 시 카메라 흔들림"
	shake.position = Vector2(82.0, 248.0)
	shake.size = Vector2(460.0, 42.0)
	shake.button_pressed = screen_shake_enabled
	shake.toggled.connect(_set_screen_shake)
	panel.add_child(shake)
	var damage := CheckButton.new()
	damage.text = "피해 숫자 표시"
	damage.position = Vector2(82.0, 298.0)
	damage.size = Vector2(460.0, 42.0)
	damage.button_pressed = damage_numbers_enabled
	damage.toggled.connect(_set_damage_numbers)
	panel.add_child(damage)
	var fullscreen := CheckButton.new()
	fullscreen.text = "전체 화면"
	fullscreen.position = Vector2(82.0, 348.0)
	fullscreen.size = Vector2(460.0, 42.0)
	fullscreen.button_pressed = fullscreen_enabled
	fullscreen.toggled.connect(_set_fullscreen)
	panel.add_child(fullscreen)
	var reset_guide := _button("온보딩 다시 표시", GamePalette.MAGENTA, Vector2(220.0, 44.0))
	reset_guide.position = Vector2(200.0, 410.0)
	reset_guide.pressed.connect(_reset_onboarding_hide)
	panel.add_child(reset_guide)
	var back := _button("로비로 돌아가기 · ESC", GamePalette.YELLOW, Vector2(260.0, 48.0))
	back.position = Vector2(180.0, 478.0)
	back.pressed.connect(_show_menu)
	panel.add_child(back)
	_animate_modal(panel, Vector2(0.0, 20.0))
	if is_instance_valid(first_slider):
		first_slider.grab_focus()

func _volume_percent_text(volume_db: float) -> String:
	return "%d%%" % int(round(db_to_linear(volume_db) * 100.0))

func _add_settings_slider(parent: Control, title: String, y: float, value: float, callback: Callable) -> HSlider:
	var label := _label(title, UI_HEADING_SIZE, GamePalette.TEXT)
	label.position = Vector2(82.0, y)
	label.size = Vector2(160.0, 28.0)
	parent.add_child(label)
	var slider := HSlider.new()
	slider.position = Vector2(245.0, y - 2.0)
	slider.size = Vector2(290.0, 34.0)
	# 원시 데시벨은 플레이어에게 아무 의미가 없어 오른쪽에 % 값을 함께 보여 줍니다.
	# 하한도 -40 → -60으로 낮춰 왼쪽 끝이 실질 무음(0%)이 되게 했습니다 (P1-12).
	slider.min_value = -60.0
	slider.max_value = 0.0
	slider.step = 1.0
	slider.value = value
	slider.value_changed.connect(callback)
	parent.add_child(slider)
	var value_label := _label(_volume_percent_text(value), UI_BODY_SIZE, GamePalette.CYAN)
	value_label.position = Vector2(548.0, y)
	value_label.size = Vector2(56.0, 28.0)
	parent.add_child(value_label)
	slider.value_changed.connect(func(changed: float) -> void: value_label.text = _volume_percent_text(changed))
	return slider

func _set_master_volume(value: float) -> void:
	master_volume_db = value
	var master_bus := AudioServer.get_bus_index("Master")
	if master_bus >= 0:
		AudioServer.set_bus_volume_db(master_bus, master_volume_db)
	_save_progress()

func _set_effects_volume(value: float) -> void:
	effects_volume_db = value
	_save_progress()

func _set_screen_shake(enabled: bool) -> void:
	screen_shake_enabled = enabled
	_save_progress()

func _set_damage_numbers(enabled: bool) -> void:
	damage_numbers_enabled = enabled
	_save_progress()

func _set_fullscreen(enabled: bool) -> void:
	fullscreen_enabled = enabled
	DisplayServer.window_set_mode(DisplayServer.WINDOW_MODE_FULLSCREEN if enabled else DisplayServer.WINDOW_MODE_WINDOWED)
	_save_progress()

func _reset_onboarding_hide() -> void:
	onboarding_seen_session = false
	onboarding_skip_today = false
	var config := ConfigFile.new()
	config.load(PROGRESS_PATH)
	config.set_value("settings", "onboarding_hide_date", "")
	config.save(PROGRESS_PATH)
	_show_banner("다음 새 게임에서 온보딩을 다시 표시합니다", GamePalette.CYAN, 2.2)

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 온보딩 4페이지 (2026-08-07 정적 도식 재설계)
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 사용자 요청 원문 요지: "온보딩 모달의 애니메이션이 엉망이야. 애니메이션을 빼주고,
# 간단한 텍스트와 도식화된 디자인으로 처리해줘. 기존 테마 다 부셔도 돼."
# 그래서 이 화면에서는 트윈을 단 하나도 만들지 않는다.
#   - 삭제: _add_onboarding_motion() — 그림 위에 반투명 마커를 얹어 무한 루프로 돌리던 트윈
#   - 삭제: 패널 등장 _animate_modal() 호출 (다른 화면의 _animate_modal은 그대로 둔다)
#   - 삭제: AI 생성 그림 4장(ONBOARDING_TEXTURES) 의존. 전부 Panel/ColorRect/Label 도식으로 대체
# 골격: 헤더(액센트 바 + 26px 타이틀 + 우측 상태 2줄) → 한 줄 부제 →
#       도식 무대(위 = 그림 없는 정적 도식 / 아래 = 규칙 2~3줄) →
#       스파인 라인 위 페이지 인디케이터 + 이전/다음 → 하단 보조 버튼.
const ONBOARDING_PAGE_COUNT := 4
const ONBOARDING_PANEL_RECT := Rect2(12.0, 6.0, 1256.0, 708.0)
const ONBOARDING_STAGE_RECT := Rect2(28.0, 84.0, 1200.0, 494.0)
const ONBOARDING_STAGE_RULE_TOP := 336.0
const ONBOARDING_NAV_Y := 594.0
const ONBOARDING_NAV_SIZE := Vector2(152.0, 44.0)
const ONBOARDING_FOOTER_Y := 654.0
const ONBOARDING_STEP_PITCH := 96.0
const ONBOARDING_STEP_SIZE := Vector2(74.0, 38.0)

func _onboarding_pages() -> Array:
	# 텍스트는 짧고 명확하게. 페이지마다 규칙은 최대 3줄이고 나머지는 도식이 설명한다.
	# 반드시 전달해야 하는 두 가지는 2페이지(딜싸이클)와 3페이지(미선택 카드 → 마왕)다.
	return [
		{
			"title":"조작은 네 가지뿐",
			"subtitle":"이동 · 대시 · 상호작용 · 딜싸이클 공장",
			"color":GamePalette.GREEN,
			"rules":[
				"이동하면 용사가 가장 짧은 방향으로 돌아 그쪽을 공격합니다.",
				"공격 버튼은 없습니다. 무엇을 언제 쓸지는 딜싸이클이 정합니다."
			]
		},
		{
			"title":"딜싸이클 — 한 번에 한 칸",
			"subtitle":"전투 규칙 — 왼쪽에서 오른쪽으로, 현재 칸만 실행",
			"color":GamePalette.CYAN,
			"rules":[
				"왼쪽 → 오른쪽. 한 번에 지금 칸 하나만 실행합니다.",
				"빈칸은 쉬는 칸이 아니라 기본 베기를 실행합니다.",
				"마지막 칸이 끝나면 RELOAD만큼 쉬고 다시 첫 칸으로 돌아옵니다."
			]
		},
		{
			"title":"고르지 않은 카드는 마왕에게 간다",
			"subtitle":"성장 규칙 — 내가 버린 카드가 그대로 적의 전력이 된다",
			"color":GamePalette.RED,
			"rules":[
				"레벨업 카드 2장 중 고른 1장만 내 딜싸이클에 들어갑니다.",
				"고르지 않은 1장은 사라지지 않고 마왕의 딜싸이클에 실립니다.",
				"딜싸이클 업그레이드를 고르면 두 장 모두 마왕에게 갑니다."
			]
		},
		{
			"title":"낮에 준비하고, 밤을 넘어 마왕에게",
			"subtitle":"목표 — 낮과 밤을 버티고 시련 구슬 4개로 전직해 마왕성으로",
			"color":GamePalette.YELLOW,
			"rules":[
				"낮에는 마물을 찾아 성장하고, 밤에는 습격을 버팁니다.",
				"성에서 카드 합성 · 상점 · 강화를 이용할 수 있습니다.",
				"마왕성에 들어가기 전에 마왕의 딜싸이클 전체를 먼저 보여줍니다."
			]
		}
	]

func _show_onboarding(page: int = 0) -> void:
	onboarding_page = clampi(page, 0, ONBOARDING_PAGE_COUNT - 1)
	state = "onboarding"
	hud.visible = false
	_clear_overlay()
	overlay = Control.new()
	overlay.name = "OnboardingPage%d" % (onboarding_page + 1)
	overlay.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	overlay.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	ui_root.add_child(overlay)
	var background := ColorRect.new()
	background.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	background.color = Color("070b14")
	overlay.add_child(background)
	var data: Dictionary = _onboarding_pages()[onboarding_page]
	var page_color: Color = data["color"]
	var panel := Panel.new()
	panel.position = ONBOARDING_PANEL_RECT.position
	panel.size = ONBOARDING_PANEL_RECT.size
	panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(UI_MODAL_BG, UI_EDGE, 0, UI_BORDER_MODAL))
	overlay.add_child(panel)
	var accent := ColorRect.new()
	accent.position = Vector2(26.0, 14.0)
	accent.size = Vector2(7.0, 34.0)
	accent.color = page_color
	panel.add_child(accent)
	var heading := _label(String(data["title"]), UI_TITLE_SIZE, GamePalette.TEXT)
	heading.position = Vector2(46.0, 10.0)
	heading.size = Vector2(780.0, 42.0)
	panel.add_child(heading)
	var counter := _label("%d / %d" % [onboarding_page + 1, ONBOARDING_PAGE_COUNT], UI_HEADING_SIZE, page_color)
	counter.position = Vector2(872.0, 12.0)
	counter.size = Vector2(356.0, 24.0)
	counter.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT
	panel.add_child(counter)
	var key_hint := _label("← → 이동 · SPACE 다음 · ESC 캐릭터 선택", UI_LABEL_SIZE, GamePalette.MUTED.darkened(0.1))
	key_hint.position = Vector2(872.0, 36.0)
	key_hint.size = Vector2(356.0, 22.0)
	key_hint.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT
	panel.add_child(key_hint)
	var subtitle := _label(String(data["subtitle"]), UI_BODY_SIZE + 1, GamePalette.MUTED)
	subtitle.position = Vector2(28.0, 56.0)
	subtitle.size = Vector2(820.0, 22.0)
	panel.add_child(subtitle)

	var stage := Panel.new()
	stage.position = ONBOARDING_STAGE_RECT.position
	stage.size = ONBOARDING_STAGE_RECT.size
	stage.clip_contents = true
	stage.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	stage.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(UI_PANEL_BG, page_color.darkened(0.4), 0, UI_BORDER_CARD))
	panel.add_child(stage)
	match onboarding_page:
		0:
			_onboarding_diagram_controls(stage, page_color)
		1:
			_onboarding_diagram_cycle(stage, page_color)
		2:
			_onboarding_diagram_fate(stage, page_color)
		_:
			_onboarding_diagram_journey(stage, page_color)
	_onboarding_rules(stage, data.get("rules", []), page_color)

	_build_onboarding_steps(panel, page_color)
	var previous := _button("◀ 이전", GamePalette.MUTED, ONBOARDING_NAV_SIZE)
	previous.position = Vector2(ONBOARDING_STAGE_RECT.position.x, ONBOARDING_NAV_Y)
	previous.disabled = onboarding_page == 0
	previous.focus_mode = Control.FOCUS_NONE
	previous.pressed.connect(_show_onboarding.bind(onboarding_page - 1))
	panel.add_child(previous)
	var last_page := onboarding_page == ONBOARDING_PAGE_COUNT - 1
	var next := _button("게임 시작 ▶" if last_page else "다음 ▶", GamePalette.YELLOW if last_page else GamePalette.CYAN, ONBOARDING_NAV_SIZE)
	next.position = Vector2(ONBOARDING_STAGE_RECT.end.x - ONBOARDING_NAV_SIZE.x, ONBOARDING_NAV_Y)
	next.focus_mode = Control.FOCUS_NONE
	if last_page:
		next.pressed.connect(_finish_onboarding)
	else:
		next.pressed.connect(_show_onboarding.bind(onboarding_page + 1))
	panel.add_child(next)

	var back := _button("캐릭터 선택", GamePalette.MUTED, Vector2(148.0, 34.0))
	back.position = Vector2(ONBOARDING_STAGE_RECT.position.x, ONBOARDING_FOOTER_Y)
	back.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", UI_BODY_SIZE + 1)
	back.focus_mode = Control.FOCUS_NONE
	back.pressed.connect(_show_character_select)
	panel.add_child(back)
	# 기본 CheckBox는 게임 팔레트와 어긋나므로 공장과 같은 버튼 스타일 토글로 통일합니다.
	var hide_today := _button("%s  오늘은 그만 보기" % ("☑" if onboarding_skip_today else "☐"), GamePalette.CYAN if onboarding_skip_today else GamePalette.MUTED, Vector2(238.0, 34.0))
	hide_today.position = Vector2((ONBOARDING_PANEL_RECT.size.x - 238.0) * 0.5, ONBOARDING_FOOTER_Y)
	hide_today.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", UI_BODY_SIZE + 1)
	hide_today.focus_mode = Control.FOCUS_NONE
	hide_today.pressed.connect(_toggle_onboarding_skip_today)
	panel.add_child(hide_today)
	var footer_note := _label("숫자 칸을 눌러 원하는 페이지로 바로 이동", UI_CAPTION_SIZE, GamePalette.MUTED.darkened(0.08))
	footer_note.position = Vector2(858.0, ONBOARDING_FOOTER_Y + 9.0)
	footer_note.size = Vector2(370.0, 20.0)
	footer_note.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT
	panel.add_child(footer_note)

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 온보딩 도식 프리미티브 (정적 전용 — 여기서 트윈을 만들지 않는다)
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
func _onboarding_box(parent: Control, rect: Rect2, background: Color, border: Color, width: int = UI_BORDER_CARD) -> Panel:
	var box := Panel.new()
	box.position = rect.position
	box.size = rect.size
	box.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	box.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(background, border, 0, width))
	parent.add_child(box)
	return box

func _onboarding_bar(parent: Control, rect: Rect2, color: Color) -> ColorRect:
	var bar := ColorRect.new()
	bar.position = rect.position
	bar.size = rect.size
	bar.color = color
	bar.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	parent.add_child(bar)
	return bar

func _onboarding_text(parent: Control, rect: Rect2, text: String, font_size: int, color: Color) -> Label:
	var label := _label(text, font_size, color)
	label.position = rect.position
	label.size = rect.size
	parent.add_child(label)
	return label

func _onboarding_center_text(parent: Control, rect: Rect2, text: String, font_size: int, color: Color) -> Label:
	var label := _onboarding_text(parent, rect, text, font_size, color)
	label.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	label.vertical_alignment = VERTICAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	return label

func _onboarding_glyph(parent: Control, center: Vector2, glyph: String, font_size: int, color: Color) -> void:
	# 화살표는 선(ColorRect) + 글리프 라벨 조합이다. 회전이나 이동은 쓰지 않는다.
	_onboarding_center_text(parent, Rect2(center.x - 24.0, center.y - 16.0, 48.0, 32.0), glyph, font_size, color)

func _onboarding_keycap(parent: Control, rect: Rect2, cap_text: String, color: Color) -> void:
	_onboarding_box(parent, rect, Color(color, 0.14), color, UI_BORDER_CARD)
	_onboarding_center_text(parent, rect, cap_text, 17, GamePalette.TEXT)

func _onboarding_rules(stage: Control, rules: Array, page_color: Color) -> void:
	# 무대 아래쪽 고정 위치의 규칙 목록. 도식이 그림, 이 줄들이 확정 문장이다.
	_onboarding_bar(stage, Rect2(40.0, ONBOARDING_STAGE_RULE_TOP, ONBOARDING_STAGE_RECT.size.x - 80.0, 2.0), UI_EDGE_SOFT)
	for index in rules.size():
		var top := ONBOARDING_STAGE_RULE_TOP + 26.0 + float(index) * 40.0
		_onboarding_bar(stage, Rect2(44.0, top + 10.0, 10.0, 10.0), page_color)
		var line := _onboarding_text(stage, Rect2(70.0, top, ONBOARDING_STAGE_RECT.size.x - 120.0, 30.0), String(rules[index]), 16, GamePalette.TEXT)
		line.vertical_alignment = VERTICAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER

func _onboarding_diagram_controls(stage: Control, page_color: Color) -> void:
	# 왼쪽: WASD 키캡 십자. 오른쪽: 나머지 3키를 한 줄씩.
	var cap := Vector2(62.0, 54.0)
	var cross_center := 250.0
	_onboarding_keycap(stage, Rect2(cross_center - cap.x * 0.5, 52.0, cap.x, cap.y), "W", page_color)
	_onboarding_keycap(stage, Rect2(cross_center - cap.x * 1.5 - 8.0, 114.0, cap.x, cap.y), "A", page_color)
	_onboarding_keycap(stage, Rect2(cross_center - cap.x * 0.5, 114.0, cap.x, cap.y), "S", page_color)
	_onboarding_keycap(stage, Rect2(cross_center + cap.x * 0.5 + 8.0, 114.0, cap.x, cap.y), "D", page_color)
	_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(70.0, 190.0, 360.0, 28.0), "이동 · 바라보는 방향", 19, GamePalette.TEXT)
	_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(70.0, 220.0, 360.0, 24.0), "방향키도 똑같이 씁니다", UI_BODY_SIZE, GamePalette.MUTED)
	_onboarding_bar(stage, Rect2(470.0, 40.0, 2.0, 226.0), UI_EDGE_SOFT)
	var rows := [
		{"key":"SHIFT", "width":118.0, "title":"대시", "desc":"약 0.14초 동안 피격 무효. 기본 쿨타임 10초.", "color":GamePalette.CYAN},
		{"key":"E", "width":62.0, "title":"상호작용", "desc":"성 · 보물상자 · NPC 앞에서 누릅니다.", "color":GamePalette.YELLOW},
		{"key":"ESC", "width":88.0, "title":"딜싸이클 공장", "desc":"언제든 열어 카드 배치를 바꿉니다.", "color":GamePalette.MAGENTA}
	]
	for index in rows.size():
		var row: Dictionary = rows[index]
		var row_color: Color = row["color"]
		var top := 46.0 + float(index) * 78.0
		_onboarding_keycap(stage, Rect2(524.0, top, float(row["width"]), 54.0), String(row["key"]), row_color)
		_onboarding_text(stage, Rect2(664.0, top + 2.0, 500.0, 28.0), String(row["title"]), 19, row_color)
		_onboarding_text(stage, Rect2(664.0, top + 30.0, 500.0, 24.0), String(row["desc"]), UI_BODY_SIZE + 1, GamePalette.MUTED)

func _onboarding_diagram_cycle(stage: Control, page_color: Color) -> void:
	# [1칸]─[2칸]─[3칸]→RELOAD 미니 레일. 실제 공장 레일과 같은 스파인 언어를 쓴다.
	var card := Vector2(210.0, 128.0)
	var gap := 58.0
	var reload_width := 180.0
	var rail_top := 54.0
	var total := card.x * 3.0 + gap * 3.0 + reload_width
	var start_x := (ONBOARDING_STAGE_RECT.size.x - total) * 0.5
	var line_y := rail_top + card.y * 0.5 - 2.0
	_onboarding_bar(stage, Rect2(start_x + 20.0, line_y, total - 40.0, 4.0), FACTORY_RAIL_SPINE_BUILT)
	# 현재 칸 표식은 정지된 배지다. 깜빡이거나 움직이지 않는다.
	_onboarding_box(stage, Rect2(start_x, 12.0, card.x, 30.0), Color(page_color, 0.16), page_color, UI_BORDER_FRAME)
	_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(start_x, 12.0, card.x, 30.0), "지금 실행 중", UI_BODY_SIZE, page_color)
	var slots := [
		{"badge":"1칸", "name":"회전베기", "note":"배치한 스킬", "color":GamePalette.CYAN},
		{"badge":"2칸", "name":"빈칸", "note":"기본 베기 실행", "color":GamePalette.STONE},
		{"badge":"3칸", "name":"화염 장판", "note":"배치한 스킬", "color":GamePalette.ORANGE}
	]
	for index in slots.size():
		var slot: Dictionary = slots[index]
		var slot_color: Color = slot["color"]
		var slot_x := start_x + float(index) * (card.x + gap)
		_onboarding_box(stage, Rect2(slot_x, rail_top, card.x, card.y), UI_CHIP_BG, slot_color, UI_BORDER_CARD)
		_onboarding_bar(stage, Rect2(slot_x + 2.0, rail_top + 2.0, card.x - 4.0, 26.0), Color(slot_color, 0.20))
		_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(slot_x + 2.0, rail_top + 2.0, card.x - 4.0, 26.0), String(slot["badge"]), UI_LABEL_SIZE + 1, slot_color)
		_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(slot_x, rail_top + 44.0, card.x, 32.0), String(slot["name"]), 20, GamePalette.TEXT)
		_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(slot_x, rail_top + 82.0, card.x, 24.0), String(slot["note"]), UI_BODY_SIZE, GamePalette.MUTED)
		_onboarding_glyph(stage, Vector2(slot_x + card.x + gap * 0.5, line_y + 2.0), "▶", 22, page_color)
	var reload_x := start_x + 3.0 * (card.x + gap)
	_onboarding_box(stage, Rect2(reload_x, rail_top, reload_width, card.y), UI_CHIP_BG, GamePalette.ORANGE, UI_BORDER_CARD)
	_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(reload_x, rail_top + 36.0, reload_width, 32.0), "RELOAD", 22, GamePalette.ORANGE)
	_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(reload_x + 10.0, rail_top + 72.0, reload_width - 20.0, 44.0), "이번 바퀴에 쓴\nRELOAD 합만큼 대기", UI_BODY_SIZE, GamePalette.MUTED)
	# RELOAD → 첫 칸으로 돌아오는 회귀 화살표 (직선 3개 + ▲ 글리프)
	var rail_bottom := rail_top + card.y
	var loop_y := rail_bottom + 44.0
	var first_center := start_x + card.x * 0.5
	var reload_center := reload_x + reload_width * 0.5
	var loop_color := page_color.darkened(0.15)
	_onboarding_bar(stage, Rect2(reload_center - 1.5, rail_bottom + 6.0, 3.0, loop_y - rail_bottom - 6.0), loop_color)
	_onboarding_bar(stage, Rect2(first_center, loop_y, reload_center - first_center, 3.0), loop_color)
	_onboarding_bar(stage, Rect2(first_center - 1.5, rail_bottom + 22.0, 3.0, loop_y - rail_bottom - 22.0), loop_color)
	_onboarding_glyph(stage, Vector2(first_center, rail_bottom + 14.0), "▲", 20, loop_color)
	_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(first_center, loop_y + 8.0, reload_center - first_center, 26.0), "한 바퀴가 끝나면 다시 1칸으로", UI_BODY_SIZE + 1, page_color)

func _onboarding_diagram_fate(stage: Control, page_color: Color) -> void:
	# 게임의 두 번째 불변 규칙: 고르지 않은 카드가 마왕 공장으로 간다.
	# 카드 2장 → 아래로 갈라지는 화살표 → 왼쪽 내 공장 / 오른쪽 마왕 공장.
	_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(0.0, 10.0, ONBOARDING_STAGE_RECT.size.x, 26.0), "레벨업 — 카드 2장 중 1장만 고른다", UI_HEADING_SIZE, GamePalette.MUTED)
	var card := Vector2(232.0, 108.0)
	var card_top := 44.0
	var left_x := 300.0
	var right_x := 668.0
	_onboarding_box(stage, Rect2(left_x, card_top, card.x, card.y), UI_CHIP_BG, GamePalette.GREEN, UI_BORDER_CARD)
	_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(left_x, card_top + 18.0, card.x, 30.0), "회전베기", 20, GamePalette.TEXT)
	_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(left_x, card_top + 58.0, card.x, 26.0), "☑ 내가 고른 카드", UI_BODY_SIZE + 1, GamePalette.GREEN)
	_onboarding_box(stage, Rect2(right_x, card_top, card.x, card.y), UI_CHIP_BG, GamePalette.RED, UI_BORDER_CARD)
	_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(right_x, card_top + 18.0, card.x, 30.0), "화염 장판", 20, GamePalette.TEXT)
	_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(right_x, card_top + 58.0, card.x, 26.0), "☒ 고르지 않은 카드", UI_BODY_SIZE + 1, GamePalette.RED)
	var card_bottom := card_top + card.y
	var junction_y := 186.0
	var target := Vector2(340.0, 96.0)
	var target_top := 220.0
	var mine_x := 96.0
	var demon_x := 764.0
	var left_center := left_x + card.x * 0.5
	var right_center := right_x + card.x * 0.5
	var mine_center := mine_x + target.x * 0.5
	var demon_center := demon_x + target.x * 0.5
	_onboarding_bar(stage, Rect2(left_center - 1.5, card_bottom, 3.0, junction_y - card_bottom), GamePalette.GREEN)
	_onboarding_bar(stage, Rect2(mine_center - 1.5, junction_y, left_center - mine_center + 3.0, 3.0), GamePalette.GREEN)
	_onboarding_bar(stage, Rect2(mine_center - 1.5, junction_y, 3.0, 14.0), GamePalette.GREEN)
	_onboarding_glyph(stage, Vector2(mine_center, junction_y + 20.0), "▼", 20, GamePalette.GREEN)
	_onboarding_bar(stage, Rect2(right_center - 1.5, card_bottom, 3.0, junction_y - card_bottom), GamePalette.RED)
	_onboarding_bar(stage, Rect2(right_center - 1.5, junction_y, demon_center - right_center + 3.0, 3.0), GamePalette.RED)
	_onboarding_bar(stage, Rect2(demon_center - 1.5, junction_y, 3.0, 14.0), GamePalette.RED)
	_onboarding_glyph(stage, Vector2(demon_center, junction_y + 20.0), "▼", 20, GamePalette.RED)
	_onboarding_box(stage, Rect2(mine_x, target_top, target.x, target.y), Color(GamePalette.GREEN, 0.10), GamePalette.GREEN, UI_BORDER_CARD)
	_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(mine_x, target_top + 18.0, target.x, 30.0), "내 딜싸이클", 21, GamePalette.GREEN)
	_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(mine_x, target_top + 54.0, target.x, 26.0), "바로 배치되어 함께 싸운다", UI_BODY_SIZE + 1, GamePalette.TEXT)
	_onboarding_box(stage, Rect2(demon_x, target_top, target.x, target.y), Color(GamePalette.RED, 0.12), GamePalette.RED, UI_BORDER_CARD)
	_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(demon_x, target_top + 18.0, target.x, 30.0), "마왕의 딜싸이클", 21, GamePalette.RED)
	_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(demon_x, target_top + 54.0, target.x, 26.0), "마왕이 합성해 나를 공격한다", UI_BODY_SIZE + 1, GamePalette.TEXT)
	_onboarding_box(stage, Rect2(470.0, target_top + 12.0, 260.0, 72.0), UI_CHIP_BG, page_color.darkened(0.2), UI_BORDER_FRAME)
	_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(470.0, target_top + 22.0, 260.0, 26.0), "버린 카드는 사라지지 않는다", UI_BODY_SIZE + 1, page_color)
	_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(470.0, target_top + 48.0, 260.0, 24.0), "내가 안 쓰면 마왕이 쓴다", UI_BODY_SIZE, GamePalette.MUTED)

func _onboarding_diagram_journey(stage: Control, page_color: Color) -> void:
	# 위: 낮/밤 길이 띠. 아래: 마왕성까지의 4단계 사슬.
	var band_x := 108.0
	var band_w := 984.0
	var band_top := 28.0
	var band_h := 76.0
	var day_w := band_w * DAY_DURATION / (DAY_DURATION + NIGHT_DURATION)
	_onboarding_box(stage, Rect2(band_x, band_top, day_w, band_h), Color(GamePalette.YELLOW, 0.12), GamePalette.YELLOW, UI_BORDER_CARD)
	_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(band_x, band_top + 12.0, day_w, 28.0), "낮  %d초" % int(DAY_DURATION), 20, GamePalette.YELLOW)
	_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(band_x, band_top + 42.0, day_w, 24.0), "마물을 찾아다니며 성장", UI_BODY_SIZE, GamePalette.TEXT)
	_onboarding_box(stage, Rect2(band_x + day_w, band_top, band_w - day_w, band_h), Color(GamePalette.NIGHT, 0.28), GamePalette.NIGHT, UI_BORDER_CARD)
	_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(band_x + day_w, band_top + 12.0, band_w - day_w, 28.0), "밤  %d초" % int(NIGHT_DURATION), 20, GamePalette.CYAN)
	_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(band_x + day_w, band_top + 42.0, band_w - day_w, 24.0), "습격을 버틴다", UI_BODY_SIZE, GamePalette.TEXT)
	_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(band_x, band_top + band_h + 10.0, band_w, 24.0), "낮과 밤은 계속 반복되고, 주기가 오를수록 거세집니다", UI_BODY_SIZE + 1, GamePalette.MUTED)
	var steps := ["레벨업으로\n딜싸이클 확장", "동서남북 시련\n구슬 4개", "성에서 전직", "마왕성 도전"]
	var chip := Vector2(240.0, 88.0)
	var chip_gap := 44.0
	var chain_w := chip.x * float(steps.size()) + chip_gap * float(steps.size() - 1)
	var chain_x := (ONBOARDING_STAGE_RECT.size.x - chain_w) * 0.5
	var chain_top := 178.0
	for index in steps.size():
		var chip_x := chain_x + float(index) * (chip.x + chip_gap)
		var chip_color := GamePalette.RED if index == steps.size() - 1 else page_color
		_onboarding_box(stage, Rect2(chip_x, chain_top, chip.x, chip.y), UI_CHIP_BG, chip_color, UI_BORDER_CARD)
		_onboarding_center_text(stage, Rect2(chip_x + 12.0, chain_top, chip.x - 24.0, chip.y), String(steps[index]), UI_HEADING_SIZE, GamePalette.TEXT)
		if index < steps.size() - 1:
			_onboarding_glyph(stage, Vector2(chip_x + chip.x + chip_gap * 0.5, chain_top + chip.y * 0.5), "▶", 22, page_color)

func _build_onboarding_steps(panel: Control, page_color: Color) -> void:
	# 공장 레일과 같은 언어: 연속 스파인 라인 위에 페이지 칸이 인라인으로 올라가고
	# 지나온 구간만 밝은 색으로 이어진다. (정적 — 트윈 없음)
	var strip_width := ONBOARDING_STEP_PITCH * float(ONBOARDING_PAGE_COUNT - 1) + ONBOARDING_STEP_SIZE.x
	var strip := Control.new()
	strip.position = Vector2((ONBOARDING_PANEL_RECT.size.x - strip_width) * 0.5, ONBOARDING_NAV_Y)
	strip.size = Vector2(strip_width, ONBOARDING_NAV_SIZE.y)
	strip.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_PASS
	panel.add_child(strip)
	var line_y := (strip.size.y - 4.0) * 0.5
	var spine := ColorRect.new()
	spine.position = Vector2(0.0, line_y)
	spine.size = Vector2(strip_width, 4.0)
	spine.color = FACTORY_RAIL_SPINE_GHOST
	spine.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	strip.add_child(spine)
	var travelled := ColorRect.new()
	travelled.position = Vector2(0.0, line_y)
	travelled.size = Vector2(float(onboarding_page) * ONBOARDING_STEP_PITCH + ONBOARDING_STEP_SIZE.x * 0.5, 4.0)
	travelled.color = FACTORY_RAIL_SPINE_BUILT
	travelled.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	strip.add_child(travelled)
	for index in ONBOARDING_PAGE_COUNT:
		# 현재 칸만 페이지 색으로 강조하고, 지나온 칸은 중립 회색으로 한 단계 밝게 둔다.
		var step_color := GamePalette.STONE_DARK
		if index == onboarding_page:
			step_color = page_color
		elif index < onboarding_page:
			step_color = GamePalette.STONE
		var step := _button("%d" % (index + 1), step_color, ONBOARDING_STEP_SIZE)
		step.position = Vector2(float(index) * ONBOARDING_STEP_PITCH, (strip.size.y - ONBOARDING_STEP_SIZE.y) * 0.5)
		step.focus_mode = Control.FOCUS_NONE
		step.pressed.connect(_show_onboarding.bind(index))
		strip.add_child(step)

func _set_onboarding_skip_today(enabled: bool) -> void:
	onboarding_skip_today = enabled

func _toggle_onboarding_skip_today() -> void:
	_set_onboarding_skip_today(not onboarding_skip_today)
	_show_onboarding(onboarding_page)

func _onboarding_hidden_today() -> bool:
	var config := ConfigFile.new()
	if config.load(PROGRESS_PATH) != OK:
		return false
	return String(config.get_value("settings", "onboarding_hide_date", "")) == Time.get_date_string_from_system()

func _finish_onboarding() -> void:
	onboarding_seen_session = true
	if onboarding_skip_today:
		var config := ConfigFile.new()
		config.load(PROGRESS_PATH)
		config.set_value("settings", "onboarding_hide_date", Time.get_date_string_from_system())
		config.save(PROGRESS_PATH)
	_begin_run()

func _continue_saved_run() -> void:
	var snapshot := _read_run_snapshot()
	if snapshot.is_empty():
		saved_run_available = false
		saved_run_playtime = 0.0
		_show_menu()
		return
	selected_character_id = String(snapshot.get("character_id", "swordsman"))
	_begin_run(snapshot)

func _begin_run(snapshot: Dictionary = {}) -> void:
	var resuming := not snapshot.is_empty()
	onboarding_seen_session = true
	if not resuming:
		selected_character_id = "swordsman"
		if not automated_test and OS.get_cmdline_user_args().is_empty():
			_clear_run_save()
	get_tree().paused = false
	_clear_overlay()
	if is_instance_valid(gameplay_root):
		gameplay_root.free()
	gameplay_root = Node2D.new()
	gameplay_root.name = "Gameplay"
	gameplay_root.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_PAUSABLE
	add_child(gameplay_root)

	player = PLAYER_SCRIPT.new()
	player.setup(self, selected_character_id)
	player.position = Vector2.ZERO
	player.health_changed.connect(_on_player_health_changed)
	player.shield_changed.connect(_on_player_shield_changed)
	player.died.connect(_on_player_died)
	world = WORLD_GRID_SCRIPT.new()
	world.setup(player)
	gameplay_root.add_child(world)
	gameplay_root.add_child(player)
	factory = FACTORY_SCRIPT.new()
	factory.reset()
	player_cycle = CYCLE_CONTROLLER_SCRIPT.new()
	player_cycle.setup(self, player, factory, false, true)
	gameplay_root.add_child(player_cycle)
	skill_effect_controller = SKILL_EFFECT_SCRIPT.new()
	skill_effect_controller.setup(self, player)
	gameplay_root.add_child(skill_effect_controller)
	canvas_modulate = CanvasModulate.new()
	canvas_modulate.color = Color.WHITE
	gameplay_root.add_child(canvas_modulate)

	selected_skills.clear()
	rejected_skills.clear()
	boss_items.clear()
	boss_advancement_skills.clear()
	camp_states.clear()
	current_pair.clear()
	opened_features.clear()
	current_interactable.clear()
	active_enemies.clear()
	enemy_spatial.clear()
	active_effect_nodes = 0
	xp_fraction = 0.0
	inside_castle = false
	current_castle.clear()
	castle_interior = null
	level = 1
	experience = 0
	xp_target = 8
	kills = 0
	gold = 20
	elapsed_time = 0.0
	phase_elapsed = 0.0
	cycle_number = 1
	is_night = false
	spawn_timer = 0.2
	interaction_timer = 0.0
	spatial_refresh_timer = 0.0
	hud_refresh_timer = 0.0
	hotfix_burst_running = false
	collect_sound_counter = 0
	boss = null
	boss_factory = null
	boss_cycle = null
	pending_boss_toast_cards.clear()
	boss_toast_queue.clear()
	if is_instance_valid(boss_toast):
		boss_toast.queue_free()
	boss_toast = null
	pending_advancement.clear()
	shop_offers.clear()
	shop_refresh_count = 0
	shop_castle_id = ""
	for direction: String in world.get_trial_camps():
		camp_states[direction] = {"position":world.get_trial_camps()[direction], "activated":false, "remaining":0, "cleared":false}
	world.set_cleared_trial_camps({})
	if resuming:
		_restore_run_snapshot(snapshot)
	else:
		world.set_opened_features(opened_features)
	state = "playing"
	hud.visible = true
	boss_panel.visible = false
	interaction_text.visible = false
	# 런 시작 필드 population. 마지막 behavior 4는 낮 선공몹 게이트
	# (MonsterLibrary.aggro_gate_ok)를 그대로 받으므로 주기 1~4에서는 순한 몹으로
	# 대체되고, 이어하기로 주기 5 이상에서 시작하면 그때부터 선공몹이 섞인다.
	var starter_behaviors: Array[int] = [1, 1, 1, 2, 2, 2, 3, 3, 4]
	for behavior: int in starter_behaviors:
		var spawn_position: Vector2 = world.find_walkable_near(player.global_position, rng, 260.0, 720.0)
		_spawn_enemy_instance(spawn_position, behavior)
	_update_hud()
	if state == "playing":
		_check_first_advancement()
	run_save_timer = 5.0
	_show_banner("모험을 이어갑니다" if resuming else "제1일 · 마왕성을 찾아 떠나라", GamePalette.CYAN if resuming else GamePalette.YELLOW, 2.6)
	if not automated_test and OS.get_cmdline_user_args().is_empty():
		_save_run_snapshot()

func uses_factory_combat() -> bool:
	return true

func can_cycle_run(is_boss_cycle: bool = false) -> bool:
	if is_boss_cycle:
		return state == "boss"
	return state in ["playing", "boss"] and not inside_castle

func get_factory_dash_multiplier() -> float:
	if factory == null:
		return 1.0
	return clampf(1.0 + float(factory.global_effects().get("dash_reload", 0.0)), 0.45, 1.5)

func can_player_attack() -> bool:
	return state in ["playing", "boss"]

func can_player_dash() -> bool:
	return state in ["playing", "boss"]

# 일시정지 모달(스킬 확정·공장 배치/편집/강화/건설·아이템 2지선다·운명 전달·NPC 서비스·
# 전직 특별 카드)에서 필드로 돌아올 때 0.5초 유예 무적을 준다.
# 성 내부에서 필드로 나올 때도 같은 무적을 준다(페이드 전환 후 위치 복귀 직후).
# 필드 전투가 없는 상태(castle_interior·menu 등)에서는 부여하지 않는다.
func _grant_modal_return_invulnerability() -> void:
	if state not in ["playing", "boss"] or not is_instance_valid(player):
		return
	player.grant_invulnerability(MODAL_RETURN_INVULN)

func on_player_dash_started(attacker: Node2D) -> void:
	spawn_burst(attacker.global_position, GamePalette.CYAN, 10, 125.0, 0.24)
	spawn_attack_effect(attacker.global_position, "dash", GamePalette.CYAN, -attacker.dash_direction, 82.0, 0.18)
	play_sound("shoot", -7.0)

func process_dash_damage(hit_ids: Dictionary) -> void:
	if is_instance_valid(skill_effect_controller):
		skill_effect_controller.process_dash_damage(hit_ids)

func can_player_stand(point: Vector2) -> bool:
	if inside_castle and is_instance_valid(castle_interior):
		return castle_interior.is_walkable(point)
	return is_instance_valid(world) and world.is_walkable(point)

func can_enemy_stand(point: Vector2) -> bool:
	return is_instance_valid(world) and world.is_walkable(point)

func _current_spawn_interval() -> float:
	if is_night:
		return clampf(NIGHT_SPAWN_INTERVAL_BASE - cycle_number * NIGHT_SPAWN_INTERVAL_STEP, NIGHT_SPAWN_INTERVAL_FLOOR, NIGHT_SPAWN_INTERVAL_BASE)
	return clampf(1.15 - cycle_number * 0.06, 0.62, 1.15)

func _current_enemy_limit() -> int:
	if is_night:
		return mini(MAX_ENEMIES, NIGHT_ENEMY_LIMIT_BASE + cycle_number * NIGHT_ENEMY_LIMIT_STEP)
	return mini(MAX_ENEMIES, 27 + cycle_number * 3)

# 낮 -> 밤 전환 순간에 즉시 소환하는 습격체 수.
func _night_raid_burst_count() -> int:
	return mini(NIGHT_RAID_BURST_BASE + cycle_number * NIGHT_RAID_BURST_STEP, NIGHT_RAID_BURST_CAP)

func _maintain_field_population() -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(player):
		return
	if active_enemies.size() >= _current_enemy_limit():
		return
	var archetype := MonsterLibrary.roll(rng, cycle_number, is_night)
	var behavior: int = int(archetype.get("behavior", _roll_field_behavior()))
	var minimum: float = 560.0 if is_night else 270.0
	var maximum: float = 790.0 if is_night else 760.0
	var spawn_position: Vector2 = world.find_walkable_near(player.global_position, rng, minimum, maximum)
	var anchor := _spawn_enemy_instance(spawn_position, behavior, "", false, "", false, String(archetype.get("id", "")))
	if behavior == 3 and not is_night and rng.randf() < 0.45:
		var pack_kind := String(anchor.kind) if is_instance_valid(anchor) else ""
		for index in 2:
			var pack_position: Vector2 = spawn_position + Vector2.from_angle(index * PI + rng.randf()) * rng.randf_range(35.0, 75.0)
			if world.is_walkable(pack_position):
				_spawn_enemy_instance(pack_position, 3, "", false, "", false, pack_kind)

func _roll_field_behavior() -> int:
	if is_night:
		return rng.randi_range(1, 4)
	var roll := rng.randi_range(0, 99)
	if roll < 36: return 1
	if roll < 68: return 2
	if roll < 91: return 3
	return 4

# allow_aggro_override=true는 낮 선공몹 게이트(MonsterLibrary.aggro_gate_ok)를 건너뛰는
# 의도된 강제 스폰 경로에만 쓴다(시련 캠프 정예·미믹 함정·보스 하수인·자동 테스트).
# 일반 필드 population과 런 시작 스타터는 기본값 false로 게이트를 그대로 받는다.
func _spawn_enemy_instance(world_position: Vector2, behavior: int = 0, forced_module: String = "", split_child: bool = false, camp_id: String = "", camp_elite: bool = false, archetype_id: String = "", allow_aggro_override: bool = false) -> Node2D:
	if not is_instance_valid(gameplay_root) or not is_instance_valid(player):
		return null
	if active_enemies.size() >= MAX_ENEMIES + 26:
		return null
	var archetype := MonsterLibrary.by_id(archetype_id) if not archetype_id.is_empty() else MonsterLibrary.roll(rng, cycle_number, is_night, behavior, allow_aggro_override)
	if archetype.is_empty():
		archetype = MonsterLibrary.roll(rng, cycle_number, is_night, behavior, allow_aggro_override)
	behavior = int(archetype.get("behavior", rng.randi_range(1, 4)))
	var kind: String = String(archetype.get("id", "mossling"))
	var enemy := ENEMY_SCRIPT.new()
	var power_level := float(cycle_number - 1) * 1.1 + float(level - 1) * 0.32 + minf(elapsed_time / 180.0, 2.5)
	enemy.setup(self, player, kind, behavior, power_level, rejected_skills, false, split_child)
	if not forced_module.is_empty():
		enemy.force_module(forced_module)
	if not camp_id.is_empty():
		enemy.mark_trial(camp_id, camp_elite)
	enemy.position = world_position
	gameplay_root.add_child(enemy)
	if is_night:
		enemy.set_night_raid(true)
	return enemy

func _check_trial_camps() -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(player):
		return
	for direction: String in camp_states:
		var data: Dictionary = camp_states[direction]
		if bool(data.get("cleared", false)) or bool(data.get("activated", false)):
			continue
		if player.global_position.distance_to(data["position"]) <= 520.0:
			_activate_trial_camp(direction)

func _activate_trial_camp(direction: String) -> void:
	if not camp_states.has(direction):
		return
	var data: Dictionary = camp_states[direction]
	if bool(data.get("activated", false)) or bool(data.get("cleared", false)):
		return
	data["activated"] = true
	data["remaining"] = 9
	camp_states[direction] = data
	var center: Vector2 = data["position"]
	for index in 9:
		var angle := TAU * float(index) / 9.0
		var distance := 84.0 if index == 8 else 145.0 + float(index % 2) * 48.0
		var spawn_position := center + Vector2.from_angle(angle) * distance
		if not world.is_walkable(spawn_position):
			spawn_position = world.find_walkable_near(center, rng, 70.0, 230.0)
		# 시련 캠프는 플레이어가 스스로 걸어 들어가는 도전 콘텐츠이고 정예는 behavior 4를
		# 강제하므로 낮 선공몹 게이트를 건너뛴다(allow_aggro_override=true).
		var trial_enemy := _spawn_enemy_instance(spawn_position, 4 if index == 8 else 1 + index % 4, "", false, direction, index == 8, "", true)
		if is_instance_valid(trial_enemy):
			trial_enemy.set_night_raid(true)
	var names := {"north":"북방", "east":"동방", "south":"남방", "west":"서방"}
	_show_banner("%s 시련 캠프 · 9마리를 전멸시키세요" % names.get(direction, direction), GamePalette.MAGENTA, 3.2)
	spawn_burst(center, GamePalette.MAGENTA, 30, 290.0, 0.8)
	play_sound("night", -3.0)

func _trial_enemy_defeated(direction: String) -> void:
	if not camp_states.has(direction):
		return
	var data: Dictionary = camp_states[direction]
	if bool(data.get("cleared", false)):
		return
	data["remaining"] = maxi(0, int(data.get("remaining", 0)) - 1)
	if int(data["remaining"]) > 0:
		camp_states[direction] = data
		if int(data["remaining"]) <= 3:
			show_world_text(player.global_position - Vector2(0.0, 70.0), "시련 마물 %d 남음" % int(data["remaining"]), GamePalette.MAGENTA, 16)
		return
	data["cleared"] = true
	camp_states[direction] = data
	player.add_trophy_orb(direction)
	var cleared := {}
	for key: String in camp_states:
		cleared[key] = bool(camp_states[key].get("cleared", false))
	world.set_cleared_trial_camps(cleared)
	var orb_colors := {"north":GamePalette.CYAN, "east":GamePalette.YELLOW, "south":GamePalette.RED, "west":GamePalette.MAGENTA}
	var orb_color: Color = orb_colors.get(direction, GamePalette.TEXT)
	spawn_burst(player.global_position, orb_color, 42, 340.0, 1.0)
	_show_banner("시련 전멸 · %s 구슬이 당신의 주위를 돌기 시작합니다 (%d/4)" % [direction.to_upper(), player.trophy_orbs.size()], orb_color, 4.0)
	play_sound("choice", 0.0)
	_update_hud()

func perform_character_attack(attacker: Node2D) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(attacker):
		return
	var facing: Vector2 = attacker.current_attack_direction.normalized()
	if facing == Vector2.ZERO:
		facing = Vector2.DOWN
	match attacker.character_id:
		"archer": _perform_archer_attack(attacker, facing)
		"mage": _perform_mage_attack(attacker, facing)
		_: _perform_sword_attack(attacker, facing)

func on_factory_cycle_started(actor: Node2D, deck: FactoryDeck, is_boss_cycle: bool) -> void:
	if is_boss_cycle or not is_instance_valid(actor):
		return
	var effects := deck.global_effects()
	var heal_amount := float(effects.get("heal_cycle", 0.0))
	if heal_amount > 0.0 and actor.has_method("heal"):
		actor.heal(heal_amount)
	var shield_chance := clampf(float(effects.get("shield_cycle_chance", 0.0)), 0.0, 0.9)
	if rng.randf() < shield_chance and actor.has_method("grant_cycle_shield"):
		actor.grant_cycle_shield(1)

func on_cycle_card_started(actor: Node2D, card: Dictionary, is_boss_cycle: bool) -> void:
	var shield_amount := int(card.get("shield", 0))
	if shield_amount <= 0:
		return
	if is_boss_cycle:
		if is_instance_valid(actor) and actor.get("shield") != null:
			actor.max_shield = maxf(actor.max_shield, actor.max_health * 0.18)
			actor.shield = minf(actor.max_shield, actor.shield + actor.max_health * 0.04 * shield_amount)
	elif actor.has_method("grant_cycle_shield"):
		actor.grant_cycle_shield(shield_amount)

func apply_cycle_melee(actor: Node2D, card: Dictionary, hit_ids: Dictionary, is_boss_cycle: bool, facing: Vector2) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(actor):
		return
	var range_value := float(card.get("range", 130.0))
	var arc := float(card.get("arc", 1.8))
	if is_boss_cycle:
		if not is_instance_valid(player) or hit_ids.has(player.get_instance_id()):
			return
		var offset := player.global_position - actor.global_position
		var distance := offset.length()
		var direction := offset.normalized() if distance > 0.01 else facing
		if distance <= range_value + 18.0 and (arc >= TAU - 0.1 or facing.dot(direction) >= cos(arc * 0.5)):
			hit_ids[player.get_instance_id()] = true
			player.take_damage(_cycle_damage_value(actor, card, true), actor.global_position)
			spawn_burst(player.global_position, GamePalette.RED, 8, 120.0, 0.22)
		return
	for target: Node in query_enemies(actor.global_position, range_value + 72.0):
		if not is_instance_valid(target) or target.dead or hit_ids.has(target.get_instance_id()):
			continue
		var offset_to_target: Vector2 = target.global_position - actor.global_position
		var distance := offset_to_target.length()
		var to_target := offset_to_target.normalized() if distance > 0.01 else facing
		var in_cone := distance <= range_value + float(target.radius) and (arc >= TAU - 0.1 or facing.dot(to_target) >= cos(arc * 0.5))
		var forward := offset_to_target.dot(facing)
		var lateral := absf(offset_to_target.cross(facing))
		var swept := forward >= 0.0 and forward <= range_value + float(target.radius) and lateral <= 28.0 + float(target.radius)
		if in_cone or swept:
			hit_ids[target.get_instance_id()] = true
			var damage_value := _cycle_damage_value(actor, card, false)
			target.take_damage(damage_value, actor.global_position)
			_apply_card_status_to_enemy(target, card, actor.global_position)
			var lifesteal := float(card.get("lifesteal", 0.0))
			if lifesteal > 0.0:
				player.heal(damage_value * lifesteal)
			var impact_color := Color(String(card.get("color", "f4d35e")))
			spawn_burst(target.global_position, impact_color, 10 if bool(card.get("heavy", false)) else 7, 145.0 if bool(card.get("heavy", false)) else 110.0, 0.28)

func trigger_cycle_card_pulse(actor: Node2D, card: Dictionary, is_boss_cycle: bool, pulse_index: int, action_origin: Vector2) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(actor):
		return
	if is_boss_cycle:
		_trigger_boss_cycle_pulse(actor, card, action_origin)
		return
	var kind := String(card.get("kind", "area"))
	var direction: Vector2 = actor.last_move_direction.normalized()
	if direction == Vector2.ZERO:
		direction = Vector2.DOWN
	var damage_value := _cycle_damage_value(actor, card, false)
	var card_color := Color(String(card.get("color", "f4d35e")))
	match kind:
		"projectile":
			var projectile_count := maxi(1, int(card.get("projectiles", 1)))
			for index in projectile_count:
				var spread_step := float(card.get("spread", 0.18))
				var spread := (float(index) - float(projectile_count - 1) * 0.5) * spread_step
				spawn_player_projectile(actor.global_position + direction * 24.0, direction.rotated(spread), damage_value, 650.0, float(card.get("homing", 0.0)), int(card.get("ricochet", 0)), int(card.get("pierce", 0)), card_color, "blade", float(card.get("range", 520.0)))
			spawn_attack_effect(actor.global_position, "magic", card_color, direction, 86.0, 0.26)
			spawn_burst(actor.global_position + direction * 24.0, card_color, 8 + mini(projectile_count, 5), 120.0, 0.26)
		"chain":
			var excluded := {}
			var chain_origin := actor.global_position
			for _bounce in maxi(1, int(card.get("ricochet", 3))):
				var target := find_nearest_enemy(chain_origin, excluded, float(card.get("range", 410.0)))
				if not is_instance_valid(target): break
				excluded[target.get_instance_id()] = true
				target.take_damage(damage_value, chain_origin)
				_apply_card_status_to_enemy(target, card, chain_origin)
				spawn_attack_effect(target.global_position, "magic", card_color, Vector2.UP, 65.0, 0.2)
				spawn_burst(target.global_position, card_color, 9, 135.0, 0.26)
				chain_origin = target.global_position
		"area", "orbit", "ground":
			var center := cycle_pulse_center(actor, card, pulse_index, action_origin)
			var radius := cycle_pulse_radius(card)
			for target: Node in query_enemies(center, radius):
				if is_instance_valid(target) and not target.dead:
					target.take_damage(damage_value, center)
					_apply_card_status_to_enemy(target, card, center)
			var burst_count := 18 if bool(card.get("heavy", false)) else 12 if kind == "ground" else 9
			spawn_burst(center, card_color, burst_count, 180.0 if bool(card.get("heavy", false)) else 120.0, 0.38)
			# "magic" 링은 진행 방향으로 0.55×size 밀려 그려지므로 중심 판정형 광역에는 쓰지 않는다.
			# 광역 링과 룬은 CycleSkillEffect가 같은 center/radius로 직접 그린다.
		"shield":
			var shield_radius := float(card.get("range", 0.0))
			if damage_value > 0.0 and shield_radius > 0.0:
				for target: Node in query_enemies(actor.global_position, shield_radius):
					if is_instance_valid(target) and not target.dead:
						target.take_damage(damage_value, actor.global_position)
						_apply_card_status_to_enemy(target, card, actor.global_position)
			spawn_burst(actor.global_position, card_color, 18, 145.0, 0.42)
	var heal_amount := float(card.get("heal", 0.0))
	if heal_amount > 0.0 and pulse_index == 0 and actor.has_method("heal"):
		actor.heal(heal_amount)
		show_world_text(actor.global_position - Vector2(0.0, 54.0), "+%.0f 회복" % heal_amount, GamePalette.GREEN, 16)
	if bool(card.get("heavy", false)) and pulse_index == 0:
		shake_camera(7.0, 0.22)
	play_sound("shoot", -10.0)

# 장판/광역 타격의 중심과 반지름은 여기 한 곳에서만 계산합니다.
# CycleSkillEffect가 같은 값을 그리기에 사용하므로 이펙트 위치와 피해 위치가 어긋날 수 없습니다.
func cycle_pulse_center(actor: Node2D, card: Dictionary, pulse_index: int, action_origin: Vector2) -> Vector2:
	var kind := String(card.get("kind", "area"))
	var center := action_origin
	if kind != "ground" and is_instance_valid(actor):
		center = actor.global_position
	if not bool(card.get("random_impacts", false)):
		return center
	var radius := float(card.get("range", 140.0))
	var hit_count := maxi(1, int(card.get("hits", 1)))
	var angle := TAU * fmod(float(pulse_index) * 0.6180339, 1.0)
	var distance_ratio := 0.2 + 0.62 * float((pulse_index * 7) % hit_count) / float(hit_count)
	return center + Vector2.from_angle(angle) * radius * distance_ratio

func cycle_pulse_radius(card: Dictionary) -> float:
	var radius := float(card.get("range", 140.0))
	if bool(card.get("random_impacts", false)):
		return maxf(64.0, radius * 0.42)
	return radius

func _apply_card_status_to_enemy(target: Node, card: Dictionary, center: Vector2) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(target):
		return
	var reaction := DealCardLibrary.knockback_profile(card)
	if target.has_method("apply_hit_reaction"):
		target.apply_hit_reaction(center, float(reaction.get("force", 0.0)), float(reaction.get("stun", 0.0)))
	if bool(target.get("is_boss")):
		return
	var slow_strength := float(card.get("slow", 0.0))
	if slow_strength > 0.0 and target.has_method("apply_cycle_slow"):
		target.apply_cycle_slow(slow_strength, 1.35)
	var pull_strength := float(card.get("pull", 0.0))
	if pull_strength > 0.0:
		var pulled_position: Vector2 = target.global_position.lerp(center, clampf(pull_strength, 0.0, 0.42))
		if can_enemy_stand(pulled_position):
			target.global_position = pulled_position

func _trigger_boss_cycle_pulse(actor: Node2D, card: Dictionary, action_origin: Vector2) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(player):
		return
	var kind := String(card.get("kind", "melee"))
	var direction := actor.global_position.direction_to(player.global_position)
	var damage_value := _cycle_damage_value(actor, card, true)
	var range_value := float(card.get("range", 150.0))
	match kind:
		"projectile", "chain":
			var count := clampi(int(card.get("projectiles", 1)) + int(card.get("ricochet", 0)) / 2, 1, 7)
			for index in count:
				var spread := (float(index) - float(count - 1) * 0.5) * 0.22
				spawn_enemy_bullet(actor.global_position + direction * 58.0, direction.rotated(spread), kind == "chain", damage_value, 235.0)
		"area", "orbit":
			if actor.global_position.distance_to(player.global_position) <= range_value:
				player.take_damage(damage_value, actor.global_position)
			spawn_burst(actor.global_position, GamePalette.RED, 10, 100.0, 0.25)
		"ground":
			if action_origin.distance_to(player.global_position) <= range_value:
				player.take_damage(damage_value, action_origin)
			spawn_burst(action_origin, GamePalette.RED, 12, 120.0, 0.28)
		"shield":
			spawn_burst(actor.global_position, GamePalette.BLUE, 12, 95.0, 0.28)

func _cycle_damage_value(actor: Node2D, card: Dictionary, is_boss_cycle: bool) -> float:
	if is_boss_cycle:
		return maxf(3.0, 2.4 + float(card.get("damage", 1.0)) * (2.0 + float(cycle_number) * 0.18))
	var base_damage := player.damage * float(card.get("damage", 1.0))
	var crit_chance := player.crit_chance + float(card.get("crit", 0.0))
	return base_damage * (player.crit_multiplier if rng.randf() < crit_chance else 1.0)

func _perform_sword_attack(attacker: Node2D, facing: Vector2) -> void:
	var attack_count: int = maxi(attacker.projectile_count, 1)
	var already_hit: Dictionary = {}
	var slash_color := GamePalette.YELLOW
	if not attacker.advancement_branch_id.is_empty():
		slash_color = Color(String(ClassLibrary.by_id(attacker.advancement_branch_id).get("color", "f4d35e")))
	spawn_attack_effect(attacker.global_position, "slash", slash_color, facing, attacker.attack_range, 0.18)
	for strike in attack_count:
		var offset := float(strike) - float(attack_count - 1) * 0.5
		var strike_direction := facing.rotated(offset * 0.22)
		for target: Node in query_enemies(attacker.global_position, attacker.attack_range + 70.0):
			if not is_instance_valid(target) or target.dead:
				continue
			var offset_to_target: Vector2 = target.global_position - attacker.global_position
			var distance := offset_to_target.length()
			var to_target := offset_to_target.normalized() if distance > 0.01 else strike_direction
			var in_visible_cone: bool = distance <= float(attacker.attack_range) + float(target.radius) and strike_direction.dot(to_target) >= cos(float(attacker.attack_arc) * 0.5)
			# Target radius padding prevents the blade from visually clipping an enemy without a hit.
			var forward := offset_to_target.dot(strike_direction)
			var lateral := absf(offset_to_target.cross(strike_direction))
			var swept_blade: bool = forward >= 0.0 and forward <= float(attacker.attack_range) + float(target.radius) and lateral <= 26.0 + float(target.radius)
			if in_visible_cone or swept_blade:
				var hit_damage := _roll_player_damage(attacker)
				target.take_damage(hit_damage, attacker.global_position, 185.0, 0.08)
				already_hit[target.get_instance_id()] = true
				spawn_burst(target.global_position, GamePalette.YELLOW, 7, 110.0, 0.22)
	if attacker.ricochet_count > 0 and not already_hit.is_empty():
		_chain_damage(attacker, already_hit)
	play_sound("shoot", -8.0)

func process_orbit_blades(attacker: Node2D) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(attacker) or attacker.orbit_blade_count <= 0:
		return
	var hits := 0
	for target: Node in query_enemies(attacker.global_position, 115.0):
		if not is_instance_valid(target) or target.dead:
			continue
		var distance := attacker.global_position.distance_to(target.global_position)
		if distance >= 30.0 and distance <= 82.0 + target.radius:
			target.take_damage(attacker.damage * (0.28 + attacker.orbit_blade_count * 0.08), attacker.global_position, 82.0, 0.04)
			spawn_burst(target.global_position, GamePalette.CYAN, 5, 90.0, 0.2)
			hits += 1
			if hits >= attacker.orbit_blade_count:
				break

func _perform_archer_attack(attacker: Node2D, facing: Vector2) -> void:
	var count: int = maxi(attacker.projectile_count, 1)
	for index in count:
		var offset := float(index) - float(count - 1) * 0.5
		var direction := facing.rotated(offset * attacker.spread_angle)
		spawn_player_projectile(
			attacker.global_position + direction * 25.0,
			direction,
			_roll_player_damage(attacker),
			attacker.projectile_speed,
			0.0,
			attacker.ricochet_count,
			attacker.pierce_count,
			GamePalette.YELLOW,
			"arrow"
		)
	spawn_attack_effect(attacker.global_position, "bow", GamePalette.GREEN, facing, 70.0, 0.12)
	play_sound("shoot", -10.0)

func _perform_mage_attack(attacker: Node2D, facing: Vector2) -> void:
	var count: int = maxi(attacker.projectile_count, 1)
	for index in count:
		var offset := float(index) - float(count - 1) * 0.5
		var direction := facing.rotated(offset * 0.32)
		var center: Vector2 = attacker.global_position + direction * attacker.attack_range * 0.58
		spawn_attack_effect(attacker.global_position, "magic", GamePalette.MAGENTA, direction, attacker.attack_range, 0.32)
		for target: Node in query_enemies(center, attacker.attack_range * 0.7):
			if is_instance_valid(target) and not target.dead and target.global_position.distance_to(center) < attacker.attack_range * 0.58 + target.radius:
				target.take_damage(_roll_player_damage(attacker), center, 125.0, 0.065)
				spawn_burst(target.global_position, GamePalette.MAGENTA, 8, 125.0, 0.28)
	play_sound("shoot", -7.0)

func _roll_player_damage(attacker: Node) -> float:
	return attacker.damage * (attacker.crit_multiplier if rng.randf() < attacker.crit_chance else 1.0)

func _chain_damage(attacker: Node, excluded: Dictionary) -> void:
	var origin: Vector2 = attacker.global_position
	for _bounce in attacker.ricochet_count:
		var next_target := find_nearest_enemy(origin, excluded, 270.0)
		if not is_instance_valid(next_target):
			break
		excluded[next_target.get_instance_id()] = true
		next_target.take_damage(attacker.damage * 0.62, origin, 70.0, 0.04)
		spawn_burst(next_target.global_position, GamePalette.CYAN, 8, 125.0, 0.25)
		origin = next_target.global_position

func spawn_player_projectile(world_position: Vector2, direction: Vector2, damage: float, speed: float, homing: float, ricochets: int, pierces: int, color: Color, visual_kind: String = "arrow", travel_range: float = 0.0) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(gameplay_root):
		return
	var projectile := PROJECTILE_SCRIPT.new()
	projectile.setup(self, direction, damage, speed, homing, ricochets, pierces, color, visual_kind, travel_range)
	projectile.position = world_position
	gameplay_root.add_child(projectile)

func spawn_enemy_bullet(world_position: Vector2, direction: Vector2, homing: bool, damage: float, speed: float) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(gameplay_root) or not is_instance_valid(player):
		return
	var bullet := ENEMY_BULLET_SCRIPT.new()
	bullet.setup(self, player, direction, homing, damage, speed)
	bullet.position = world_position
