	gameplay_root.add_child(bullet)

func spawn_attack_effect(world_position: Vector2, kind: String, color: Color, direction: Vector2, size: float, duration: float = 0.18) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(gameplay_root) or active_effect_nodes >= MAX_TRANSIENT_EFFECTS:
		return
	var effect := ATTACK_EFFECT_SCRIPT.new()
	effect.setup(kind, color, direction, size, duration)
	effect.position = world_position
	effect.tree_exiting.connect(_on_transient_effect_exiting)
	active_effect_nodes += 1
	gameplay_root.add_child(effect)

func register_enemy(enemy: Node) -> void:
	if not active_enemies.has(enemy):
		active_enemies.append(enemy)
	_add_enemy_to_spatial(enemy)

func unregister_enemy(enemy: Node) -> void:
	active_enemies.erase(enemy)

func _enemy_cell(point: Vector2) -> Vector2i:
	return Vector2i(floori(point.x / ENEMY_SPATIAL_CELL), floori(point.y / ENEMY_SPATIAL_CELL))

func _add_enemy_to_spatial(enemy: Node) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(enemy):
		return
	var cell := _enemy_cell(enemy.global_position)
	if not enemy_spatial.has(cell):
		enemy_spatial[cell] = []
	var bucket: Array = enemy_spatial[cell]
	if not bucket.has(enemy):
		bucket.append(enemy)
		enemy_spatial[cell] = bucket

func _rebuild_enemy_spatial() -> void:
	enemy_spatial.clear()
	for index in range(active_enemies.size() - 1, -1, -1):
		var enemy := active_enemies[index]
		if not is_instance_valid(enemy):
			active_enemies.remove_at(index)
			continue
		if enemy.dead:
			continue
		_add_enemy_to_spatial(enemy)

func query_enemies(center: Vector2, radius: float) -> Array[Node]:
	var result: Array[Node] = []
	if enemy_spatial.is_empty() and not active_enemies.is_empty():
		_rebuild_enemy_spatial()
	var minimum_cell := _enemy_cell(center - Vector2(radius, radius))
	var maximum_cell := _enemy_cell(center + Vector2(radius, radius))
	for cell_x in range(minimum_cell.x, maximum_cell.x + 1):
		for cell_y in range(minimum_cell.y, maximum_cell.y + 1):
			var cell := Vector2i(cell_x, cell_y)
			if not enemy_spatial.has(cell):
				continue
			for candidate in enemy_spatial[cell]:
				if not is_instance_valid(candidate):
					continue
				var enemy: Node = candidate
				var reach := radius + float(enemy.radius)
				if not enemy.dead and center.distance_squared_to(enemy.global_position) <= reach * reach:
					result.append(enemy)
	return result

func find_nearest_enemy(origin: Vector2, excluded: Dictionary = {}, maximum_distance: float = INF) -> Node2D:
	var nearest: Node2D
	var nearest_distance := maximum_distance * maximum_distance
	var candidates: Array[Node] = active_enemies if is_inf(maximum_distance) else query_enemies(origin, maximum_distance)
	for candidate: Node in candidates:
		if not is_instance_valid(candidate) or candidate.dead:
			continue
		if excluded.has(candidate.get_instance_id()):
			continue
		var distance := origin.distance_squared_to(candidate.global_position)
		if distance < nearest_distance:
			nearest_distance = distance
			nearest = candidate
	return nearest

func alert_same_species(species: String, origin: Vector2) -> void:
	for candidate: Node in query_enemies(origin, 780.0):
		if is_instance_valid(candidate) and not candidate.is_boss and candidate.species == species and candidate.global_position.distance_to(origin) <= 780.0:
			candidate.receive_pack_alert()

func collect_xp(value: int) -> void:
	if state not in ["playing", "boss"]:
		return
	var factory_xp := 0.0 if factory == null else float(factory.global_effects().get("xp_all", 0.0))
	var adjusted_xp := float(value) * player.xp_multiplier * maxf(0.1, 1.0 + factory_xp) + xp_fraction
	var gained_xp := floori(adjusted_xp)
	xp_fraction = adjusted_xp - float(gained_xp)
	experience += gained_xp
	collect_sound_counter += 1
	if collect_sound_counter % 3 == 0:
		play_sound("collect", -10.0)
	_update_hud()
	if state == "playing" and experience >= xp_target:
		call_deferred("_show_skill_choice", "level")

func enemy_defeated(enemy: Node) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(enemy):
		return
	var enemy_position: Vector2 = enemy.global_position
	var enemy_xp: int = enemy.xp_value
	var enemy_gold: int = enemy.gold_value
	var was_boss: bool = enemy.is_boss
	if not was_boss:
		kills += 1
		gold += int(ceil(float(enemy_gold) * player.gold_multiplier))
		if player.life_on_kill > 0.0:
			player.heal(player.life_on_kill)
	spawn_burst(enemy_position, GamePalette.RED if not was_boss else GamePalette.YELLOW, 12 if not was_boss else 48, 180.0 if not was_boss else 350.0, 0.4 if not was_boss else 1.1)
	if not was_boss and enemy_xp > 0:
		_spawn_or_merge_xp_orb(enemy_position, enemy_xp)
	if was_boss:
		call_deferred("_finish_run", true)
		return
	if not String(enemy.camp_id).is_empty():
		_trial_enemy_defeated(String(enemy.camp_id))
	if is_instance_valid(player) and player.holy_pulse_enabled and kills > 0 and kills % 8 == 0 and not hotfix_burst_running:
		_trigger_holy_pulse(enemy_position)
	if is_instance_valid(player) and player.thunder_rank > 0 and kills % maxi(2, 6 - player.thunder_rank) == 0:
		_trigger_thunder(enemy_position)
	if kills % 3 == 0:
		play_sound("hit", -14.0)

func _spawn_or_merge_xp_orb(world_position: Vector2, value: int) -> void:
	var orbs: Array[Node] = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("xp_orbs")
	if orbs.size() >= MAX_XP_ORBS:
		var nearest_orb: Node
		var nearest_distance := INF
		for orb: Node in orbs:
			if not is_instance_valid(orb):
				continue
			var distance := world_position.distance_squared_to(orb.global_position)
			if distance < nearest_distance:
				nearest_distance = distance
				nearest_orb = orb
		if is_instance_valid(nearest_orb) and nearest_orb.has_method("add_value"):
			nearest_orb.add_value(value)
			return
	var orb := XP_ORB_SCRIPT.new()
	orb.setup(self, player, value)
	orb.position = world_position
	gameplay_root.add_child(orb)

func _trigger_holy_pulse(origin: Vector2) -> void:
	hotfix_burst_running = true
	spawn_burst(origin, GamePalette.YELLOW, 38, 315.0, 0.7)
	show_world_text(origin - Vector2(0.0, 40.0), "성스러운 파동", GamePalette.YELLOW, 21)
	for target: Node in query_enemies(origin, 275.0):
		if is_instance_valid(target) and not target.is_boss and origin.distance_to(target.global_position) < 275.0:
			target.take_damage(player.damage * 1.65, origin, 255.0, 0.13)
	hotfix_burst_running = false

func _trigger_thunder(origin: Vector2) -> void:
	var excluded := {}
	var lightning_origin := origin
	for _strike in mini(2 + player.thunder_rank, 6):
		var target := find_nearest_enemy(lightning_origin, excluded, 420.0)
		if not is_instance_valid(target):
			break
		excluded[target.get_instance_id()] = true
		spawn_attack_effect(target.global_position, "magic", GamePalette.CYAN, Vector2.UP, 100.0, 0.28)
		spawn_burst(target.global_position, GamePalette.CYAN, 12, 155.0, 0.32)
		target.take_damage(player.damage * (0.72 + player.thunder_rank * 0.12), lightning_origin, 66.0, 0.04)
		lightning_origin = target.global_position
	play_sound("shoot", -4.0)

func spawn_split_enemies(origin: Vector2, count: int) -> void:
	for index in count:
		var offset := Vector2.from_angle(TAU * float(index) / float(count) + randf()) * 34.0
		_spawn_enemy_instance(origin + offset, 1, "", true)
	spawn_burst(origin, GamePalette.MAGENTA, 16, 195.0, 0.5)

func spawn_boss_minions(origin: Vector2, count: int) -> void:
	for index in count:
		var angle := TAU * float(index) / float(count) + fmod(elapsed_time * 0.71, TAU)
		# 마왕 전투 하수인은 낮 선공몹 게이트 대상이 아니다(보스전 전용 강제 스폰).
		var enemy := _spawn_enemy_instance(origin + Vector2.from_angle(angle) * 125.0, 4, "", false, "", false, "", true)
		if is_instance_valid(enemy):
			enemy.set_night_raid(true)
	spawn_burst(origin, GamePalette.MAGENTA, 18, 160.0, 0.45)

func get_player_speed_multiplier(point: Vector2) -> float:
	if inside_castle:
		return 1.0
	var item_speed := 0.0 if factory == null else float(factory.global_effects().get("move_speed", 0.0))
	for enemy: Node in query_enemies(point, 250.0):
		if is_instance_valid(enemy) and enemy.has_method("slows_player") and enemy.slows_player(point):
			return (0.6 if enemy.is_boss else 0.74) * maxf(0.5, 1.0 + item_speed)
	return maxf(0.5, 1.0 + item_speed)

func _show_skill_choice(source: String = "level") -> void:
	if state != "playing":
		return
	choice_source = source
	state = "choice"
	get_tree().paused = true
	current_pair = DealCardLibrary.random_two(rng)
	_reset_choice_focus()
	_clear_overlay()
	overlay = Control.new()
	overlay.name = "SkillChoice"
	overlay.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	overlay.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	ui_root.add_child(overlay)
	var dim := ColorRect.new()
	dim.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	dim.color = Color(0.03, 0.035, 0.08, 0.91)
	overlay.add_child(dim)
	var panel := Panel.new()
	panel.position = CHOICE_MODAL_RECT.position
	panel.size = CHOICE_MODAL_RECT.size
	panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(UI_MODAL_BG, UI_EDGE, 0, UI_BORDER_MODAL))
	overlay.add_child(panel)
	var eyebrow := _label("보물상자의 기억" if source == "chest" else "레벨 업 · 운명의 갈림길", UI_LABEL_SIZE, GamePalette.YELLOW)
	eyebrow.position = Vector2(0.0, 20.0)
	eyebrow.size = Vector2(CHOICE_MODAL_RECT.size.x, 20.0)
	eyebrow.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	panel.add_child(eyebrow)
	# 보물상자에서 열린 선택은 레벨업이 아니므로 제목도 함께 바뀌어야 합니다.
	var heading := _label("봉인된 기술 중 하나를 고르세요" if source == "chest" else "이번 레벨의 스킬 카드를 고르세요", UI_TITLE_SIZE, GamePalette.TEXT)
	heading.position = Vector2(0.0, 42.0)
	heading.size = Vector2(CHOICE_MODAL_RECT.size.x, 38.0)
	heading.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	panel.add_child(heading)
	var warning := _label("지속시간 = 카드가 행동하는 시간   ·   RELOAD = 레일 한 바퀴 뒤 합산되는 대기시간", UI_LABEL_SIZE, GamePalette.MUTED)
	warning.position = Vector2(0.0, 86.0)
	warning.size = Vector2(CHOICE_MODAL_RECT.size.x, 24.0)
	warning.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	panel.add_child(warning)
	var left := _deal_choice_button(current_pair[0])
	left.position = Vector2(55.0, 120.0)
	panel.add_child(left)
	_register_choice_button(left, "skill", _choose_skill.bind(current_pair[0], current_pair[1]))
	var right := _deal_choice_button(current_pair[1])
	right.position = Vector2(625.0, 120.0)
	panel.add_child(right)
	_register_choice_button(right, "skill", _choose_skill.bind(current_pair[1], current_pair[0]))
	var next_piece := "연결 다리" if not factory.trailing_bridge else "새 스킬 칸"
	var build_text := "딜싸이클 업그레이드   ·   %s 건설\n두 카드는 마왕에게 전달   ·   %s" % [next_piece, _factory_shape_text()]
	var build := _button(build_text, GamePalette.ORANGE, Vector2(520.0, 58.0))
	build.position = Vector2(335.0, 396.0)
	build.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", UI_BODY_SIZE)
	build.disabled = not factory.can_build()
	panel.add_child(build)
	_register_choice_button(build, "cancel", _choose_factory_growth)
	var footer := _label("← 왼쪽 스킬   ·   → 오른쪽 스킬   ·   ↓ 딜싸이클 업그레이드   ·   ↑ 스킬 카드로 복귀   ·   SPACE 결정", UI_LABEL_SIZE, GamePalette.CYAN)
	footer.position = Vector2(0.0, 462.0)
	footer.size = Vector2(CHOICE_MODAL_RECT.size.x, 22.0)
	footer.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	panel.add_child(footer)
	_animate_modal(panel, Vector2(0.0, 18.0))
	_set_choice_index(0)
	play_sound("choice", -2.0)

# 2지선다 계열 화면(choice / advancement_choice / item_choice)의 단일 포커스 모델.
# 포커스 상태는 choice_selected_index 하나뿐이며 키보드·마우스·자동 테스트가 모두 이 경로만 사용합니다.
# Godot 내장 Control 포커스 이동(ui_left/ui_right/ui_accept)이 두 번째 선택자로 끼어들지 않도록
# 등록된 버튼은 focus_mode를 FOCUS_NONE으로 강제합니다.
func _reset_choice_focus() -> void:
	choice_buttons.clear()
	choice_selected_index = 0
	choice_last_card_index = 0
	# 직전 화면의 Control이 키 포커스를 들고 있으면 Godot 내장 포커스 이동이
	# 두 번째 선택자로 되살아나므로 2지선다에 들어가기 전에 반드시 풀어줍니다.
	if is_instance_valid(ui_root):
		var viewport := ui_root.get_viewport()
		if is_instance_valid(viewport):
			var focus_owner := viewport.gui_get_focus_owner()
			if is_instance_valid(focus_owner):
				focus_owner.release_focus()

func _register_choice_button(button: Button, role: String, confirm: Callable) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(button):
		return
	button.focus_mode = Control.FOCUS_NONE
	button.set_meta("choice_role", role)
	button.set_meta("choice_confirm", confirm)
	var index := choice_buttons.size()
	button.mouse_entered.connect(_set_choice_index.bind(index))
	button.pressed.connect(_confirm_choice_index.bind(index))
	choice_buttons.append(button)

func _choice_extra_index() -> int:
	for index in choice_buttons.size():
		if is_instance_valid(choice_buttons[index]) and String(choice_buttons[index].get_meta("choice_role", "")) == "cancel":
			return index
	return choice_selected_index

func _handle_choice_keyboard(key_event: InputEventKey) -> void:
	if choice_buttons.is_empty():
		return
	if key_event.keycode in [KEY_LEFT, KEY_A]:
		_set_choice_index(0)
	elif key_event.keycode in [KEY_RIGHT, KEY_D]:
		_set_choice_index(1)
	elif key_event.keycode in [KEY_UP, KEY_W]:
		_set_choice_index(choice_last_card_index)
	elif key_event.keycode in [KEY_DOWN, KEY_S]:
		_set_choice_index(_choice_extra_index())
	elif key_event.keycode in [KEY_SPACE, KEY_ENTER]:
		_confirm_choice_index(choice_selected_index)

func _set_choice_index(index: int) -> void:
	if choice_buttons.is_empty():
		return
	var target := clampi(index, 0, choice_buttons.size() - 1)
	var button := choice_buttons[target]
	if not is_instance_valid(button) or button.disabled:
		return
	choice_selected_index = target
	if String(button.get_meta("choice_role", "")) != "cancel":
		choice_last_card_index = target
	_refresh_choice_highlight()

func _refresh_choice_highlight() -> void:
	for button_index in choice_buttons.size():
		var button := choice_buttons[button_index]
		if not is_instance_valid(button):
			continue
		var accent: Color = button.get_meta("choice_color", GamePalette.STONE_LIGHT)
		var focused := button_index == choice_selected_index
		button.modulate = Color.WHITE if focused else Color(0.6, 0.62, 0.68, 0.86)
		button.add_theme_stylebox_override("normal", _panel_style(accent.darkened(0.7 if focused else 0.86), Color.WHITE if focused else accent.darkened(0.52), 0, UI_BORDER_FOCUS if focused else UI_BORDER_FRAME))
		button.add_theme_stylebox_override("hover", _panel_style(accent.darkened(0.64 if focused else 0.8), Color.WHITE if focused else accent.darkened(0.34), 0, UI_BORDER_FOCUS if focused else UI_BORDER_FRAME))

func _choice_highlight_count() -> int:
	var count := 0
	for button: Button in choice_buttons:
		if is_instance_valid(button) and button.modulate.is_equal_approx(Color.WHITE):
			count += 1
	return count

func _confirm_choice_index(index: int) -> void:
	if choice_buttons.is_empty():
		return
	var target := clampi(index, 0, choice_buttons.size() - 1)
	var button := choice_buttons[target]
	if not is_instance_valid(button) or button.disabled:
		return
	choice_selected_index = target
	_refresh_choice_highlight()
	var confirm = button.get_meta("choice_confirm", Callable())
	if confirm is Callable and (confirm as Callable).is_valid():
		(confirm as Callable).call()

func _choose_skill(selected: Dictionary, rejected: Dictionary) -> void:
	if state != "choice":
		return
	selected_skills.append(selected["id"])
	rejected_skills.append(rejected["id"])
	if choice_source == "level":
		experience = maxi(0, experience - xp_target)
		level += 1
		xp_target = 7 + level * 5
	pending_boss_toast_cards = [rejected.duplicate(true)]
	_clear_overlay()
	_show_factory_menu("place", DealCardLibrary.instance(String(selected["id"]), 1), "playing")
	play_sound("choice", -2.0)

func _choose_factory_growth() -> void:
	if state != "choice" or factory == null or not factory.can_build() or current_pair.size() != 2:
		return
	for card: Dictionary in current_pair:
		rejected_skills.append(String(card["id"]))
	if choice_source == "level":
		experience = maxi(0, experience - xp_target)
		level += 1
		xp_target = 7 + level * 5
	var result := factory.build_next_piece()
	pending_boss_toast_cards = [current_pair[0].duplicate(true), current_pair[1].duplicate(true)]
	_clear_overlay()
	_show_factory_menu("build", result, "playing")
	play_sound("debt", -2.0)

# 마왕 카드 획득 토스트의 공개 진입점.
# 카드 2장을 한 번에 넘기면(딜싸이클 업그레이드 등) 한 토스트가 두 줄로 함께 알린다.
# 서로 다른 사건이 3초 안에 연달아 발생하면(연속 레벨업 등) 예전에는 먼저 뜬 토스트를
# 즉시 free해서 읽기 전에 사라졌다. 이제 큐에 넣어 순서대로 3초씩 보여준다.
func _show_boss_growth_toast(rejected_value) -> void:
	var cards: Array[Dictionary] = []
	if rejected_value is Dictionary:
		cards.append((rejected_value as Dictionary).duplicate(true))
	elif rejected_value is Array:
		for entry in rejected_value:
			if entry is Dictionary:
				cards.append((entry as Dictionary).duplicate(true))
	if cards.is_empty():
		return
	if is_instance_valid(boss_toast):
		if boss_toast_queue.size() < BOSS_TOAST_QUEUE_MAX:
			boss_toast_queue.append(cards)
		else:
			# 큐가 가득 찰 정도로 몰리면 마지막 대기 항목에 합쳐 한 토스트로 알린다.
			var tail: Array[Dictionary] = boss_toast_queue[boss_toast_queue.size() - 1]
			tail.append_array(cards)
			boss_toast_queue[boss_toast_queue.size() - 1] = tail
		return
	_build_boss_growth_toast(cards)

func _finish_boss_growth_toast(finished_toast: Control) -> void:
	if is_instance_valid(finished_toast):
		finished_toast.queue_free()
	if boss_toast == finished_toast:
		boss_toast = null
	if boss_toast_queue.is_empty():
		return
	var next_cards: Array[Dictionary] = boss_toast_queue.pop_front()
	_build_boss_growth_toast(next_cards)

func _build_boss_growth_toast(cards: Array[Dictionary]) -> void:
	if cards.is_empty() or not is_instance_valid(ui_root):
		return
	if is_instance_valid(boss_toast):
		boss_toast.free()
	boss_toast = Control.new()
	boss_toast.name = "BossGrowthToast"
	boss_toast.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	boss_toast.position = Vector2(940.0, 410.0)
	boss_toast.size = Vector2(315.0, 142.0)
	boss_toast.modulate.a = 0.0
	ui_root.add_child(boss_toast)
	var accent := ColorRect.new()
	accent.position = Vector2(0.0, 8.0)
	accent.size = Vector2(4.0, 126.0)
	accent.color = GamePalette.RED
	accent.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	boss_toast.add_child(accent)
	var portrait := PORTRAIT_SCRIPT.new()
	portrait.position = Vector2(10.0, 18.0)
	portrait.size = Vector2(74.0, 104.0)
	portrait.setup("boss", GamePalette.RED)
	boss_toast.add_child(portrait)
	var laugh := _label(BOSS_QUIPS[rng.randi_range(0, BOSS_QUIPS.size() - 1)], 18, GamePalette.RED.lightened(0.18))
	laugh.position = Vector2(94.0, 10.0)
	laugh.size = Vector2(215.0, 30.0)
	boss_toast.add_child(laugh)
	var detail_lines: Array[String] = []
	for rejected: Dictionary in cards:
		var rejected_id := String(rejected["id"])
		var boss_rank := maxi(rejected_skills.count(rejected_id), boss_items.count(rejected_id))
		detail_lines.append("%s ×%d" % [rejected.get("name", "카드"), boss_rank])
	if cards.size() == 1:
		detail_lines.append(String(cards[0].get("debt_desc", "마왕의 공장이 강해졌습니다.")))
	else:
		detail_lines.append("두 카드가 모두 마왕 공장으로 배송됨")
	var detail := _label("\n".join(detail_lines), 13, GamePalette.TEXT)
	detail.position = Vector2(94.0, 43.0)
	detail.size = Vector2(215.0, 92.0)
	detail.autowrap_mode = TextServer.AUTOWRAP_WORD_SMART
	boss_toast.add_child(detail)
	# 3차 피드백 ⑲: 예전 코드는 대기(tween_interval)와 페이드 아웃을 parallel()로 묶어
	# 실제로는 등장 직후 0.35초 만에 사라지고 남은 3.1초는 투명한 상태로 흘렀다.
	# 이제 등장 → BOSS_TOAST_HOLD(3.0초) 완전 표시 → 페이드 아웃 순서로 확실히 분리한다.
	var tween := boss_toast.create_tween()
	tween.set_pause_mode(Tween.TWEEN_PAUSE_PROCESS)
	tween.set_trans(Tween.TRANS_QUAD).set_ease(Tween.EASE_OUT)
	tween.tween_property(boss_toast, "modulate:a", 1.0, BOSS_TOAST_FADE_IN)
	tween.parallel().tween_property(boss_toast, "position:x", 905.0, BOSS_TOAST_SLIDE_IN)
	tween.tween_interval(BOSS_TOAST_HOLD)
	tween.tween_property(boss_toast, "modulate:a", 0.0, BOSS_TOAST_FADE_OUT)
	tween.parallel().tween_property(boss_toast, "position:x", 930.0, BOSS_TOAST_FADE_OUT)
	tween.tween_callback(_finish_boss_growth_toast.bind(boss_toast))

func _toggle_day_night() -> void:
	phase_elapsed = 0.0
	is_night = not is_night
	if is_night:
		for enemy: Node in active_enemies.duplicate():
			if is_instance_valid(enemy) and not enemy.is_boss:
				enemy.set_night_raid(true)
		for index in _night_raid_burst_count():
			var spawn_position: Vector2 = world.find_walkable_near(player.global_position, rng, 570.0, 790.0)
			_spawn_enemy_instance(spawn_position)
		_show_banner("밤 %d · 마물들이 흉측한 습격체로 변이합니다" % cycle_number, GamePalette.RED, 3.2)
		play_sound("night", -1.0)
	else:
		cycle_number += 1
		for enemy: Node in active_enemies.duplicate():
			if is_instance_valid(enemy) and not enemy.is_boss:
				enemy.set_night_raid(false)
		var unlocked_name := " · 새로운 마물이 출현합니다" if cycle_number <= 5 else ""
		_show_banner("제%d일 · 더 강한 마물 비중 증가%s" % [cycle_number, unlocked_name], GamePalette.YELLOW, 3.0)
	_update_hud()

func _update_world_lighting(delta: float) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(canvas_modulate) or not is_instance_valid(world):
		return
	var target_color := Color("8995c9") if is_night else Color.WHITE
	canvas_modulate.color = canvas_modulate.color.lerp(target_color, clampf(delta * 2.0, 0.0, 1.0))
	var target_night := 1.0 if is_night else 0.0
	world.set_night_amount(lerpf(world.night_amount, target_night, clampf(delta * 2.0, 0.0, 1.0)))

func _refresh_interactable() -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(world) or not is_instance_valid(player):
		return
	current_interactable = world.get_nearest_interactable(player.global_position, 105.0)
	if current_interactable.is_empty():
		interaction_text.visible = false
		return
	interaction_text.visible = true
	match current_interactable["type"]:
		"castle": interaction_text.text = "[ E ]  성에 들어가기 · 랜덤 베이스캠프"
		"demon_castle": interaction_text.text = "[ E ]  마왕에게 도전 · 버린 운명 %d개" % (rejected_skills.size() + boss_items.size())
		"chest": interaction_text.text = "[ E ]  보물상자 열기 · 보상 또는 함정"
		_: interaction_text.visible = false

func _refresh_castle_interactable() -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(castle_interior) or not is_instance_valid(player):
		return
	current_interactable = castle_interior.get_nearest_interactable(player.global_position, 82.0)
	if current_interactable.is_empty():
		interaction_text.visible = false
		return
	interaction_text.visible = true
	match current_interactable["type"]:
		"castle_exit":
			interaction_text.text = "[ E ]  성 밖으로 나가기"
		"castle_npc":
			var service: String = current_interactable["service"]
			var info := _service_info(service)
			# 비용 0은 "무료"라고 말합니다. 그대로 "0 G"를 찍으면 고장처럼 보입니다.
			var service_cost := int(info["cost"])
			var payment := "시련 구슬 4개" if service == "awakening_master" else ("무료" if service_cost <= 0 else "%d G" % service_cost)
			var npc_name := String(info["name"])
			interaction_text.text = "[ E ]  %s%s 대화 · %s" % [npc_name, _particle_wa(npc_name), payment]
		_:
			interaction_text.visible = false

func _interact_with_world() -> void:
	if current_interactable.is_empty():
		return
	match current_interactable["type"]:
		"castle": _enter_castle(current_interactable)
		"castle_exit": _exit_castle()
		"castle_npc": _show_single_npc_service(String(current_interactable["service"]))
		"demon_castle": _challenge_demon_king()
		"chest": _open_chest(current_interactable)

func _open_chest(feature: Dictionary) -> void:
	var feature_id: String = feature["id"]
	var feature_position: Vector2 = feature["position"]
	if opened_features.has(feature_id):
		return
	opened_features[feature_id] = true
	world.set_opened_features(opened_features)
	current_interactable.clear()
	interaction_text.visible = false
	var opening_effect := CHEST_OPEN_EFFECT_SCRIPT.new()
	opening_effect.position = feature_position
	gameplay_root.add_child(opening_effect)
	play_sound("chest", -1.0)
	if not automated_test:
		await get_tree().create_timer(0.34).timeout
	spawn_burst(feature_position, GamePalette.YELLOW, 18, 170.0, 0.45)
	var roll := rng.randi_range(0, 99)
	if roll < 19:
		var reward := rng.randi_range(18, 42)
		gold += reward
		_show_banner("보물상자 · %d G 획득" % reward, GamePalette.YELLOW, 2.3)
	elif roll < 35:
		var xp_reward := rng.randi_range(5, 11)
		_show_banner("보물상자 · 경험의 수정 %d" % xp_reward, GamePalette.CYAN, 2.3)
		collect_xp(xp_reward)
	elif roll < 51:
		_show_banner("보물상자 · 봉인된 기술", GamePalette.MAGENTA, 1.3)
		call_deferred("_show_skill_choice", "chest")
	elif roll < 66:
		call_deferred("_show_item_offer", "treasure")
	elif roll < 75:
		if factory.can_build():
			var build_result := factory.build_next_piece()
			factory_return_state = "playing"
			call_deferred("_show_factory_menu", "build", build_result, "playing")
		else:
			gold += 45
			_show_banner("공장 부품이 45 G로 환불되었습니다", GamePalette.YELLOW, 2.3)
	elif roll < 82:
		var cursed_reward := rng.randi_range(38, 68)
		gold += cursed_reward
		var curse := DealCardLibrary.SKILLS[rng.randi_range(0, DealCardLibrary.SKILLS.size() - 1)]
		rejected_skills.append(curse["id"])
		_show_banner("저주받은 보물 +%d G · 마왕이 %s 획득" % [cursed_reward, curse["name"]], GamePalette.RED, 3.0)
	elif roll < 90:
		_show_banner("함정! 체력 24 피해", GamePalette.RED, 2.2)
		player.take_damage(24.0, feature_position)
	elif roll < 97:
		_show_banner("미믹! 상자가 마물로 변했습니다", GamePalette.RED, 2.4)
		# 미믹은 플레이어가 상자를 열어서 발동시킨 함정이므로 낮 선공몹 게이트를 건너뛴다.
		var mimic := _spawn_enemy_instance(feature_position, 4, "overclock", false, "", false, "", true)
		if is_instance_valid(mimic):
			mimic.set_night_raid(true)
	else:
		_show_banner("빈 상자 · 누군가 먼저 다녀갔습니다", GamePalette.MUTED, 2.3)
	_update_hud()

func _show_item_offer(source: String = "treasure") -> void:
	if state not in ["playing", "castle_interior"]:
		return
	item_return_state = state
	current_item_pair.assign(_roll_item_pair())
	if current_item_pair.size() != 2:
		return
	state = "item_choice"
	get_tree().paused = true
	_clear_overlay()
	_show_item_offer_pair(current_item_pair, source)

func _roll_item_pair() -> Array[Dictionary]:
	var character_id := player.character_id if is_instance_valid(player) else "swordsman"
	var first := ItemLibrary.random_item(rng, "", character_id)
	var second := first
	for _attempt in 16:
		second = ItemLibrary.random_item(rng, "", character_id)
		if String(second.get("id", "")) != String(first.get("id", "")):
			break
	var pair: Array[Dictionary] = [first.duplicate(true), second.duplicate(true)]
	return pair

func _show_item_offer_card(item: Dictionary, source: String) -> void:
	# 구버전 진입점(단일 아이템 제안). 새 규칙에 맞춰 짝을 뽑아 이지선다로 표시합니다.
	var partner := item
	var character_id := player.character_id if is_instance_valid(player) else "swordsman"
	for _attempt in 16:
		partner = ItemLibrary.random_item(rng, "", character_id)
		if String(partner.get("id", "")) != String(item.get("id", "")):
			break
	current_item_pair.assign([item.duplicate(true), partner.duplicate(true)])
	_show_item_offer_pair(current_item_pair, source)

func _show_item_offer_pair(pair: Array[Dictionary], source: String) -> void:
	_reset_choice_focus()
	overlay = Control.new()
	overlay.name = "ItemChoice"
	overlay.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	overlay.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	ui_root.add_child(overlay)
	var dim := ColorRect.new()
	dim.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	dim.color = Color(0.03, 0.035, 0.07, 0.9)
	overlay.add_child(dim)
	var panel := Panel.new()
	panel.position = CHOICE_MODAL_RECT.position
	panel.size = CHOICE_MODAL_RECT.size
	panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(UI_MODAL_BG, UI_EDGE, 0, UI_BORDER_MODAL))
	overlay.add_child(panel)
	var eyebrow := _label("성의 대장장이" if source == "armorer" else "보물상자의 전리품", UI_LABEL_SIZE, GamePalette.YELLOW)
	eyebrow.position = Vector2(0.0, 20.0)
	eyebrow.size = Vector2(CHOICE_MODAL_RECT.size.x, 20.0)
	eyebrow.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	panel.add_child(eyebrow)
	var heading := _label("가져갈 아이템 카드를 고르세요", UI_TITLE_SIZE, GamePalette.TEXT)
	heading.position = Vector2(0.0, 42.0)
	heading.size = Vector2(CHOICE_MODAL_RECT.size.x, 38.0)
	heading.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	panel.add_child(heading)
	var warning := _label("고른 카드는 카드 보관함으로 직행   ·   고르지 않은 카드는 마왕에게 전달", UI_LABEL_SIZE, GamePalette.MUTED)
	warning.position = Vector2(0.0, 86.0)
	warning.size = Vector2(CHOICE_MODAL_RECT.size.x, 24.0)
	warning.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	panel.add_child(warning)
	for index in 2:
		var button := _item_choice_button(pair[index])
		button.position = Vector2(55.0 + index * 570.0, 120.0)
		panel.add_child(button)
		_register_choice_button(button, "item", _choose_offered_item.bind(index))
	# 카드 아래(y 372)와 푸터(y 462) 사이에 42px 빈 띠가 남아 skill choice와 리듬이 달랐습니다 (P2-6).
	var storage_note := _label("보관함 %d장 보유   ·   배치는 ESC 딜싸이클 공장에서" % (0 if factory == null else factory.inventory.size()), UI_BODY_SIZE, GamePalette.GREEN)
	storage_note.position = Vector2(0.0, 412.0)
	storage_note.size = Vector2(CHOICE_MODAL_RECT.size.x, 24.0)
	storage_note.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	panel.add_child(storage_note)
	var footer := _label("← 왼쪽 아이템   ·   → 오른쪽 아이템   ·   SPACE 결정", UI_LABEL_SIZE, GamePalette.CYAN)
	footer.position = Vector2(0.0, 462.0)
	footer.size = Vector2(CHOICE_MODAL_RECT.size.x, 22.0)
	footer.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	panel.add_child(footer)
	_animate_modal(panel, Vector2(0.0, 18.0))
	_set_choice_index(0)
	play_sound("choice", -2.0)
	if automated_test:
		call_deferred("_choose_offered_item", 0)

func _item_choice_button(item: Dictionary, card_size: Vector2 = CHOICE_CARD_SIZE) -> Button:
	# ⑳ 아이템 2지선다도 스킬과 같은 골격 · 같은 카드 블록을 쓴다.
	var rarity := String(item.get("rarity", "common"))
	var rarity_color := ItemLibrary.rarity_color(rarity)
	var slot_name := _item_slot_korean(String(item.get("slot", "weapon")))
	var owned := 0 if factory == null else factory.get_total_count(String(item["id"]))
	var button := _button("", rarity_color, card_size)
	button.set_meta("owned_text", "%d장 보유중" % owned)
	var reload_cut := ItemLibrary.global_reload_reduction(item)
	var reload_text := "지속시간 없음 · 칸을 즉시 통과"
	if reload_cut > 0.0:
		reload_text = "전체 RELOAD  -%d%%" % int(round(reload_cut * 100.0))
	var tag_text := "%s · %s\n적용 범위  %s" % [ItemLibrary.rarity_name(rarity), slot_name, ItemLibrary.effect_scope(item)]
	_build_choice_card_body(button, ItemLibrary.instance(String(item["id"])), card_size, tag_text, String(item.get("desc", "")), [
		{"text":reload_text, "color":GamePalette.ORANGE if reload_cut > 0.0 else GamePalette.MUTED},
		{"text":"상점 가치 %d G" % ItemLibrary.price_for(item), "color":GamePalette.YELLOW},
		{"text":String(button.get_meta("owned_text", "")), "color":GamePalette.GREEN if owned > 0 else GamePalette.MUTED}
	])
	return button

func _store_item_card(item: Dictionary) -> bool:
	if factory == null or item.is_empty():
		return false
	var before := factory.inventory.size()
	factory.add_inventory(ItemLibrary.instance(String(item["id"])))
	return factory.inventory.size() > before

func _choose_offered_item(index: int) -> void:
	if state != "item_choice" or current_item_pair.size() != 2:
		return
	var picked := clampi(index, 0, 1)
	var chosen: Dictionary = (current_item_pair[picked] as Dictionary).duplicate(true)
	var passed: Dictionary = (current_item_pair[1 - picked] as Dictionary).duplicate(true)
	current_item_pair.clear()
	var stored := _store_item_card(chosen)
	boss_items.append(String(passed["id"]))
	_clear_overlay()
	get_tree().paused = false
	state = item_return_state
	_grant_modal_return_invulnerability()
	var passed_name := String(passed.get("name", "아이템"))
	if stored:
		_show_banner("%s → 카드 보관함에 추가 · %s%s 마왕에게" % [chosen.get("name", "아이템"), passed_name, _particle_eun(passed_name)], GamePalette.GREEN, 3.0)
	else:
		_show_banner("보관함에 넣지 못했습니다 · %s%s 마왕에게" % [passed_name, _particle_eun(passed_name)], GamePalette.RED, 3.0)
	_show_boss_growth_toast([{"id":passed["id"], "name":passed.get("name", "아이템"), "debt_desc":"고르지 않은 아이템 카드가 마왕의 최적 딜싸이클에 추가됩니다."}])
	_update_hud()
	_check_first_advancement()

func _resolve_item_offer(item: Dictionary, keep: bool) -> void:
	# 구버전 진입점. keep=true는 보관함 직행, keep=false는 마왕 전달입니다.
	# 새 규칙에서는 아이템 획득이 즉시 배치(factory_place)로 이어지지 않습니다.
	if state != "item_choice" or item.is_empty():
		return
	current_item_pair.clear()
	_clear_overlay()
	get_tree().paused = false
	state = item_return_state
	_grant_modal_return_invulnerability()
	if keep:
		if _store_item_card(item):
			_show_banner("%s → 카드 보관함에 추가 · ESC 공장에서 배치" % item.get("name", "아이템"), GamePalette.GREEN, 2.8)
		else:
			_show_banner("보관함에 넣지 못했습니다", GamePalette.RED, 2.4)
	else:
		boss_items.append(String(item["id"]))
		var discarded_name := String(item.get("name", "아이템"))
		_show_banner("%s%s 버림 · 마왕 공장으로 배송" % [discarded_name, _particle_eul(discarded_name)], GamePalette.RED, 3.0)
		_show_boss_growth_toast([{"id":item["id"], "name":item.get("name", "아이템"), "debt_desc":"아이템 카드가 마왕의 최적 딜싸이클에 추가됩니다."}])
	_update_hud()
	_check_first_advancement()

func _show_base_camp(castle: Dictionary) -> void:
	# Kept as a compatibility entry point for automated checks and older calls.
	_enter_castle(castle)

func _enter_castle(castle: Dictionary) -> void:
	if state != "playing" or inside_castle or not is_instance_valid(player):
		return
	_play_scene_transition(_enter_castle_now.bind(castle.duplicate(true)), Color("090d1a"))

func _enter_castle_now(castle: Dictionary) -> void:
	if state != "playing" or inside_castle or not is_instance_valid(player):
		return
	# current_interactable과 같은 Dictionary가 전달될 수 있으므로 먼저 독립 복사합니다.
	# 복사 전에 current_interactable을 clear하면 실제 E 입력 경로에서 성 id가 사라집니다.
	var castle_data := castle.duplicate(true)
	field_return_position = player.global_position
	current_castle = castle_data.duplicate(true)
	inside_castle = true
	state = "castle_interior"
	current_interactable.clear()
	interaction_text.visible = false
	castle_interior = CASTLE_INTERIOR_SCRIPT.new()
	var awakening_boost := player.trophy_orbs.size() >= 4 and player.advancement_tier == 1
	castle_interior.setup(world.get_castle_services(String(castle_data["id"]), awakening_boost), String(castle_data["id"]))
	gameplay_root.add_child(castle_interior)
	_set_field_suspended(true)
	player.global_position = castle_interior.get_spawn_position()
	player.velocity = Vector2.ZERO
	player.dash_time_left = 0.0
	var camera := player.get_node_or_null("PlayerCamera") as Camera2D
	if is_instance_valid(camera):
		camera.reset_smoothing()
	canvas_modulate.color = Color.WHITE
	phase_text.text = "여행자의 성 · 베이스캠프"
	phase_text.add_theme_color_override("font_color", GamePalette.YELLOW)
	compass_text.text = "성 내부 · NPC 배치는 성마다 고정"
	_show_banner("성 안으로 들어왔습니다 · NPC에게 다가가 E", GamePalette.YELLOW, 2.5)
	play_sound("camp", -1.0)

func _set_field_suspended(suspended: bool) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(gameplay_root):
		return
	for child: Node in gameplay_root.get_children():
		if child == player or child == castle_interior or child == canvas_modulate:
			continue
		child.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_DISABLED if suspended else Node.PROCESS_MODE_INHERIT
		if child is CanvasItem:
			(child as CanvasItem).visible = not suspended

func _exit_castle() -> void:
	if not inside_castle or not is_instance_valid(player):
		return
	_play_scene_transition(_exit_castle_now, Color("090d1a"))

func _exit_castle_now() -> void:
	if not inside_castle or not is_instance_valid(player):
		return
	current_interactable.clear()
	interaction_text.visible = false
	_set_field_suspended(false)
	if is_instance_valid(castle_interior):
		castle_interior.queue_free()
	castle_interior = null
	inside_castle = false
	state = "playing"
	player.global_position = field_return_position
	player.velocity = Vector2.ZERO
	var camera := player.get_node_or_null("PlayerCamera") as Camera2D
	if is_instance_valid(camera):
		camera.reset_smoothing()
	# 성에서 필드로 나올 때도 모달 복귀와 같은 유예 무적을 준다. 이 함수는 커튼이 완전히
	# 닫힌 순간에 실행되므로 커튼이 다시 열릴 때까지의 시간을 더해 보이는 0.5초를 확보한다.
	player.grant_invulnerability(MODAL_RETURN_INVULN + (SCENE_TRANSITION_TAIL if transition_active else 0.0))
	_update_world_lighting(1.0)
	_update_hud()
	_show_banner("필드로 복귀했습니다", GamePalette.GREEN, 1.8)

func _show_single_npc_service(service: String) -> void:
	if state != "castle_interior":
		return
	if service == "card_fusion":
		_show_fusion_service()
		return
	if service == "card_shop":
		_show_card_shop(false)
		return
	if service == "factory_mage":
		_show_factory_mage()
		return
	var info := _service_info(service)
	state = "camp"
	get_tree().paused = true
	_clear_overlay()
	overlay = Control.new()
	overlay.name = "CastleNPCService"
	overlay.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	overlay.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	ui_root.add_child(overlay)
	var dim := ColorRect.new()
	dim.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	dim.color = Color("080c17a8")
	overlay.add_child(dim)
	var panel := Panel.new()
	panel.position = Vector2(340.0, 120.0)
	panel.size = Vector2(600.0, 465.0)
	panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(UI_MODAL_BG, UI_EDGE, 0, UI_BORDER_MODAL))
	overlay.add_child(panel)
	var accent := ColorRect.new()
	accent.position = Vector2(28.0, 28.0)
	accent.size = Vector2(4.0, 34.0)
	accent.color = info["color"]
	panel.add_child(accent)
	var title := _label(String(info["name"]), UI_TITLE_SIZE, info["color"])
	title.position = Vector2(44.0, 26.0)
	title.size = Vector2(520.0, 40.0)
	panel.add_child(title)
	var dialogue := _label("어서 오게, 여행자.\n%s" % String(info["desc"]), UI_HEADING_SIZE, GamePalette.TEXT)
	dialogue.position = Vector2(55.0, 100.0)
	dialogue.size = Vector2(490.0, 130.0)
	dialogue.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	dialogue.vertical_alignment = VERTICAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	dialogue.autowrap_mode = TextServer.AUTOWRAP_WORD_SMART
	panel.add_child(dialogue)
	var payment := "네 시련 구슬을 바칩니다" if service == "awakening_master" else "비용 %d G · 현재 %d G" % [int(info["cost"]), gold]
	var cost_label := _label(payment, UI_BODY_SIZE, GamePalette.YELLOW)
	cost_label.position = Vector2(40.0, 245.0)
	cost_label.size = Vector2(520.0, 32.0)
	cost_label.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	panel.add_child(cost_label)
	var use_button := _button("부탁한다", info["color"], Vector2(220.0, 58.0))
	use_button.position = Vector2(65.0, 320.0)
	use_button.pressed.connect(_use_service.bind(service))
	panel.add_child(use_button)
	var close := _button("대화를 마친다 · ESC", GamePalette.MUTED, Vector2(220.0, 58.0))
	close.position = Vector2(315.0, 320.0)
	close.pressed.connect(_close_base_camp)
	panel.add_child(close)
	var hint := _label("대화를 마치면 같은 성 내부로 돌아갑니다.", UI_CAPTION_SIZE, GamePalette.MUTED)
	hint.position = Vector2(30.0, 410.0)
	hint.size = Vector2(540.0, 24.0)
	hint.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	panel.add_child(hint)
	use_button.grab_focus()
	play_sound("choice", -4.0)

func _service_info(service: String) -> Dictionary:
	match service:
		"card_fusion": return {"name":"카드 합성 장인", "desc":"같은 스킬·같은 RANK 두 장을 다음 RANK로 합칩니다.", "cost":0, "color":GamePalette.MAGENTA}
		"card_shop": return {"name":"딜싸이클 카드상", "desc":"스킬 카드와 아이템 카드를 사고 상품을 새로고침합니다.", "cost":0, "color":GamePalette.CYAN}
		"factory_mage": return {"name":"레일 강화술사", "desc":"연결 부품·분열·2회 실행·속도 강화를 판매합니다.", "cost":0, "color":GamePalette.ORANGE}
		"merchant": return {"name":"약초 상인", "desc":"체력을 45 회복합니다.", "cost":25, "color":GamePalette.GREEN}
		"skill_remove": return {"name":"망각의 사제", "desc":"가장 최근 기술을 지웁니다.\n지운 힘은 마왕에게 갑니다.", "cost":35, "color":GamePalette.MAGENTA}
		"boss_remove": return {"name":"퇴마사", "desc":"마왕의 최근 기술 하나를\n완전히 봉인합니다.", "cost":60, "color":GamePalette.BLUE}
		"skill_swap": return {"name":"운명의 직조사", "desc":"최근 기술을 무작위 기술로\n교체합니다.", "cost":45, "color":GamePalette.CYAN}
		"armorer": return {"name":"왕실 대장장이", "desc":"공장에 넣을 무기 또는 액세서리\n아이템 카드를 제안합니다.", "cost":50, "color":GamePalette.ORANGE}
		"awakening_master": return {"name":"전승의 현자", "desc":"네 시련 구슬을 바쳐\n2차 전직을 완성합니다.", "cost":0, "color":GamePalette.YELLOW}
		_: return {"name":"여관 주인", "desc":"체력을 모두 회복합니다.", "cost":30, "color":GamePalette.YELLOW}

func _show_fusion_service() -> void:
	state = "camp"
	get_tree().paused = true
	_clear_overlay()
	overlay = Control.new()
	overlay.name = "CardFusion"
	overlay.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	overlay.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	ui_root.add_child(overlay)
	var dim := ColorRect.new()
	dim.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	dim.color = Color("070b14dc")
	overlay.add_child(dim)
	var panel := Panel.new()
	panel.position = Vector2(170.0, 60.0)
	panel.size = Vector2(940.0, 600.0)
	panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(UI_MODAL_BG, UI_EDGE, 0, UI_BORDER_MODAL))
	overlay.add_child(panel)
	var accent := ColorRect.new()
	accent.position = Vector2(28.0, 22.0)
	accent.size = Vector2(4.0, 34.0)
	accent.color = GamePalette.MAGENTA
	panel.add_child(accent)
	var title := _label("카드 합성소 · 두 장을 한 장으로", UI_TITLE_SIZE, GamePalette.MAGENTA)
	title.position = Vector2(44.0, 20.0)
	title.size = Vector2(700.0, 40.0)
	panel.add_child(title)
	var rule := _label("같은 스킬·같은 RANK 2장 → 다음 RANK 1장 · 최대 RANK 5 · 레일에 배치된 카드도 재료에 포함", UI_BODY_SIZE, GamePalette.MUTED)
	rule.position = Vector2(44.0, 66.0)
	rule.size = Vector2(872.0, 24.0)
	panel.add_child(rule)
	var scroll := ScrollContainer.new()
	scroll.position = Vector2(35.0, 120.0)
	scroll.size = Vector2(870.0, 380.0)
	panel.add_child(scroll)
	var list := VBoxContainer.new()
	list.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 8)
	scroll.add_child(list)
	var candidates := factory.fusion_candidates()
	if candidates.is_empty():
		var empty := _label("지금 합칠 수 있는 카드가 없습니다.\n같은 카드가 두 장 모이면 제가 야무지게 눌러드립니다.", UI_HEADING_SIZE, GamePalette.MUTED)
		empty.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(840.0, 150.0)
		empty.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
		empty.vertical_alignment = VERTICAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
		list.add_child(empty)
	for candidate: Dictionary in candidates:
		var definition := DealCardLibrary.by_id(String(candidate["id"]))
		var fusion_color := Color(String(definition.get("color", "f4d35e")))
		var button := _button("", fusion_color, Vector2(840.0, 66.0))
		var fusion_icon := SKILL_ICON_SCRIPT.new()
		fusion_icon.position = Vector2(12.0, 7.0)
		fusion_icon.size = Vector2(52.0, 52.0)
		fusion_icon.setup(String(candidate["id"]), fusion_color)
		button.add_child(fusion_icon)
		var fusion_text := _label("%s    R%d ×2  →  R%d    ·    현재 %d장" % [definition.get("name", "기술"), int(candidate["rank"]), int(candidate["rank"]) + 1, int(candidate["count"])], UI_HEADING_SIZE, GamePalette.TEXT)
		fusion_text.position = Vector2(82.0, 8.0)
		fusion_text.size = Vector2(730.0, 50.0)
		fusion_text.vertical_alignment = VERTICAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
		button.add_child(fusion_text)
		button.pressed.connect(_fuse_card.bind(String(candidate["id"]), int(candidate["rank"])))
		list.add_child(button)
	var close := _button("합성소 나가기 · ESC", GamePalette.MUTED, Vector2(260.0, 48.0))
	close.position = Vector2(340.0, 530.0)
	close.pressed.connect(_close_base_camp)
	panel.add_child(close)
	close.grab_focus()

func _fuse_card(card_id: String, rank: int) -> void:
	if state != "camp" or not factory.fuse(card_id, rank):
		return
	var definition := DealCardLibrary.by_id(card_id)
	_reset_player_cycle()
	_show_banner("합성 완료 · %s R%d" % [definition.get("name", "기술"), rank + 1], GamePalette.MAGENTA, 2.4)
	_check_first_advancement()
	if state == "camp":
		_show_fusion_service()

func _show_card_shop(refresh: bool = false) -> void:
	var active_castle_id := String(current_castle.get("id", "field_shop"))
	if not refresh and shop_castle_id != active_castle_id:
		shop_refresh_count = 0
		shop_offers.clear()
		shop_castle_id = active_castle_id
	if shop_offers.is_empty() or refresh:
		shop_offers.clear()
		var skill_pool := DealCardLibrary.SKILLS.duplicate(true)
		for _index in 2:
			var definition: Dictionary = skill_pool.pop_at(rng.randi_range(0, skill_pool.size() - 1))
			shop_offers.append({"card":DealCardLibrary.instance(String(definition["id"]), 1), "name":definition["name"], "desc":definition["desc"], "price":rng.randi_range(24, 42), "sold":false})
		for _index in 2:
			var item := ItemLibrary.random_item(rng, "", "swordsman", 0.25)
			shop_offers.append({"card":ItemLibrary.instance(String(item["id"])), "name":item["name"], "desc":item["desc"], "price":ItemLibrary.price_for(item), "sold":false})
	state = "camp"
	get_tree().paused = true
	_clear_overlay()
	overlay = Control.new()
	overlay.name = "CardShop"
	overlay.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	overlay.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	ui_root.add_child(overlay)
	var dim := ColorRect.new()
	dim.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	dim.color = Color("070b14e8")
	overlay.add_child(dim)
	var panel := Panel.new()
	panel.position = Vector2(62.0, 55.0)
	panel.size = Vector2(1156.0, 610.0)
	panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(UI_MODAL_BG, UI_EDGE, 0, UI_BORDER_MODAL))
	overlay.add_child(panel)
	var accent := ColorRect.new()
	accent.position = Vector2(28.0, 20.0)
	accent.size = Vector2(4.0, 34.0)
	accent.color = GamePalette.CYAN
	panel.add_child(accent)
	var title := _label("딜싸이클 카드상", UI_TITLE_SIZE, GamePalette.CYAN)
	title.position = Vector2(44.0, 18.0)
	title.size = Vector2(700.0, 40.0)
	panel.add_child(title)
	var money := _label("보유 %d G · 새로고침 %d회" % [gold, shop_refresh_count], UI_BODY_SIZE, GamePalette.YELLOW)
	money.position = Vector2(788.0, 26.0)
	money.size = Vector2(340.0, 24.0)
	money.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT
	panel.add_child(money)
	for index in shop_offers.size():
		var offer: Dictionary = shop_offers[index]
		var card: Dictionary = offer["card"]
		var color := _factory_card_color(card)
		var sold := bool(offer.get("sold", false))
		var button := _button("", color, Vector2(252.0, 360.0))
		button.position = Vector2(42.0 + index * 273.0, 95.0)
		button.disabled = sold
		# 판매 완료 카드도 같은 카드 렌더러를 타게 두고 딤 + 띠로만 구분합니다.
		# 예전에는 아이콘·이름 없이 텅 빈 어두운 상자만 남아 무엇이 팔렸는지 알 수 없었습니다 (P2-5).
		_decorate_shop_offer(button, card, offer)
		if sold:
			button.modulate = Color(0.46, 0.46, 0.53)
			var sold_band := Panel.new()
			# 띠는 이름·설명·지표를 가리지 않도록 가격 자리에 얹습니다.
			sold_band.position = Vector2(0.0, 308.0)
			sold_band.size = Vector2(252.0, 46.0)
			sold_band.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
			sold_band.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(Color("080d16f4"), UI_EDGE, 0, UI_BORDER_FRAME))
			button.add_child(sold_band)
			var sold_label := _label("판매 완료", UI_HEADING_SIZE, GamePalette.TEXT)
			sold_label.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
			sold_label.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
			sold_label.vertical_alignment = VERTICAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
			sold_band.add_child(sold_label)
		button.pressed.connect(_buy_shop_offer.bind(index))
		panel.add_child(button)
	var refresh_cost := 10 + shop_refresh_count * 6
	var reroll := _button("상품 새로고침 · %d G" % refresh_cost, GamePalette.ORANGE, Vector2(280.0, 52.0))
	reroll.position = Vector2(270.0, 500.0)
	reroll.pressed.connect(_refresh_shop.bind(refresh_cost))
	panel.add_child(reroll)
	var close := _button("상점 나가기 · ESC", GamePalette.MUTED, Vector2(260.0, 52.0))
	close.position = Vector2(640.0, 500.0)
	close.pressed.connect(_close_base_camp)
	panel.add_child(close)
	close.grab_focus()

func _refresh_shop(cost: int) -> void:
	if state != "camp": return
	if gold < cost:
		_show_banner("새로고침 비용이 부족합니다", GamePalette.RED, 1.8)
		return
	gold -= cost
	shop_refresh_count += 1
	_show_card_shop(true)

func _buy_shop_offer(index: int) -> void:
	if state != "camp" or index < 0 or index >= shop_offers.size(): return
	var offer: Dictionary = shop_offers[index]
	if bool(offer.get("sold", false)): return
	var cost := int(offer["price"])
	if gold < cost:
		_show_banner("골드가 부족합니다 · 필요 %d G" % cost, GamePalette.RED, 1.8)
		return
	gold -= cost
	offer["sold"] = true
	shop_offers[index] = offer
	var purchased: Dictionary = (offer["card"] as Dictionary).duplicate(true)
	if String(purchased.get("kind", "skill")) == "item":
		# 아이템 카드는 즉시 배치하지 않고 카드 보관함으로 직행합니다.
		if factory != null:
			factory.add_inventory(purchased)
		_show_card_shop(false)
		_show_banner("%s → 카드 보관함에 추가 · ESC 공장에서 배치" % offer.get("name", "아이템 카드"), GamePalette.GREEN, 2.6)
		_update_hud()
		_check_first_advancement()
		return
	_clear_overlay()
	_show_factory_menu("place", purchased, "castle_interior")

func _show_factory_mage() -> void:
	state = "camp"
	get_tree().paused = true
	_clear_overlay()
	overlay = Control.new()
	overlay.name = "FactoryMage"
	overlay.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	overlay.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	ui_root.add_child(overlay)
	var dim := ColorRect.new()
	dim.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	dim.color = Color("070b14e8")
	overlay.add_child(dim)
	var panel := Panel.new()
	panel.position = Vector2(250.0, 52.0)
	panel.size = Vector2(780.0, 620.0)
	panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(UI_MODAL_BG, UI_EDGE, 0, UI_BORDER_MODAL))
	overlay.add_child(panel)
	var accent := ColorRect.new()
	accent.position = Vector2(28.0, 20.0)
	accent.size = Vector2(4.0, 34.0)
	accent.color = GamePalette.ORANGE
	panel.add_child(accent)
	var title := _label("레일 강화술사", UI_TITLE_SIZE, GamePalette.ORANGE)
	title.position = Vector2(44.0, 18.0)
	title.size = Vector2(500.0, 40.0)
	panel.add_child(title)
	var mage_money := _label("보유 %d G" % gold, UI_BODY_SIZE, GamePalette.YELLOW)
	mage_money.position = Vector2(452.0, 26.0)
	mage_money.size = Vector2(300.0, 24.0)
	mage_money.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT
	panel.add_child(mage_money)
	var split_stock_roll := absi((String(current_castle.get("id", "factory")) + "_%d" % cycle_number).hash()) % 100
	var split_in_stock: bool = automated_test or split_stock_roll < 35
	# 적용할 수 있는 칸이 하나도 없는 강화는 아예 구매를 막습니다.
	# (예전에는 3칸 모두 "2회 실행"을 받은 뒤에도 구매가 가능해 골드만 잃고 갇혔습니다.)
	var can_split: bool = split_in_stock and factory.can_apply_upgrade("split")
	var can_repeat := factory.can_apply_upgrade("repeat")
	var can_duration := factory.can_apply_upgrade("duration")
	var can_reload := factory.can_apply_upgrade("reload")
	var sold_out_note := " · 모든 칸에 이미 적용됨"
	var offers: Array[Dictionary] = [
		{"id":"build","name":"다음 레일 부품 건설","desc":"다리와 칸을 번갈아 건설합니다.","cost":55,"enabled":factory.can_build()},
		{"id":"split","name":"동시 레일 분열","desc":"희귀 입고품 · 한 칸에 실행 줄 +1. 런당 2회.%s" % ("" if can_split else (" · 이 성은 품절" if not split_in_stock else sold_out_note)),"cost":120,"enabled":can_split},
		{"id":"repeat","name":"같은 칸 2회 실행","desc":"선택한 슬롯의 모든 스킬을 두 번 실행합니다.%s" % ("" if can_repeat else sold_out_note),"cost":95,"enabled":can_repeat},
		{"id":"duration","name":"고속 컨베이어","desc":"선택한 슬롯의 지속시간을 14%% 줄입니다.%s" % ("" if can_duration else sold_out_note),"cost":72,"enabled":can_duration},
		{"id":"reload","name":"RELOAD 압축","desc":"선택한 슬롯이 더하는 RELOAD를 18%% 줄입니다.%s" % ("" if can_reload else sold_out_note),"cost":78,"enabled":can_reload}
	]
	for index in offers.size():
		var offer: Dictionary = offers[index]
		var button := _button("%s · %d G\n%s" % [offer["name"], offer["cost"], offer["desc"]], GamePalette.ORANGE if index < 2 else GamePalette.CYAN, Vector2(680.0, 72.0))
		button.position = Vector2(50.0, 86.0 + index * 83.0)
		button.disabled = not bool(offer["enabled"])
		button.pressed.connect(_buy_factory_upgrade.bind(String(offer["id"]), int(offer["cost"])))
		panel.add_child(button)
	var close := _button("공방을 나선다 · ESC", GamePalette.MUTED, Vector2(260.0, 45.0))
	close.position = Vector2(260.0, 538.0)
	close.pressed.connect(_close_base_camp)
	panel.add_child(close)
	close.grab_focus()

func _buy_factory_upgrade(upgrade_type: String, cost: int) -> void:
	if state != "camp": return
	if gold < cost:
		_show_banner("골드가 부족합니다 · 필요 %d G" % cost, GamePalette.RED, 1.8)
		return
	gold -= cost
	if upgrade_type == "build":
		var result := factory.build_next_piece()
		_show_factory_menu("build", result, "castle_interior")
		return
	if not factory.can_apply_upgrade(upgrade_type):
		# 버튼 게이트를 우회해 들어온 경우에도 골드를 잃지 않도록 마지막 방어선을 둡니다.
		gold += cost
		_show_banner("이 강화를 적용할 칸이 남아 있지 않습니다", GamePalette.MUTED, 2.2)
		return
	pending_factory_upgrade = upgrade_type
	factory_upgrade_refund = cost
	_show_factory_menu("upgrade_%s" % upgrade_type, {}, "castle_interior")

func _last_factory_skill_location() -> Dictionary:
	# 망각의 사제 / 운명의 직조사가 대상으로 삼는 "가장 최근 스킬 카드"를 찾습니다.
	# 보관함을 뒤에서부터 뒤지고, 비어 있으면 레일의 뒤쪽 칸부터 찾습니다.
	# 아이템 카드와 빈칸(기본 베기)은 대상이 아닙니다.
	if factory == null:
		return {}
	for index in range(factory.inventory.size() - 1, -1, -1):
		var stored: Dictionary = factory.inventory[index]
		if String(stored.get("kind", "skill")) == "item" or String(stored.get("id", "")) == "basic":
			continue
		return {"zone":"inventory", "index":index, "card":stored.duplicate(true)}
	for slot_index in range(factory.slots.size() - 1, -1, -1):
		var lanes: Array = factory.slots[slot_index].get("lanes", [])
		for lane_index in range(lanes.size() - 1, -1, -1):
			if not lanes[lane_index] is Dictionary:
				continue
			var lane_card := lanes[lane_index] as Dictionary
			if lane_card.is_empty() or String(lane_card.get("kind", "skill")) == "item":
				continue
			if String(lane_card.get("id", "")) == "basic":
				continue
			return {"zone":"rail", "slot":slot_index, "lane":lane_index, "card":lane_card.duplicate(true)}
	return {}

func _take_factory_skill_card() -> Dictionary:
	var location := _last_factory_skill_location()
	if location.is_empty():
		return {}
	var removed := {}
	if String(location.get("zone", "")) == "inventory":
		removed = factory.remove_inventory_at(int(location["index"]))
	else:
		removed = factory.clear_lane(int(location["slot"]), int(location["lane"]))
	_reset_player_cycle()
	return removed

func _use_service(service: String) -> void:
	if state != "camp":
		return
	if service == "awakening_master":
		if player.advancement_tier == 0:
			_show_banner("먼저 대응 무기와 RANK 5 카드 두 장으로 1차 전직해야 합니다", GamePalette.MUTED, 2.5)
			return
		if player.advancement_tier >= 2:
			_show_banner("이미 전승을 완성했습니다", GamePalette.YELLOW, 2.2)
			return
		if player.trophy_orbs.size() < 4:
			_show_banner("시련 구슬이 부족합니다 · 현재 %d / 4" % player.trophy_orbs.size(), GamePalette.MAGENTA, 2.5)
			return
		_begin_advancement(ClassLibrary.by_id(player.advancement_branch_id), 2)
		return
	var info := _service_info(service)
	var cost: int = info["cost"]
	if gold < cost:
		_show_banner("골드가 부족합니다 · 필요 %d G" % cost, GamePalette.RED, 2.0)
		return
	# 딜싸이클 개편 이후 스킬은 player.applied_skills가 아니라 FactoryDeck(보관함 + 레일)에
	# 보관됩니다. 예전 게이트는 항상 빈 배열을 봐서 두 NPC가 영구히 작동하지 않았습니다.
	if service in ["skill_remove", "skill_swap"] and _last_factory_skill_location().is_empty():
		_show_banner("지우거나 바꿀 스킬 카드가 없습니다", GamePalette.MUTED, 2.0)
		return
	if service == "boss_remove" and rejected_skills.is_empty():
		_show_banner("봉인할 마왕의 기술이 없습니다", GamePalette.MUTED, 2.0)
		return
	gold -= cost
	match service:
		"merchant":
			player.heal(45.0)
			_close_base_camp()
			_show_banner("약초로 체력 45 회복", GamePalette.GREEN, 2.2)
		"skill_remove":
			var removed_card := _take_factory_skill_card()
			var removed_id := String(removed_card.get("id", ""))
			selected_skills.erase(removed_id)
			rejected_skills.append(removed_id)
			_close_base_camp()
			var removed_name := String(DealCardLibrary.by_id(removed_id).get("name", removed_id))
			_show_banner("%s%s 지움 · 마왕이 가져감" % [removed_name, _particle_eul(removed_name)], GamePalette.RED, 2.7)
		"boss_remove":
			var sealed: String = String(rejected_skills.pop_back())
			_close_base_camp()
			_show_banner("마왕의 %s 봉인" % DealCardLibrary.by_id(sealed).get("name", sealed), GamePalette.BLUE, 2.5)
		"skill_swap":
			var discarded_card := _take_factory_skill_card()
			var discarded_id := String(discarded_card.get("id", ""))
			var discarded_rank := clampi(int(discarded_card.get("rank", 1)), 1, 5)
			selected_skills.erase(discarded_id)
			rejected_skills.append(discarded_id)
			# 같은 RANK로 바꿔 줍니다. 교체이지 강등이 아니기 때문입니다.
			var replacement_id := String(DealCardLibrary.SKILLS[rng.randi_range(0, DealCardLibrary.SKILLS.size() - 1)]["id"])
			factory.add_inventory(DealCardLibrary.instance(replacement_id, discarded_rank))
			selected_skills.append(replacement_id)
			_close_base_camp()
			_show_banner("%s → %s R%d · 카드 보관함에 입고" % [
				DealCardLibrary.by_id(discarded_id).get("name", discarded_id),
				DealCardLibrary.by_id(replacement_id).get("name", replacement_id), discarded_rank
			], GamePalette.CYAN, 2.8)
		"armorer":
			_close_base_camp()
			call_deferred("_show_item_offer", "armorer")
		_:
			player.heal_full()
			_close_base_camp()
			_show_banner("여관에서 완전히 회복했습니다", GamePalette.YELLOW, 2.2)
	_update_hud()
	if state in ["playing", "castle_interior"] and service == "skill_swap":
		_check_first_advancement()

func _check_first_advancement() -> void:
	if state not in ["playing", "castle_interior", "camp"] or not is_instance_valid(player) or player.advancement_tier > 0 or factory == null:
		return
	for branch: Dictionary in ClassLibrary.for_base(player.character_id):
		var required: Array = branch.get("skills", [])
		if required.size() != 2:
			continue
		var required_rank := int(branch.get("rank", 5))
		if factory.owns_weapon_type(String(branch.get("weapon_type", ""))) and factory.get_rank_count(String(required[0]), required_rank) >= 1 and factory.get_rank_count(String(required[1]), required_rank) >= 1:
			_begin_advancement(branch, 1)
			return

func _begin_advancement(branch: Dictionary, tier: int) -> void:
	if branch.is_empty() or not is_instance_valid(player):
		return
	advancement_return_state = "castle_interior" if inside_castle else "playing"
	var old_name := player.get_character_name()
	if tier == 1:
		for required_skill in branch.get("skills", []):
			factory.consume_skill(String(required_skill), int(branch.get("rank", 5)), 1)
	else:
		player.clear_trophy_orbs()
	player.apply_advancement(branch, tier)
	pending_advancement = {"branch":branch.duplicate(true), "tier":tier}
	_reset_player_cycle()
	state = "evolution"
	get_tree().paused = true
	_clear_overlay()
	if automated_test:
		_show_advancement_card_choice(branch, tier)
		return
	overlay = Control.new()
	overlay.name = "Evolution"
	overlay.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	overlay.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	ui_root.add_child(overlay)
	var dim := ColorRect.new()
	dim.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	dim.color = Color("080b18f8")
	overlay.add_child(dim)
	var branch_color := Color(String(branch.get("color", "f4d35e")))
	for index in 18:
		var ray := ColorRect.new()
		ray.position = Vector2(640.0, 330.0)
		ray.size = Vector2(250.0 + index * 7.0, 3.0 if index % 2 else 6.0)
		ray.rotation = TAU * float(index) / 18.0
		ray.color = Color(branch_color, 0.13)
		ray.pivot_offset = Vector2.ZERO
		overlay.add_child(ray)
	var dialogue_panel := Panel.new()
	dialogue_panel.position = Vector2(155.0, 34.0)
	dialogue_panel.size = Vector2(970.0, 92.0)
	dialogue_panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(UI_MODAL_BG, UI_EDGE, 0, UI_BORDER_MODAL))
	overlay.add_child(dialogue_panel)
	var dialogue_accent := ColorRect.new()
	dialogue_accent.position = Vector2(12.0, 20.0)
	dialogue_accent.size = Vector2(4.0, 52.0)
	dialogue_accent.color = branch_color
	dialogue_panel.add_child(dialogue_accent)
	var dialogue := _label("……어라? 갑자기 몸이… 이상해!\n%s의 운명이 빛나기 시작했다!" % old_name, UI_HEADING_SIZE, GamePalette.TEXT)
	dialogue.position = Vector2(30.0, 13.0)
	dialogue.size = Vector2(912.0, 66.0)
	dialogue.vertical_alignment = VERTICAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	dialogue_panel.add_child(dialogue)
	var portrait := PORTRAIT_SCRIPT.new()
	portrait.position = Vector2(440.0, 153.0)
	portrait.size = Vector2(400.0, 330.0)
	portrait.setup("hero", branch_color)
	overlay.add_child(portrait)
	var new_class_name: String = branch["tier2_name"] if tier >= 2 else branch["tier1_name"]
	var evolution_title := _label("%s  →  %s" % [old_name, new_class_name], UI_TITLE_SIZE, branch_color)
	evolution_title.position = Vector2(150.0, 490.0)
	evolution_title.size = Vector2(980.0, 48.0)
	evolution_title.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	overlay.add_child(evolution_title)
	var detail_text := String(branch["tier2_desc"] if tier >= 2 else branch["tier1_desc"])
	if tier == 1:
		var requirements: Array = branch.get("skills", [])
		detail_text += "\n%s R5 + %s R5가 합쳐졌습니다. 무기 카드는 유지됩니다." % [DealCardLibrary.by_id(String(requirements[0])).get("name", "기술"), DealCardLibrary.by_id(String(requirements[1])).get("name", "기술")]
	detail_text += "\n이제 특별 스킬 두 장 중 하나를 고릅니다. 버린 한 장은 마왕에게 갑니다."
	var detail := _label(detail_text, UI_BODY_SIZE, GamePalette.TEXT)
	detail.position = Vector2(190.0, 540.0)
	detail.size = Vector2(900.0, 76.0)
	detail.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	detail.autowrap_mode = TextServer.AUTOWRAP_WORD_SMART
	overlay.add_child(detail)
	var accept := _button("%s의 특별 스킬을 선택한다 · SPACE" % new_class_name, branch_color, Vector2(420.0, 54.0))
	accept.position = Vector2(430.0, 637.0)
	accept.pressed.connect(_show_advancement_card_choice.bind(branch, tier))
	overlay.add_child(accept)
	accept.grab_focus()
	play_sound("choice", 1.0)

func _accept_evolution() -> void:
	# 전직 연출에서 SPACE/ENTER로 다음 화면(특별 카드 2지선다)으로 넘어갑니다 (P1-5).
	if state != "evolution" or pending_advancement.is_empty():
		return
	_show_advancement_card_choice(pending_advancement["branch"], int(pending_advancement["tier"]))

func _show_advancement_card_choice(branch: Dictionary, tier: int) -> void:
	_clear_overlay()
	var branch_color := Color(String(branch.get("color", "f4d35e")))
	spawn_burst(player.global_position, branch_color, 72 if tier >= 2 else 44, 430.0 if tier >= 2 else 310.0, 1.2)
	shake_camera(13.0 if tier >= 2 else 8.0, 0.55)
	var promoted_name: String = branch["tier2_name"] if tier >= 2 else branch["tier1_name"]
	var choice_ids: Array = branch.get("tier2_choices", []) if tier >= 2 else branch.get("tier1_choices", [])
	if choice_ids.size() != 2:
		return
	state = "advancement_choice"
	get_tree().paused = true
	_reset_choice_focus()
	overlay = Control.new()
	overlay.name = "AdvancementCardChoice"
	overlay.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	overlay.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	ui_root.add_child(overlay)
	var dim := ColorRect.new(); dim.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT); dim.color = Color("080b18f5"); overlay.add_child(dim)
	var panel := Panel.new(); panel.position = Vector2(105.0, 150.0); panel.size = Vector2(1070.0, 420.0); panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(UI_MODAL_BG, UI_EDGE, 0, UI_BORDER_MODAL)); overlay.add_child(panel)
	var title := _label("%d차 전직 · %s 특별 카드" % [tier, promoted_name], UI_TITLE_SIZE, branch_color); title.position = Vector2(20.0, 20.0); title.size = Vector2(1030.0, 38.0); title.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER; panel.add_child(title)
	var warning := _label("하나를 고르면 다른 하나는 마왕의 딜싸이클로 들어갑니다.", UI_LABEL_SIZE, GamePalette.RED); warning.position = Vector2(20.0, 62.0); warning.size = Vector2(1030.0, 22.0); warning.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER; panel.add_child(warning)
	for index in 2:
		var selected_id := String(choice_ids[index])
		var rejected_id := String(choice_ids[1 - index])
		var definition := DealCardLibrary.by_id(selected_id)
		var button := _deal_choice_button(definition, Vector2(494.0, CHOICE_CARD_SIZE.y))
		button.position = Vector2(24.0 + index * 528.0, 96.0)
		panel.add_child(button)
		_register_choice_button(button, "skill", _choose_advancement_card.bind(branch, tier, selected_id, rejected_id))
	var advancement_footer := _label("← 왼쪽 카드   ·   → 오른쪽 카드   ·   SPACE 결정", UI_LABEL_SIZE, GamePalette.CYAN)
	advancement_footer.position = Vector2(20.0, 376.0)
	advancement_footer.size = Vector2(1030.0, 24.0)
	advancement_footer.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	panel.add_child(advancement_footer)
	_set_choice_index(0)
	if automated_test:
		call_deferred("_choose_advancement_card", branch, tier, String(choice_ids[0]), String(choice_ids[1]))

func _choose_advancement_card(branch: Dictionary, tier: int, selected_id: String, rejected_id: String) -> void:
	if state != "advancement_choice":
		return
	var selected_definition := DealCardLibrary.by_id(selected_id)
	var rejected_definition := DealCardLibrary.by_id(rejected_id)
	selected_skills.append(selected_id)
	rejected_skills.append(rejected_id)
	boss_advancement_skills.append({"branch":branch["id"], "tier":tier, "name":rejected_definition.get("name", "전직 카드"), "module":DealCardLibrary.debt_module(rejected_id)})
	player.class_skill_id = selected_id
	player.class_skill_name = String(selected_definition.get("name", "전직 기술"))
	pending_boss_toast_cards = [rejected_definition]
	_clear_overlay()
	_show_factory_menu("place", DealCardLibrary.instance(selected_id, 1), advancement_return_state)
	_show_banner("%d차 전직 · %s / 특별 카드 선택" % [tier, branch["tier2_name"] if tier >= 2 else branch["tier1_name"]], Color(String(branch.get("color", "f4d35e"))), 4.0)

func _finish_advancement(branch: Dictionary, tier: int) -> void:
	# 이전 테스트 진입점. 새 버전에서는 전직 후 두 장 중 하나를 선택합니다.
	_show_advancement_card_choice(branch, tier)

func _close_base_camp() -> void:
	if state != "camp":
		return
	_clear_overlay()
	get_tree().paused = false
	state = "castle_interior" if inside_castle else "playing"
	# 필드에서 바로 연 NPC/캠프 모달이면 필드 복귀 무적을 준다(성 내부는 전투가 없어 제외).
	_grant_modal_return_invulnerability()
	interaction_timer = 0.0

const BOSS_PREVIEW_PANEL_RECT := Rect2(30.0, 26.0, 1220.0, 668.0)
const BOSS_PREVIEW_BODY_X := 28.0
const BOSS_PREVIEW_BODY_W := 1164.0

func _challenge_demon_king() -> void:
	if state != "playing" or not is_instance_valid(player):
		return
	boss_factory = _build_boss_factory()
	state = "boss_preview"
	get_tree().paused = true
	current_interactable.clear()
	interaction_text.visible = false
	_clear_overlay()
	overlay = Control.new()
	overlay.name = "BossDealCyclePreview"
	overlay.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	overlay.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	ui_root.add_child(overlay)
	var dim := ColorRect.new()
	dim.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	dim.color = Color("07040af8")
	overlay.add_child(dim)
	var panel := Panel.new()
	panel.position = BOSS_PREVIEW_PANEL_RECT.position
	panel.size = BOSS_PREVIEW_PANEL_RECT.size
	panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(UI_MODAL_BG, UI_EDGE, 0, UI_BORDER_MODAL))
	overlay.add_child(panel)
	var accent := ColorRect.new()
	accent.position = Vector2(BOSS_PREVIEW_BODY_X, 20.0)
	accent.size = Vector2(4.0, 34.0)
	accent.color = GamePalette.RED
	panel.add_child(accent)
	var title := _label("마왕의 딜싸이클", UI_TITLE_SIZE, GamePalette.TEXT)
	title.position = Vector2(BOSS_PREVIEW_BODY_X + 16.0, 18.0)
	title.size = Vector2(700.0, 40.0)
	panel.add_child(title)
	var lane_total := 0
	for slot: Dictionary in boss_factory.slots:
		lane_total += (slot.get("lanes", []) as Array).size()
	var status := _label("칸 %d · 실행 줄 %d · RELOAD 없음" % [boss_factory.slots.size(), lane_total], UI_LABEL_SIZE, GamePalette.RED)
	status.position = Vector2(BOSS_PREVIEW_BODY_X + BOSS_PREVIEW_BODY_W - 460.0, 24.0)
	status.size = Vector2(460.0, 22.0)
	status.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT
	panel.add_child(status)
	var subtitle := _label("당신이 버린 카드가 자동으로 합쳐져 %d칸에 %d장이 들어찼습니다 · 마지막 칸 뒤 쉬는 틈 없이 처음으로 돌아갑니다" % [boss_factory.slots.size(), lane_total], UI_BODY_SIZE, GamePalette.MUTED)
	subtitle.position = Vector2(BOSS_PREVIEW_BODY_X + 16.0, 62.0)
	subtitle.size = Vector2(BOSS_PREVIEW_BODY_W - 16.0, 22.0)
	panel.add_child(subtitle)
	# 카드는 공장·결과 화면과 완전히 같은 레일 렌더러로 그립니다. "마왕의 딜싸이클"과
	# "내 딜싸이클"을 나란히 비교하는 화면인데 예전에는 자체 카드 렌더러라 비교가 되지 않았습니다.
	var frame := Panel.new()
	frame.position = Vector2(BOSS_PREVIEW_BODY_X, 100.0)
	frame.size = Vector2(BOSS_PREVIEW_BODY_W, FACTORY_RAIL_ROW_H + 24.0)
	frame.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(UI_PANEL_BG, UI_EDGE_SOFT, 0, UI_BORDER_FRAME))
	panel.add_child(frame)
	var scroll := ScrollContainer.new()
	scroll.position = Vector2(12.0, 12.0)
	scroll.size = Vector2(BOSS_PREVIEW_BODY_W - 24.0, FACTORY_RAIL_ROW_H)
	scroll.horizontal_scroll_mode = ScrollContainer.SCROLL_MODE_AUTO
	scroll.vertical_scroll_mode = ScrollContainer.SCROLL_MODE_DISABLED
	frame.add_child(scroll)
	var rail := HBoxContainer.new()
	rail.alignment = BoxContainer.ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	rail.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 0)
	scroll.add_child(rail)
	var boss_slot_count := boss_factory.slots.size()
	var boss_content_width := float(boss_slot_count) * FACTORY_RAIL_CARD_W + float(maxi(0, boss_slot_count - 1)) * FACTORY_RAIL_BRIDGE_W
	rail.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(maxf(boss_content_width, scroll.size.x), FACTORY_RAIL_ROW_H)
	for slot_index in boss_slot_count:
		_build_factory_rail_slot(rail, slot_index, false, boss_factory)
		if slot_index < boss_slot_count - 1:
			_build_factory_rail_bridge(rail, "built")
	var rail_hint := _label("좌우 스크롤로 %d칸 전체를 확인할 수 있습니다" % boss_slot_count, UI_CAPTION_SIZE, GamePalette.MUTED.darkened(0.22))
	rail_hint.position = Vector2(BOSS_PREVIEW_BODY_X, 434.0)
	rail_hint.size = Vector2(BOSS_PREVIEW_BODY_W, 20.0)
	rail_hint.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT
	panel.add_child(rail_hint)
	var quote := _label("마왕: \"네가 버린 모든 선택이 내 손에서 하나의 고리가 되었다.\"", UI_HEADING_SIZE, GamePalette.RED.lightened(0.18))
	quote.position = Vector2(BOSS_PREVIEW_BODY_X, 468.0)
	quote.size = Vector2(BOSS_PREVIEW_BODY_W, 28.0)
	quote.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	panel.add_child(quote)
	var explanation := _label("지금 들어가면 이 딜싸이클이 실시간으로 되풀이됩니다. 준비가 덜 됐다면 돌아가 카드를 다듬으세요.", UI_BODY_SIZE, GamePalette.TEXT)
	explanation.position = Vector2(BOSS_PREVIEW_BODY_X, 500.0)
	explanation.size = Vector2(BOSS_PREVIEW_BODY_W, 24.0)
	explanation.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	panel.add_child(explanation)
	var challenge := _button("그래도 들어간다 · SPACE", GamePalette.RED, Vector2(330.0, 56.0))
	challenge.position = Vector2(BOSS_PREVIEW_PANEL_RECT.size.x * 0.5 - 344.0, 546.0)
	challenge.pressed.connect(_begin_boss_battle)
	panel.add_child(challenge)
	var retreat := _button("돌아가서 준비한다 · ESC", GamePalette.MUTED, Vector2(330.0, 56.0))
	retreat.position = Vector2(BOSS_PREVIEW_PANEL_RECT.size.x * 0.5 + 14.0, 546.0)
	retreat.pressed.connect(_cancel_boss_preview)
	panel.add_child(retreat)
	challenge.grab_focus()

func _cancel_boss_preview() -> void:
	# 마왕성에서 E를 누른 순간 되돌릴 수 없던 문제(P1-2). 필드로 그대로 되돌리고
	# 다른 모달과 같은 복귀 무적을 줍니다.
	if state != "boss_preview":
		return
	_clear_overlay()
	get_tree().paused = false
	state = "playing"
	current_interactable.clear()
	interaction_timer = 0.0
	_grant_modal_return_invulnerability()
	_show_banner("마왕성 앞에서 물러났습니다 · 준비가 되면 다시 E", GamePalette.MUTED, 2.2)

func _build_boss_factory() -> FactoryDeck:
	var deck: FactoryDeck = FACTORY_SCRIPT.new()
	deck.reset()
	deck.slots.clear()
	for _index in 10:
		deck.slots.append({"lanes":[{},{}], "repeat":1, "duration_mul":1.0, "reload_mul":1.0})
	var skill_cards := _boss_auto_fused_cards()
	if not skill_cards.is_empty():
		skill_cards.sort_custom(func(a: Dictionary, b: Dictionary) -> bool: return DealCardLibrary.expected_power(a) > DealCardLibrary.expected_power(b))
		for flat_index in 20:
			var card: Dictionary = skill_cards[flat_index % skill_cards.size()].duplicate(true)
			var slot_index := flat_index / 2
			var lane_index := flat_index % 2
			deck.place_card(slot_index, lane_index, card)
	# 아이템 카드는 공격하지 않으며, 두 번째 동시 칸에서 첫 번째 스킬을 보조합니다.
	for index in mini(boss_items.size(), 10):
		var target_slot := (index * 3) % 10
		deck.place_card(target_slot, 1, ItemLibrary.instance(boss_items[index]))
	return deck

func _boss_auto_fused_cards() -> Array[Dictionary]:
	var counts: Dictionary = {}
	for id: String in rejected_skills:
		if DealCardLibrary.by_id(id).is_empty():
			continue
		counts[id] = int(counts.get(id, 0)) + 1
	var result: Array[Dictionary] = []
	for id in counts:
		var rank_counts := [0, int(counts[id]), 0, 0, 0, 0]
		for rank in range(1, 5):
			rank_counts[rank + 1] += rank_counts[rank] / 2
			rank_counts[rank] %= 2
		for rank in range(1, 6):
			for _copy in rank_counts[rank]:
				result.append(DealCardLibrary.instance(String(id), rank))
	return result

func _begin_boss_battle() -> void:
	if state != "boss_preview":
		return
	_clear_overlay()
	get_tree().paused = false
	state = "boss"
	current_interactable.clear()
	interaction_text.visible = false
	for enemy: Node in active_enemies.duplicate():
		if is_instance_valid(enemy):
			enemy.queue_free()
	active_enemies.clear()
	enemy_spatial.clear()
	for orb: Node in get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("xp_orbs"):
		if is_instance_valid(orb):
			orb.queue_free()
	var boss_position: Vector2 = _find_visible_boss_position()
	boss = ENEMY_SCRIPT.new()
	var power_level := float(cycle_number + level) * 0.7
	var boss_debts: Array[String] = rejected_skills.duplicate()
	for inherited_skill: Dictionary in boss_advancement_skills:
		boss_debts.append(String(inherited_skill["module"]))
	boss.setup(self, player, "demon_king", 4, power_level, boss_debts, true, false, boss_items)
	boss.position = boss_position
	gameplay_root.add_child(boss)
	boss.use_external_deal_cycle(true)
	boss_cycle = CYCLE_CONTROLLER_SCRIPT.new()
	boss_cycle.setup(self, boss, boss_factory, true, false)
	gameplay_root.add_child(boss_cycle)
	boss_panel.visible = true
	update_boss_health(boss.health, boss.max_health, boss.shield, boss.max_shield)
	_show_banner("딜싸이클 마왕 · 20칸 / RELOAD 없음", GamePalette.RED, 4.0)
	spawn_burst(boss_position, GamePalette.RED, 52, 390.0, 1.2)
	play_sound("boss", 0.0)
	shake_camera(12.0, 0.55)

func _find_visible_boss_position() -> Vector2:
	var offsets: Array[Vector2] = [
		Vector2(330.0, 0.0), Vector2(-330.0, 0.0),
		Vector2(0.0, -285.0), Vector2(0.0, 285.0)
	]
	for offset: Vector2 in offsets:
		var candidate := player.global_position + offset
		if world.is_walkable(candidate):
			return candidate
	return world.find_walkable_near(player.global_position, rng, 260.0, 320.0)

func update_boss_health(current: float, maximum: float, shield: float, maximum_shield: float) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(boss_fill):
		return
	var health_ratio := clampf(current / maxf(maximum, 1.0), 0.0, 1.0)
	boss_fill.size.x = 560.0 * health_ratio
	var shield_ratio := 0.0 if maximum_shield <= 0.0 else clampf(shield / maximum_shield, 0.0, 1.0)
	boss_shield_fill.size = Vector2(560.0 * shield_ratio, 12.0)
	boss_shield_fill.visible = shield_ratio > 0.0
	boss_text.text = "딜싸이클 마왕  %d%%  ·  20칸 동시공장  ·  RELOAD 없음" % int(health_ratio * 100.0)

func _on_player_health_changed(current: float, maximum: float) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(health_fill):
		return
	var ratio := clampf(current / maxf(maximum, 1.0), 0.0, 1.0)
	health_fill.size.x = 309.0 * ratio
	# 게이지 위에 흰 숫자를 얹으므로 채움색은 한 단계 어둡게 씁니다 (⑰ 가독성).
	health_fill.color = GamePalette.GREEN.darkened(0.34) if ratio > 0.52 else GamePalette.YELLOW.darkened(0.3) if ratio > 0.25 else GamePalette.RED.darkened(0.18)
	health_text.text = "%d / %d" % [int(ceil(current)), int(maximum)]

func _on_player_shield_changed(charges: int) -> void:
	if is_instance_valid(shield_text) and is_instance_valid(player):
		shield_text.text = "수호 %d  ·  부활 %d" % [charges, player.rollback_charges]

func _on_player_died() -> void:
	call_deferred("_finish_run", false)

func _finish_run(won: bool) -> void:
	if state in ["won", "lost"]:
		return
	state = "won" if won else "lost"
	get_tree().paused = true
	_clear_run_save()
	if won:
		play_sound("win", 0.0)
	_show_result(won)

func _unlock_next_character() -> void:
	if automated_test:
		return
	var before := unlocked_character_count
	if selected_character_id == "swordsman":
		unlocked_character_count = maxi(unlocked_character_count, 2)
	elif selected_character_id == "archer":
		unlocked_character_count = maxi(unlocked_character_count, 3)
	if unlocked_character_count != before:
		_save_progress()

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 결과 화면 (won / lost)
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 레이아웃과 색은 ESC 전체 공장 화면과 같은 디자인 시스템을 따릅니다.
# 바깥 패널(#101827 / #738091) → 통계 칩 4개 → 레일 패널(#0b1320 / #3e4b5d) →
# 버튼 2개 순서이며, 마지막 딜싸이클은 공장 레일 렌더러를 그대로 재사용합니다.
const RESULT_PANEL_RECT := Rect2(12.0, 26.0, 1256.0, 668.0)
const RESULT_BODY_X := 28.0
const RESULT_BODY_W := 1200.0
const RESULT_RAIL_TOP := 210.0

func _show_result(won: bool) -> void:
	_clear_overlay()
	overlay = Control.new()
	overlay.name = "Result"
	overlay.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	overlay.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	ui_root.add_child(overlay)
	var dim := ColorRect.new()
	dim.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	dim.color = Color("07101bf2")
	overlay.add_child(dim)
	var color := GamePalette.YELLOW if won else GamePalette.RED
	var panel := Panel.new()
	panel.position = RESULT_PANEL_RECT.position
	panel.size = RESULT_PANEL_RECT.size
	panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(UI_MODAL_BG, UI_EDGE, 0, UI_BORDER_MODAL))
	overlay.add_child(panel)
	# 결과 종류를 알리는 색 강조 바 + 타이틀. 공장 화면 헤더와 같은 자리·같은 크기입니다.
	var accent := ColorRect.new()
	accent.position = Vector2(RESULT_BODY_X, 18.0)
	accent.size = Vector2(4.0, 34.0)
	accent.color = color
	panel.add_child(accent)
	var heading := _label("버린 운명을 넘어섰습니다" if won else "마왕에게 도달하지 못했습니다", UI_TITLE_SIZE, GamePalette.TEXT)
	heading.position = Vector2(RESULT_BODY_X + 16.0, 16.0)
	heading.size = Vector2(760.0, 40.0)
	panel.add_child(heading)
	var status := _label("마왕 토벌 완료" if won else "밤에 삼켜졌습니다", UI_LABEL_SIZE, color)
	status.position = Vector2(872.0, 16.0)
	status.size = Vector2(356.0, 22.0)
	status.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT
	panel.add_child(status)
	var reload_readout := _label("한 바퀴 RELOAD %.2f초" % (factory.total_reload() if factory != null else 0.0), UI_HEADING_SIZE, GamePalette.ORANGE)
	reload_readout.position = Vector2(872.0, 36.0)
	reload_readout.size = Vector2(356.0, 24.0)
	reload_readout.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT
	panel.add_child(reload_readout)
	var subtitle := _label("마왕은 당신이 버린 카드로 강해집니다 · 다시하기는 같은 캐릭터로 새 런을 시작합니다" if not won else "버린 카드까지 넘어선 딜싸이클입니다 · 다시하기로 더 짧은 한 바퀴에 도전하세요", UI_BODY_SIZE, GamePalette.MUTED)
	subtitle.position = Vector2(RESULT_BODY_X + 16.0, 60.0)
	subtitle.size = Vector2(RESULT_BODY_W - 16.0, 22.0)
	panel.add_child(subtitle)

	# 통계 4칸. 카드와 같은 계층(어두운 배경 + 얇은 색 테두리 + 값/캡션 2단)입니다.
	var chips := [
		{"caption":"생존 시간", "value":_format_time(elapsed_time), "color":GamePalette.CYAN},
		{"caption":"낮 / 밤", "value":"%d회" % cycle_number, "color":GamePalette.YELLOW},
		{"caption":"레벨", "value":"%d" % level, "color":GamePalette.GREEN},
		{"caption":"처치", "value":"%d" % kills, "color":GamePalette.ORANGE}
	]
	var chip_gap := 12.0
	var chip_width := (RESULT_BODY_W - chip_gap * float(chips.size() - 1)) / float(chips.size())
	for index in chips.size():
		var chip: Dictionary = chips[index]
		_add_result_stat_chip(panel, Vector2(RESULT_BODY_X + float(index) * (chip_width + chip_gap), 90.0), Vector2(chip_width, 62.0), String(chip["caption"]), String(chip["value"]), chip["color"])

	var cycle_accent := ColorRect.new()
	cycle_accent.position = Vector2(RESULT_BODY_X, 174.0)
	cycle_accent.size = Vector2(3.0, 20.0)
	cycle_accent.color = GamePalette.YELLOW
	panel.add_child(cycle_accent)
	var cycle_heading := _label("마지막 딜싸이클", UI_HEADING_SIZE, GamePalette.YELLOW)
	cycle_heading.position = Vector2(RESULT_BODY_X + 12.0, 172.0)
	cycle_heading.size = Vector2(500.0, 24.0)
	panel.add_child(cycle_heading)
	var cycle_hint := _label("칸 %d개 · 좌우 스크롤로 전체 확인 · 한 바퀴 RELOAD는 오른쪽 위 표기" % (factory.slots.size() if factory != null else 0), UI_CAPTION_SIZE, GamePalette.MUTED.darkened(0.22))
	cycle_hint.position = Vector2(628.0, 175.0)
	cycle_hint.size = Vector2(600.0, 22.0)
	cycle_hint.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT
	panel.add_child(cycle_hint)
	_build_result_deal_cycle(panel)

	var retry := _button("다시하기", color, Vector2(300.0, 52.0))
	retry.position = Vector2(RESULT_PANEL_RECT.size.x * 0.5 - 320.0, 566.0)
	retry.pressed.connect(_retry_run)
	panel.add_child(retry)
