	var menu := _button("로비로 돌아가기", GamePalette.MUTED, Vector2(300.0, 52.0))
	menu.position = Vector2(RESULT_PANEL_RECT.size.x * 0.5 + 20.0, 566.0)
	menu.pressed.connect(_show_menu)
	panel.add_child(menu)
	var key_hint := _label("R 다시하기   ·   L 또는 ESC 로비로 돌아가기", UI_CAPTION_SIZE, GamePalette.MUTED.darkened(0.25))
	key_hint.position = Vector2(RESULT_BODY_X, 632.0)
	key_hint.size = Vector2(RESULT_BODY_W, 20.0)
	key_hint.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	panel.add_child(key_hint)
	_animate_modal(panel, Vector2(0.0, 22.0))
	retry.grab_focus()

func _add_result_stat_chip(parent: Control, chip_position: Vector2, chip_size: Vector2, caption: String, value: String, color: Color) -> void:
	var chip := Panel.new()
	chip.position = chip_position
	chip.size = chip_size
	chip.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	chip.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(UI_PANEL_BG, UI_EDGE_SOFT, 0, UI_BORDER_FRAME))
	parent.add_child(chip)
	var chip_accent := ColorRect.new()
	chip_accent.position = Vector2(11.0, 9.0)
	chip_accent.size = Vector2(3.0, 14.0)
	chip_accent.color = color
	chip.add_child(chip_accent)
	var caption_label := _label(caption, UI_CAPTION_SIZE, color)
	caption_label.position = Vector2(21.0, 7.0)
	caption_label.size = Vector2(chip_size.x - 33.0, 18.0)
	chip.add_child(caption_label)
	var value_label := _label(value, UI_TITLE_SIZE, GamePalette.TEXT)
	value_label.position = Vector2(12.0, 26.0)
	value_label.size = Vector2(chip_size.x - 24.0, 32.0)
	chip.add_child(value_label)

func _build_result_deal_cycle(parent: Control) -> void:
	# 공장 레일 렌더러(_factory_rail_cell / _build_factory_rail_slot / _build_factory_rail_bridge)를
	# 그대로 재사용합니다. 상호작용만 끈 읽기 전용 레일이므로 스파인 라인·인라인 다리·카드 계층이
	# 공장 화면과 완전히 같은 모습입니다. 레일 끝 RELOAD 마감 패널은 3차 피드백⑬으로 제거했고,
	# 그 덕에 4~5칸까지도 스크롤 없이 화면 안에 가운데 정렬로 들어옵니다.
	var frame := Panel.new()
	frame.position = Vector2(RESULT_BODY_X, RESULT_RAIL_TOP)
	frame.size = Vector2(RESULT_BODY_W, FACTORY_RAIL_ROW_H + 24.0)
	frame.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(UI_PANEL_BG, UI_EDGE_SOFT, 0, UI_BORDER_FRAME))
	parent.add_child(frame)
	var scroll := ScrollContainer.new()
	scroll.position = Vector2(12.0, 12.0)
	scroll.size = Vector2(RESULT_BODY_W - 24.0, FACTORY_RAIL_ROW_H)
	scroll.horizontal_scroll_mode = ScrollContainer.SCROLL_MODE_AUTO
	scroll.vertical_scroll_mode = ScrollContainer.SCROLL_MODE_DISABLED
	frame.add_child(scroll)
	if factory == null or factory.slots.is_empty():
		var empty_rail := HBoxContainer.new()
		empty_rail.alignment = BoxContainer.ALIGNMENT_CENTER
		scroll.add_child(empty_rail)
		var empty := _label("기록된 딜싸이클이 없습니다.", UI_HEADING_SIZE, GamePalette.MUTED)
		empty.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(scroll.size.x, FACTORY_RAIL_ROW_H)
		empty.vertical_alignment = VERTICAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
		empty.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
		empty_rail.add_child(empty)
		return
	var slot_count := factory.slots.size()
	var content_width := float(slot_count) * FACTORY_RAIL_CARD_W + float(maxi(0, slot_count - 1)) * FACTORY_RAIL_BRIDGE_W
	# §12 기지 항목(5칸+ 가로 스크롤)은 축소 레일로 시도했다가 되돌렸습니다.
	# Control.scale로 0.75배 하면 6칸이 들어오긴 하지만 픽셀 폰트가 소수 배율에서
	# 뭉개져 지속/RELOAD 숫자를 읽을 수 없습니다(캡처로 확인). 실물 크기를 유지하고
	# 대신 가로 스크롤바가 실제로 보이도록 전역 스크롤바 테마를 고쳤습니다(P2-9).
	var rail := HBoxContainer.new()
	rail.alignment = BoxContainer.ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	rail.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 0)
	scroll.add_child(rail)
	# 칸이 적으면 레일을 가운데로 모으고, 많으면 가로 스크롤로 확인합니다.
	rail.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(maxf(content_width, scroll.size.x), FACTORY_RAIL_ROW_H)
	for slot_index in slot_count:
		_build_factory_rail_slot(rail, slot_index, false)
		if slot_index < slot_count - 1:
			_build_factory_rail_bridge(rail, "built")

func _retry_run() -> void:
	_clear_overlay()
	get_tree().paused = false
	_begin_run()

func _update_hud() -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(player):
		return
	_on_player_health_changed(player.health, player.max_health)
	_on_player_shield_changed(player.shield_charges)
	class_text.text = "%s  LV %d" % [player.get_character_name(), level]
	timer_text.text = "모험 %s" % _format_time(elapsed_time)
	enemy_text.text = "필드 전투 일시 정지 · NPC 탐색" if inside_castle else "필드 마물 %03d  ·  처치 %03d" % [active_enemies.size(), kills]
	gold_text.text = "%03d" % gold
	trophy_text.text = "시련 구슬 %d / 4%s" % [player.trophy_orbs.size(), " · 전승 NPC를 찾으세요" if player.trophy_orbs.size() == 4 and player.advancement_tier == 1 else ""]
	var duration := NIGHT_DURATION if is_night else DAY_DURATION
	var remaining := maxi(0, int(ceil(duration - phase_elapsed)))
	if inside_castle:
		phase_text.text = "여행자의 성 · 베이스캠프"
		phase_text.add_theme_color_override("font_color", GamePalette.YELLOW)
	elif state == "boss":
		phase_text.text = "마왕 결전"
		phase_text.add_theme_color_override("font_color", GamePalette.RED)
	else:
		phase_text.text = "%s %d  ·  %02d초" % ["밤" if is_night else "낮", cycle_number, remaining]
		phase_text.add_theme_color_override("font_color", GamePalette.RED if is_night else GamePalette.YELLOW)
	cycle_fill.size.x = (HUD_PHASE_BAR.size.x - 4.0) * clampf(phase_elapsed / duration, 0.0, 1.0)
	cycle_fill.color = GamePalette.RED if is_night else GamePalette.YELLOW

	var xp_ratio := clampf(float(experience) / float(maxi(xp_target, 1)), 0.0, 1.0)
	xp_fill.size.x = (HUD_XP_BAR.size.x - 4.0) * xp_ratio
	xp_text.text = "경험 %d / %d  ·  다음 선택" % [experience, xp_target]
	if inside_castle:
		compass_text.text = "성 내부 · 바깥 전투는 정지됨"
	else:
		var to_castle: Vector2 = world.get_demon_castle_position() - player.global_position
		var distance_hint := "기운이 선명함" if to_castle.length() < 900.0 else "가까워지는 중" if to_castle.length() < 2600.0 else "멀리서 느껴짐" if to_castle.length() < 5200.0 else "아주 희미함"
		compass_text.text = "마왕성의 기운 %s · %s" % [_direction_arrow(to_castle), distance_hint]
	_update_deal_cycle_hud()

func _update_deal_cycle_hud() -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(cycle_hud_panel) or factory == null:
		return
	cycle_hud_panel.visible = not inside_castle
	if inside_castle or not is_instance_valid(player_cycle):
		return
	if factory.slots.is_empty():
		return
	# RELOAD 대기 표시는 되감기 릴(_update_cycle_rewind)이 전담합니다.
	# 예전처럼 가운데 칩을 RELOAD 아이콘으로 바꾸거나 패널을 주황 박스로 강조하지 않습니다.
	if player_cycle.reloading:
		return
	var current := clampi(player_cycle.current_index, 0, factory.slots.size() - 1)
	_apply_cycle_chip(cycle_chip_previous, player_cycle.previous_index(), false)
	_apply_cycle_chip(cycle_chip_next, player_cycle.next_index(), false)
	_apply_cycle_chip(cycle_chip_current, current, true)
	var lane_count := maxi(1, (factory.slots[current].get("lanes", []) as Array).size())
	cycle_slot_text.text = "칸 %02d / %02d%s" % [current + 1, factory.slots.size(), " · 동시 %d" % lane_count if lane_count > 1 else ""]
	cycle_status_text.text = "%.2f / %.2f초" % [minf(player_cycle.group_elapsed, player_cycle.group_duration), player_cycle.group_duration]
	cycle_progress_fill.size.x = (CYCLE_HUD_BAR_W - 4.0) * player_cycle.progress_ratio()

func _direction_arrow(vector: Vector2) -> String:
	var angle := wrapf(vector.angle(), -PI, PI)
	if angle > -PI / 8.0 and angle <= PI / 8.0: return "→"
	if angle > PI / 8.0 and angle <= 3.0 * PI / 8.0: return "↘"
	if angle > 3.0 * PI / 8.0 and angle <= 5.0 * PI / 8.0: return "↓"
	if angle > 5.0 * PI / 8.0 and angle <= 7.0 * PI / 8.0: return "↙"
	if angle <= -PI / 8.0 and angle > -3.0 * PI / 8.0: return "↗"
	if angle <= -3.0 * PI / 8.0 and angle > -5.0 * PI / 8.0: return "↑"
	if angle <= -5.0 * PI / 8.0 and angle > -7.0 * PI / 8.0: return "↖"
	return "←"

func spawn_burst(world_position: Vector2, color: Color, count: int, speed: float, duration: float) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(gameplay_root) or active_effect_nodes >= MAX_TRANSIENT_EFFECTS:
		return
	if active_effect_nodes >= int(MAX_TRANSIENT_EFFECTS * 0.7):
		count = mini(count, 10)
	var effect := BURST_SCRIPT.new()
	effect.setup(color, count, speed, duration)
	effect.position = world_position
	effect.tree_exiting.connect(_on_transient_effect_exiting)
	active_effect_nodes += 1
	gameplay_root.add_child(effect)

func _on_transient_effect_exiting() -> void:
	active_effect_nodes = maxi(0, active_effect_nodes - 1)

func show_world_text(world_position: Vector2, text: String, color: Color, font_size: int = 17) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(gameplay_root) or active_effect_nodes >= MAX_TRANSIENT_EFFECTS:
		return
	var holder := Node2D.new()
	holder.position = world_position
	holder.z_index = 14
	holder.tree_exiting.connect(_on_transient_effect_exiting)
	active_effect_nodes += 1
	gameplay_root.add_child(holder)
	var label := _label(text, font_size, color)
	label.position = Vector2(-130.0, -20.0)
	label.size = Vector2(260.0, 44.0)
	label.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	holder.add_child(label)
	var tween := holder.create_tween()
	tween.set_parallel(true)
	tween.tween_property(holder, "position:y", holder.position.y - 48.0, 0.82).set_trans(Tween.TRANS_QUAD).set_ease(Tween.EASE_OUT)
	tween.tween_property(label, "modulate:a", 0.0, 0.82).set_delay(0.24)
	tween.chain().tween_callback(holder.queue_free)

const BANNER_RECT := Rect2(230.0, 148.0, 820.0, 58.0)
const BANNER_BOSS_Y := 192.0

func _show_banner(text: String, color: Color, duration: float) -> void:
	# 예전에는 배너를 만들 때마다 같은 좌표에 새 패널을 얹어, 사건이 연달아 일어나면
	# 반투명 배경이 두 겹으로 깔리고 글자가 서로 위에 찍혔습니다. 마왕 토스트와 같은
	# 규칙으로 직전 배너를 먼저 걷어냅니다 (P1-15).
	if is_instance_valid(active_banner):
		active_banner.queue_free()
	active_banner = null
	var banner := Panel.new()
	banner.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	# 화면 정중앙(y 205)은 플레이어 주변 시야를 크게 가렸습니다. 상단 HUD 아래로 올리되,
	# 마왕 체력 패널(y 112~182)이 떠 있을 때만 그 아래로 비켜 줍니다.
	var banner_y := BANNER_RECT.position.y
	if is_instance_valid(boss_panel) and boss_panel.visible:
		banner_y = BANNER_BOSS_Y
	banner.position = Vector2(BANNER_RECT.position.x, banner_y)
	banner.size = BANNER_RECT.size
	banner.modulate.a = 0.0
	banner.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(UI_MODAL_BG, UI_EDGE, 0, UI_BORDER_MODAL))
	ui_root.add_child(banner)
	var accent := ColorRect.new()
	accent.position = Vector2(2.0, 12.0)
	accent.size = Vector2(4.0, BANNER_RECT.size.y - 24.0)
	accent.color = color
	accent.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	banner.add_child(accent)
	var label := _label(text, UI_HEADING_SIZE, color)
	label.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	label.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	label.vertical_alignment = VERTICAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	banner.add_child(label)
	active_banner = banner
	var tween := banner.create_tween()
	tween.set_pause_mode(Tween.TWEEN_PAUSE_PROCESS)
	tween.tween_property(banner, "modulate:a", 1.0, 0.15)
	tween.tween_interval(maxf(0.4, duration - 0.45))
	tween.tween_property(banner, "modulate:a", 0.0, 0.3)
	tween.tween_callback(_finish_banner.bind(banner))

func _finish_banner(banner: Panel) -> void:
	if active_banner == banner:
		active_banner = null
	if is_instance_valid(banner):
		banner.queue_free()

func _animate_modal(control: Control, offset: Vector2 = Vector2(0.0, 18.0)) -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(control):
		return
	var target_position := control.position
	control.position += offset
	control.modulate.a = 0.0
	control.scale = Vector2(0.985, 0.985)
	control.pivot_offset = control.size * 0.5
	var tween := control.create_tween()
	tween.set_pause_mode(Tween.TWEEN_PAUSE_PROCESS)
	tween.set_parallel(true)
	tween.tween_property(control, "position", target_position, 0.22).set_trans(Tween.TRANS_QUAD).set_ease(Tween.EASE_OUT)
	tween.tween_property(control, "modulate:a", 1.0, 0.18)
	tween.tween_property(control, "scale", Vector2.ONE, 0.22).set_trans(Tween.TRANS_BACK).set_ease(Tween.EASE_OUT)

func _play_scene_transition(midpoint: Callable, tint: Color = Color("070a14")) -> void:
	if transition_active or automated_test:
		midpoint.call()
		return
	transition_active = true
	var curtain := ColorRect.new()
	curtain.name = "SceneTransition"
	curtain.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	curtain.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	curtain.color = Color(tint, 0.0)
	curtain.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_STOP
	ui_root.add_child(curtain)
	var tween := curtain.create_tween()
	tween.set_pause_mode(Tween.TWEEN_PAUSE_PROCESS)
	tween.tween_property(curtain, "color:a", 1.0, SCENE_TRANSITION_FADE_IN).set_trans(Tween.TRANS_QUAD)
	tween.tween_callback(midpoint)
	tween.tween_interval(SCENE_TRANSITION_HOLD)
	tween.tween_property(curtain, "color:a", 0.0, SCENE_TRANSITION_FADE_OUT).set_trans(Tween.TRANS_QUAD).set_ease(Tween.EASE_OUT)
	tween.tween_callback(func(): transition_active = false; curtain.queue_free())

func shake_camera(strength: float, duration: float) -> void:
	if not screen_shake_enabled or not is_instance_valid(player):
		return
	var camera := player.get_node_or_null("PlayerCamera") as Camera2D
	if not is_instance_valid(camera):
		return
	camera.offset = Vector2(rng.randf_range(-strength, strength), rng.randf_range(-strength, strength))
	var tween := camera.create_tween()
	tween.tween_property(camera, "offset", Vector2.ZERO, duration).set_trans(Tween.TRANS_QUAD).set_ease(Tween.EASE_OUT)

func play_sound(sound_name: String, volume_db: float = 0.0) -> void:
	if is_instance_valid(sound_manager):
		sound_manager.play(sound_name, volume_db + effects_volume_db)

func _clear_overlay() -> void:
	if is_instance_valid(overlay):
		overlay.queue_free()
	overlay = null

func _format_time(seconds: float) -> String:
	var total := int(seconds)
	return "%02d:%02d" % [total / 60, total % 60]

func _save_run_snapshot() -> void:
	if not is_instance_valid(player) or factory == null or state not in ["playing", "boss"]:
		return
	var snapshot := {
		"character_id":selected_character_id,
		"playtime":elapsed_time,
		"level":level, "experience":experience, "xp_target":xp_target,
		"kills":kills, "gold":gold, "phase_elapsed":phase_elapsed,
		"cycle_number":cycle_number, "is_night":is_night,
		"player_position":player.global_position, "player_health":player.health,
		"player_skills":player.applied_skills.duplicate(),
		"player_trophies":player.trophy_orbs.duplicate(),
		"player_advancement_branch":player.advancement_branch_id,
		"player_advancement_tier":player.advancement_tier,
		"player_shields":player.shield_charges, "player_rollbacks":player.rollback_charges,
		"factory_slots":factory.slots.duplicate(true),
		"factory_inventory":factory.inventory.duplicate(true),
		"factory_trailing_bridge":factory.trailing_bridge,
		"factory_split_used":factory.split_upgrades_used,
		"factory_construction":factory.construction_count,
		"selected_skills":selected_skills.duplicate(),
		"rejected_skills":rejected_skills.duplicate(),
		"boss_items":boss_items.duplicate(),
		"boss_advancement":boss_advancement_skills.duplicate(true),
		"opened_features":opened_features.duplicate(true),
		"camp_states":camp_states.duplicate(true)
	}
	var config := ConfigFile.new()
	config.load(PROGRESS_PATH)
	config.set_value("run", "active", true)
	config.set_value("run", "snapshot", snapshot)
	config.save(PROGRESS_PATH)
	saved_run_available = true
	saved_run_playtime = elapsed_time

func _read_run_snapshot() -> Dictionary:
	var config := ConfigFile.new()
	if config.load(PROGRESS_PATH) != OK or not bool(config.get_value("run", "active", false)):
		return {}
	var snapshot = config.get_value("run", "snapshot", {})
	return (snapshot as Dictionary).duplicate(true) if snapshot is Dictionary else {}

func _clear_run_save() -> void:
	var config := ConfigFile.new()
	config.load(PROGRESS_PATH)
	config.set_value("run", "active", false)
	if config.has_section_key("run", "snapshot"):
		config.erase_section_key("run", "snapshot")
	config.save(PROGRESS_PATH)
	saved_run_available = false
	saved_run_playtime = 0.0

func _restore_run_snapshot(snapshot: Dictionary) -> void:
	elapsed_time = float(snapshot.get("playtime", 0.0))
	level = maxi(1, int(snapshot.get("level", 1)))
	experience = maxi(0, int(snapshot.get("experience", 0)))
	xp_target = maxi(1, int(snapshot.get("xp_target", 8)))
	kills = maxi(0, int(snapshot.get("kills", 0)))
	gold = maxi(0, int(snapshot.get("gold", 20)))
	phase_elapsed = maxf(0.0, float(snapshot.get("phase_elapsed", 0.0)))
	cycle_number = maxi(1, int(snapshot.get("cycle_number", 1)))
	is_night = bool(snapshot.get("is_night", false))
	selected_skills.assign(snapshot.get("selected_skills", []))
	rejected_skills.assign(snapshot.get("rejected_skills", []))
	boss_items.assign(snapshot.get("boss_items", []))
	boss_advancement_skills.assign(snapshot.get("boss_advancement", []))
	opened_features = (snapshot.get("opened_features", {}) as Dictionary).duplicate(true)
	camp_states = (snapshot.get("camp_states", camp_states) as Dictionary).duplicate(true)
	factory.slots.assign(snapshot.get("factory_slots", []))
	if factory.slots.is_empty():
		factory.reset()
	factory.inventory.assign(snapshot.get("factory_inventory", []))
	factory.trailing_bridge = bool(snapshot.get("factory_trailing_bridge", false))
	factory.split_upgrades_used = int(snapshot.get("factory_split_used", 0))
	factory.construction_count = int(snapshot.get("factory_construction", 0))
	player.applied_skills.assign(snapshot.get("player_skills", []))
	player.trophy_orbs.assign(snapshot.get("player_trophies", []))
	player.advancement_branch_id = String(snapshot.get("player_advancement_branch", ""))
	player.advancement_tier = int(snapshot.get("player_advancement_tier", 0))
	player._rebuild_stats()
	player.health = clampf(float(snapshot.get("player_health", player.max_health)), 1.0, player.max_health)
	player.displayed_health = player.health
	player.trailing_health = player.health
	player.shield_charges = mini(int(snapshot.get("player_shields", 0)), player.shield_capacity)
	player.rollback_charges = mini(int(snapshot.get("player_rollbacks", 0)), player.rollback_capacity)
	player.global_position = snapshot.get("player_position", Vector2.ZERO)
	world.set_opened_features(opened_features)
	var cleared := {}
	for direction: String in camp_states:
		cleared[direction] = bool(camp_states[direction].get("cleared", false))
	world.set_cleared_trial_camps(cleared)
	world.set_night_amount(1.0 if is_night else 0.0)
	_reset_player_cycle()

func _load_progress() -> void:
	var config := ConfigFile.new()
	if config.load(PROGRESS_PATH) == OK:
		unlocked_character_count = clampi(int(config.get_value("progress", "unlocked_characters", 1)), 1, 3)
		master_volume_db = float(config.get_value("settings", "master_volume_db", -4.0))
		effects_volume_db = float(config.get_value("settings", "effects_volume_db", 0.0))
		screen_shake_enabled = bool(config.get_value("settings", "screen_shake", true))
		damage_numbers_enabled = bool(config.get_value("settings", "damage_numbers", true))
		fullscreen_enabled = bool(config.get_value("settings", "fullscreen", false))
		saved_run_available = bool(config.get_value("run", "active", false))
		var snapshot = config.get_value("run", "snapshot", {})
		saved_run_playtime = float((snapshot as Dictionary).get("playtime", 0.0)) if snapshot is Dictionary else 0.0
	var master_bus := AudioServer.get_bus_index("Master")
	if master_bus >= 0:
		AudioServer.set_bus_volume_db(master_bus, master_volume_db)
	# 볼륨과 달리 전체 화면은 저장만 되고 복원되지 않아, 다시 켜면 창모드인데 설정
	# 체크박스만 켜짐으로 남아 있었습니다 (P1-10).
	# 자동 테스트·캡처(사용자 인자가 있는 실행)는 창 모드를 강제로 바꾸지 않습니다.
	if OS.get_cmdline_user_args().is_empty():
		var target_mode := DisplayServer.WINDOW_MODE_FULLSCREEN if fullscreen_enabled else DisplayServer.WINDOW_MODE_WINDOWED
		if DisplayServer.window_get_mode() != target_mode:
			DisplayServer.window_set_mode(target_mode)

func _save_progress() -> void:
	var config := ConfigFile.new()
	config.load(PROGRESS_PATH)
	config.set_value("progress", "unlocked_characters", unlocked_character_count)
	config.set_value("settings", "master_volume_db", master_volume_db)
	config.set_value("settings", "effects_volume_db", effects_volume_db)
	config.set_value("settings", "screen_shake", screen_shake_enabled)
	config.set_value("settings", "damage_numbers", damage_numbers_enabled)
	config.set_value("settings", "fullscreen", fullscreen_enabled)
	config.save(PROGRESS_PATH)

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 한국어 조사 헬퍼
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 카드·NPC 이름은 데이터에서 오므로 조사를 문자열에 박아 둘 수 없습니다.
# 마지막 글자의 받침 유무로 조사를 고르고, 한글이 아니면 받침 없는 쪽을 씁니다.
func _has_final_consonant(word: String) -> bool:
	if word.is_empty():
		return false
	var code := word.unicode_at(word.length() - 1)
	if code < 0xAC00 or code > 0xD7A3:
		return false
	return (code - 0xAC00) % 28 != 0

func _particle_wa(word: String) -> String:
	return "과" if _has_final_consonant(word) else "와"

func _particle_eul(word: String) -> String:
	return "을" if _has_final_consonant(word) else "를"

func _particle_eun(word: String) -> String:
	return "은" if _has_final_consonant(word) else "는"

func _label(text: String, font_size: int, color: Color) -> Label:
	var label := Label.new()
	label.text = text
	label.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	label.clip_text = true
	label.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", font_size)
	label.add_theme_color_override("font_color", color)
	label.add_theme_color_override("font_outline_color", Color(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.88))
	# 작은 글자에 4px 외곽선을 두르면 획이 뭉개집니다. 크기에 따라 두께를 나눕니다 (⑰).
	label.add_theme_constant_override("outline_size", 3 if font_size <= UI_LABEL_SIZE else 4)
	return label

func _hud_panel(panel_position: Vector2, panel_size: Vector2, accent_color: Color) -> Panel:
	# 필드 HUD 패널의 단일 골격 (⑰). 테두리는 중립색 1px 하나로 통일하고,
	# 패널의 성격은 왼쪽 3px 액센트 바와 헤딩 글자색으로만 구분합니다.
	var panel := Panel.new()
	panel.position = panel_position
	panel.size = panel_size
	panel.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _panel_style(Color("0f1521e8"), UI_EDGE_SOFT, 0, UI_BORDER_FRAME))
	hud.add_child(panel)
	var accent := ColorRect.new()
	accent.position = Vector2(1.0, 9.0)
	accent.size = Vector2(3.0, 16.0)
	accent.color = accent_color
	accent.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	panel.add_child(accent)
	return panel

func _button(text: String, color: Color, minimum_size: Vector2) -> Button:
	var button := Button.new()
	return _style_button(button, text, color, minimum_size)

func _style_button(button: Button, text: String, color: Color, minimum_size: Vector2) -> Button:
	button.text = text
	button.custom_minimum_size = minimum_size
	button.size = minimum_size
	button.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 16)
	button.add_theme_color_override("font_color", GamePalette.TEXT)
	button.add_theme_color_override("font_hover_color", Color.WHITE)
	# 비활성 라벨을 너무 어둡게 죽이면 "이어하기 · 저장된 모험 없음"처럼 정보를 담은
	# 버튼이 배경에 묻힙니다 (P2-7).
	button.add_theme_color_override("font_disabled_color", GamePalette.MUTED.darkened(0.15))
	button.add_theme_stylebox_override("normal", _panel_style(color.darkened(0.76), color.darkened(0.14), 0, 2))
	button.add_theme_stylebox_override("hover", _panel_style(color.darkened(0.62), color, 0, 2))
	button.add_theme_stylebox_override("pressed", _panel_style(color.darkened(0.5), Color.WHITE, 0, 2))
	button.add_theme_stylebox_override("focus", _panel_style(color.darkened(0.58), Color.WHITE, 0, 3))
	button.add_theme_stylebox_override("disabled", _panel_style(GamePalette.VOID_SOFT, GamePalette.STONE_DARK, 0, 1))
	button.focus_mode = Control.FOCUS_ALL
	button.set_meta("choice_color", color)
	return button

func _deal_choice_button(definition: Dictionary, card_size: Vector2 = CHOICE_CARD_SIZE) -> Button:
	# ⑳ 선택 모달 카드 = 공장 레일 카드 블록(190×142) + 부가 정보 확장부.
	# 지속/RELOAD 칩은 블록 안에만 있고, 레일 카드와 완전히 같은 자리·크기·글꼴이다.
	var instance_data := DealCardLibrary.instance(String(definition["id"]), 1)
	var ranked := DealCardLibrary.ranked(instance_data)
	var owned := 0 if factory == null else factory.get_total_count(String(definition["id"]))
	var highest := 0 if factory == null else factory.get_highest_rank(String(definition["id"]))
	var owned_text := "%d장 보유중" % owned
	if highest > 0:
		owned_text += " · 최고 R%d" % highest
	var color := Color(String(definition.get("color", "f4d35e")))
	var button := _button("", color, card_size)
	button.set_meta("choice_role", "skill")
	button.set_meta("owned_text", owned_text)
	_build_choice_card_body(button, instance_data, card_size, DealCardLibrary.combat_tags(ranked), String(definition.get("desc", "")), [
		{"text":"피해계수  %.2f  ·  범위  %d" % [float(ranked.get("damage", 0.0)), int(ranked.get("range", 0.0))], "color":GamePalette.YELLOW},
		{"text":"한 바퀴 %.2f초 → +%.2f초" % [(0.0 if factory == null else factory.total_reload()), float(ranked.get("reload", 0.0))], "color":GamePalette.ORANGE},
		{"text":owned_text, "color":GamePalette.GREEN if owned > 0 else GamePalette.MUTED}
	])
	return button

func _build_choice_card_body(button: Button, card: Dictionary, card_size: Vector2, tag_text: String, description_text: String, lines: Array) -> void:
	# 선택 모달 카드의 공통 골격. 왼쪽 위에 표준 카드 블록, 오른쪽에 분류/이름/태그,
	# 아래에 설명과 요약 줄들이 놓인다. 스킬·아이템 카드가 같은 골격을 쓴다.
	var block := _card_block_panel(card)
	block.position = Vector2(16.0, 14.0)
	button.add_child(block)
	var info_x := 16.0 + FACTORY_CARD_BLOCK_SIZE.x + 16.0
	var info_width := maxf(120.0, card_size.x - info_x - 18.0)
	var tags := _label(tag_text, UI_LABEL_SIZE, GamePalette.CYAN)
	tags.position = Vector2(info_x, 16.0)
	tags.size = Vector2(info_width, 50.0)
	tags.autowrap_mode = TextServer.AUTOWRAP_WORD_SMART
	button.add_child(tags)
	# 부가 정보는 블록 오른쪽 열에 왼쪽 정렬로 쌓아 블록과 같은 시선 흐름을 만든다.
	for line_index in lines.size():
		var line: Dictionary = lines[line_index]
		var line_label := _label(String(line.get("text", "")), UI_LABEL_SIZE, line.get("color", GamePalette.MUTED))
		line_label.position = Vector2(info_x, 74.0 + float(line_index) * 24.0)
		line_label.size = Vector2(info_width, 22.0)
		button.add_child(line_label)
	var description_top := 14.0 + FACTORY_CARD_BLOCK_SIZE.y + 14.0
	var description := _label(description_text, UI_BODY_SIZE + 1, GamePalette.TEXT)
	description.position = Vector2(18.0, description_top)
	description.size = Vector2(card_size.x - 36.0, maxf(30.0, card_size.y - description_top - 14.0))
	description.autowrap_mode = TextServer.AUTOWRAP_WORD_SMART
	description.vertical_alignment = VERTICAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	button.add_child(description)

# 전역 스크롤바 테마 (P2-9)
# 공장 레일·보관함·결과 레일·상점이 전부 Godot 기본 스크롤바를 쓰고 있어 픽셀 레트로
# 톤에서 혼자 튀었습니다. ui_root에 한 번만 걸어 모든 ScrollContainer에 적용합니다.
func _scrollbar_style(background: Color, border: Color, width: int) -> StyleBoxFlat:
	var style := StyleBoxFlat.new()
	style.bg_color = background
	style.border_color = border
	style.set_border_width_all(width)
	style.set_corner_radius_all(0)
	# ScrollBar의 최소 두께는 스타일박스의 여백 + 테두리에서 나옵니다. 0으로 두면
	# 막대가 두께 0으로 접혀 아예 보이지 않습니다. 5px씩 주어 12px 두께로 고정합니다.
	style.content_margin_left = 5.0
	style.content_margin_right = 5.0
	style.content_margin_top = 5.0
	style.content_margin_bottom = 5.0
	return style

func _build_ui_theme() -> Theme:
	var ui_theme := Theme.new()
	for bar_type: String in ["HScrollBar", "VScrollBar"]:
		ui_theme.set_stylebox("scroll", bar_type, _scrollbar_style(Color("070c15e0"), UI_EDGE_SOFT, UI_BORDER_FRAME))
		ui_theme.set_stylebox("scroll_focus", bar_type, _scrollbar_style(Color("070c15e0"), UI_EDGE, UI_BORDER_FRAME))
		ui_theme.set_stylebox("grabber", bar_type, _scrollbar_style(Color("39445a"), UI_EDGE, UI_BORDER_FRAME))
		ui_theme.set_stylebox("grabber_highlight", bar_type, _scrollbar_style(Color("4e5c76"), GamePalette.CYAN.darkened(0.2), UI_BORDER_FRAME))
		ui_theme.set_stylebox("grabber_pressed", bar_type, _scrollbar_style(Color("647491"), GamePalette.CYAN, UI_BORDER_FRAME))
	return ui_theme

func _panel_style(background: Color, border: Color, radius: int, width: int) -> StyleBoxFlat:
	var style := StyleBoxFlat.new()
	style.bg_color = background
	style.border_color = border
	style.set_border_width_all(width)
	style.set_corner_radius_all(radius)
	# 픽셀 레트로 유지를 위해 그림자는 아주 얕게만 씁니다 (⑰: 박스 중첩 인상 줄이기).
	style.shadow_color = Color(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.32)
	style.shadow_size = 2
	style.content_margin_left = 12.0
	style.content_margin_right = 12.0
	style.content_margin_top = 8.0
	style.content_margin_bottom = 8.0
	return style
